### [1] [EgoPHI: Estimating Contact and Force from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13014)
*Andela Ilic,Rachel Schuchert,Yijing Jiang,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoPHI是首个从单目RGB图像和物体几何信息中联合估计手-物网格上密集接触图和3D力分布的方法，利用物理仿真管线生成逐顶点力标注，并在仿真、分布外和真实世界场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心视觉理解手-物交互对建模人与物理世界的互动至关重要，但物理层面的交互推理需要估计作用在手和物体上的力，而不仅仅是定位接触点。现有的力估计方法局限于图像空间或平面设置，缺乏可扩展的地面真实力标注。

Method: EgoPHI包含两个核心组件：(1) 物理仿真管线，用于扩充现有手-物数据集并提供密集的逐顶点力监督标注；(2) 神经网络架构，从单目RGB图像和物体几何中联合学习交互手部和关节物体网格上的密集3D接触图和力分布。

Result: 在分布内和分布外基准测试中，EgoPHI相较于现有方法在力估计上取得更优性能，并能泛化到未见数据集。研究人员还构建了两个物理物体并采集了八名参与者的真实交互数据集，验证了sim-to-real迁移能力，证明EgoPHI在模拟、分布外和真实世界场景中都能恢复有意义的3D接触和力分布。

Conclusion: EgoPHI将自我中心手-物理解从接触定位推进到物理grounded的交互推理，实现了从单目图像到稠密3D力分布的端到端估计，为可见性受限的物理交互分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding hand-object interaction from egocentric vision is essential for modeling how people physically engage with the surrounding world. Yet reasoning about physically grounded interaction requires estimating the forces acting on hands and objects, beyond localizing contact. We present EgoPHI, the first method that jointly estimates dense contact maps and 3D force distributions on hand and object meshes from a single monocular RGB image and object geometry. To address the lack of scalable ground-truth force annotations, we introduce a physics-based simulation pipeline that augments existing hand-object datasets with dense per-vertex force supervision. EgoPHI then learns dense 3D contact and force on interacting hand and articulated object meshes, extending vision-based force estimation beyond image-space or planar settings. Our evaluation on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks shows that EgoPHI improves force estimation over existing approaches while generalizing to unseen datasets. To evaluate sim-to-real transfer, we constructed two physical objects that capture dense object contact and force magnitude and used them to record a dataset of interactions from eight participants across diverse touch and grasp types. Our results demonstrate that EgoPHI recovers meaningful 3D contact and force distributions in simulated, out-of-distribution, and real-world settings, advancing egocentric hand-object understanding from contact localization toward physically grounded interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [2] [Splat-based Metal Artifact Reduction in Cone-Beam CT via Polychromatic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13159)
*Kiseok Choi,Inchul Kim,Jaemin Cho,Hyeongjun Cho,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯溅射的物理启发式自校准方法来减少CBCT中的束硬化伪影，整合多色X射线投影模型、材料相关衰减剖面和系统响应建模，无需人工金属掩膜或强先验假设，同时联合优化重建参数和X射线光谱特征，并在合成和真实数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT通过X射线投影实现三维重建，但成像高衰减材料（如金属）时会产生严重伪影，尤其是束硬化伪影。这些伪影源于X射线的多色性，传统单色重建算法无法解决。虽然基于神经表示的近期方法改善了重建质量，但计算开销大，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出一种物理启发的自校准金属伪影减少方法，集成多色X射线投影模型、材料相关衰减剖面及系统响应建模到高斯溅射框架中。该方法消除了对手动金属掩膜或强先验假设的需求，在训练过程中联合优化重建参数和X射线光谱特性。此外，引入基于蒙特卡洛X射线仿真工具箱验证的高保真合成CBCT数据集生成流程。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在伪影抑制和重建精度方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个基于溅射方法来减少CBCT束硬化伪影的工作，同时发布了带有严重金属诱导伪影的新数据集以支持社区研究，兼顾了重建质量和计算效率。

Abstract: Cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) enables volumetric reconstruction from X-ray projections, but suffers from severe artifacts--especially beam hardening--when imaging materials with high attenuation such as metals. These artifacts arise from the polychromatic nature of X-rays and are not properly addressed by conventional monochromatic reconstruction algorithms. While recent neural representation-based methods offer improved reconstruction quality, they are computationally expensive and often impractical for deployment. We propose a novel physics-inspired, self-calibrating metal artifact reduction method that efficiently reconstructs 3D CBCT volumes while correcting beam hardening artifacts. Our method integrates a polychromatic X-ray projection model, material-dependent attenuation profiles, and system response modeling into a Gaussian Splatting framework. Unlike prior work, we eliminate the need for manual metal masks or strong prior assumptions, and we optimize both reconstruction parameters and X-ray spectral characteristics jointly during training. We further introduce a high-fidelity synthetic CBCT dataset generation pipeline validated on Monte-Carlo x-ray simulation toolbox and release new datasets with severe metal-induced artifacts to support the community. This is the first splat-based method for reducing beam hardening in CBCT. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in artifact suppression and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [3] [MV2: Multi-View Multi-Vehicle Driving Dataset for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12442)
*Sanjay Bhargav Dharavath,Hanvitha Saraswathi Mukkamala,Faizan Farooq Khan,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Aditya Arun,C V Jawahar,Zakaria Laskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV2 (Multi-View Multi-Vehicle) 数据集与基准，用于评估动态城市场景中大幅视角变化下的新视图合成（NVS）。数据集包含来自汽车、踏板车和无人机三种同步轨迹的50个高质量场景、12000张图像，通过训练一种车辆的相机数据并在另一种车辆上测试，实现比单轨迹数据集更大的视角差异评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微渲染在NVS领域取得进展，但应用于真实驾驶场景仍面临挑战：捕获视角稀疏、动态对象干扰、多轨迹数据稀缺。现有数据集主要基于单一轨迹，无法评估大视角变化下的NVS性能，亟需一个多视角多车辆的数据集与基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建MV2数据集：同步采集汽车、踏板车和无人机三种车辆轨迹的相机数据；通过Structure-from-Motion进行序列配准，并利用人工像素级对应标注验证相机位姿；定义基准协议——在一种车辆的相机流上训练NVS方法，在另一种车辆上测试；对多种NVS方法和相机位姿估计方法进行系统评估。

Result: 基准测试表明：NVS性能随视角差异增大而退化；前馈式位姿估计器性能明显落后于基于优化的方法。这验证了MV2作为驾驶场景NVS严格测试平台的有效性。

Conclusion: MV2提供了更大视角变化条件下评估NVS的严谨基准，揭示了现有方法在大视角差异下的性能局限，为自动驾驶场景中的NVS研究提供了重要测试平台和公开资源。

Abstract: Differentiable rendering has advanced novel view synthesis (NVS), yet applying it to real-world driving remains difficult due to sparse capture viewpoints, dynamic objects, and limited multi-trajectory data. We introduce the Multi-View Multi-Vehicle (MV2) dataset and benchmark for evaluating NVS models under large viewpoint changes in dynamic urban scenes. MV2 features synchronized captures from a car, scooter, and drone, each following distinct yet synchronized trajectories. Training NVS methods on one vehicle's camera stream and testing on another enables evaluation under substantially larger viewpoint variations than existing single-trajectory datasets. All sequences are registered via Structure-from-Motion and camera poses verified using manual pixel-level correspondence annotations, yielding 50 high-quality scenes with 12000 images. Benchmarking recent NVS and camera pose estimation methods shows that NVS performance degrades with increasing viewpoint disparity, and that feed-forward pose estimators notably lag behind optimization-based approaches, highlighting MV2 as a rigorous testbed for NVS in driving. The dataset, benchmark protocol, and project resources are available at https://mv2-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [4] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT框架通过减法组合（subtractive composition）实现部件感知的3D生成。它在一个结构化3D潜在空间中迭代使用联合分割预测器，同时生成被提取的部件和剩余物体，而非在生成后再进行分割或拼接。该方法在PartObjaverse基准上实现了SOTA几何性能，同时保持了完整的物体重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在根本性矛盾：分割式方法先在生成形状上划分部件，但部件边界在物体固定后才确定；叠加式方法从预定义布局/盒/令牌合成部件再调和，但共享边界容易出现间隙、相互穿插和材质不连续。这些方法都将结构约束强加在原生生成循环之外，导致几何完整性与部件可编辑性难以兼得。

Method: SCULPT采用减法组合策略：将完整物体表示在结构化3D潜在空间中，通过迭代应用联合分割预测器，每次生成一个提取部件和剩余物体。预测器执行条件于图像和当前3D状态的耦合去噪过程，使提取部件与更新后的剩余部分同时生成而非生成后调和。联合分割预测器在两者原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理输出，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制不相交的体素划分。rollout在剩余支撑为空或达到安全上限时终止，使部件数量能自适应每个物体。

Result: 在PartObjaverse基准上达到了SOTA几何性能，同时保留部件组装后的强完整物体重建。四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像输入和一个真实世界照片上的结果展示了超出基准的细粒度纹理部件分解能力。

Conclusion: 减法组合范式为部件感知3D生成提供了一条新路径：通过在生成循环内部同时预测部件和剩余物体，既保持了完整物体的几何质量，又实现了自然的部件划分，克服了分割式和叠加式方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [5] [HIMEC: Directional Change Representation and Fixed-Interface Decoding for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12502)
*Aysha Ashraf,Shaina Ashraf,Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Ali Haider,Zhi Lu,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIMEC 方法结合方向性变化表示（DCR）和固定接口解码，用于遥感图像变化描述任务。DCR 将符号差异分解为出现导向、消失导向和共享上下文三个流，通过可学习查询编码器将融合表示转换为视觉条件化的变化查询令牌。通过训练中固定的零输入技巧，场景解码器在训练和推理中保持相同接口，并在 LEVIR-CC 和 SECOND-CC 数据集上取得更优的 CIDEr 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数遥感图像变化描述方法直接将融合的视觉特征作为条件输入到描述解码器，忽略了中间变化结构和解码器接口一致性的问题。作者希望研究中间变化表示的结构化方式，以及训练与推理阶段解码器接口不一致所带来的性能差距。

Method: HIMEC 包含两个核心组件：(1) 方向性变化表示（DCR），将符号差异分离为出现导向、消失导向和共享上下文三个流向后进行融合；(2) 固定接口解码，通过可学习查询编码器将融合表示转换为视觉条件化的变化查询令牌，作为解码器唯一的样本相关记忆。训练时使用固定的零输入来保持训练和推理接口一致；另设辅助短语解码器提供字幕层面的监督。同时评估了一个局部到场景的级联结构，其中训练时使用教师强制的局部状态，推理时使用自回归状态。

Result: 在三种子匹配比较中，HIMEC 在 LEVIR-CC 上达到 CIDEr 142.81±0.60，优于直接融合特征记忆的 139.51±3.40。在 SECOND-CC 上，固定零和状态匹配的诊断条件分别达到 75.67 和 76.99 CIDEr，远优于不匹配级联的 60.77。级联中教师强制状态与自回归状态的平均余弦距离为 0.69，状态匹配的条件纠正了大部分性能差距，而置换状态对应关系没有检测到惩罚。

Conclusion: HIMEC 证明了方向性变化表示和固定接口解码的有效性。研究发现，训练与推理阶段条件接口不匹配（教师强制与自回归状态）会带来显著性能损失，而状态匹配的条件方式可以恢复大部分差距，且置换状态对应关系无惩罚，表明该级联的性能缺陷主要源于状态分布不匹配而非对应关系错误。这些发现仅限于所评估的级联结构。

Abstract: Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) converts bitemporal imagery into a sentence describing semantic changes. Most RSICC methods condition caption decoders directly on fused visual features, leaving intermediate change structure and decoder-interface consistency less studied. We present HIMEC, combining Directional Change Representation (DCR) with fixed-interface decoding. DCR separates signed differences into appearance-oriented, disappearance-oriented, and shared-context streams before fusion. A learned-query encoder converts the fused representation into visually conditioned change-query tokens that form the scene decoder's only sample-dependent memory. A training-only auxiliary phrase decoder supplies caption-derived supervision. With a fixed zero input, the scene decoder maintains the same interface during training and inference. Separately, we evaluate a local-to-scene cascade conditioned on teacher-forced local states during training and autoregressive states at inference. On changed LEVIR-CC validation pairs, these states have a mean cosine distance of 0.69. Regime-matched conditioning recovers most of the associated deficit, whereas permuting state correspondence causes no detectable penalty. These findings are limited to the evaluated cascade. In a matched three-seed comparison, HIMEC reaches a Consensus-based Image Description Evaluation (CIDEr) score of $142.81\pm0.60$ on LEVIR-CC, versus $139.51\pm3.40$ for direct fused-feature memory. On SECOND-CC, fixed-zero and regime-matched diagnostic conditioning reach 75.67 and 76.99 CIDEr, respectively, versus 60.77 for the mismatched cascade. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/ayshaashra/HIMEC upon publication.

</details>


### [6] [Can Vision-Language Models Assess Proxemic Risk from Egocentric Robot Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12515)
*Vladyslava Rudas,Dmytro Kuzmenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个开源视觉语言模型（InternVL、Qwen-VL、SmolVLM）在机器人自我中心视角下对图像进行四级危险分类的任务，比较了三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调的效果。结果显示，未经微调时模型性能接近基线，微调仅带来适度提升；但Qwen-VL配合高级提示在高危险案例的召回率上显著优于其他模型。进一步分析表明，正确的危险分类与更好的空间定位并不对应，说明模型可能在未关注场景相关区域的情况下就输出了安全标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机器人的自我中心视角评估"接近危险"（proxemic danger）对于在人类环境中进行安全的具身导航至关重要，这既需要视觉理解也需要情境推理。当前视觉语言模型在此类细粒度空间推理任务上的能力尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 评估三个开源VLM（InternVL、Qwen-VL、SmolVLM）在自我中心机器人图像的四级危险分类任务上的表现。对比三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调，以分层随机基线为对照，同时分析人物定位与危险分类之间的关系。

Result: 未微调时所有模型性能接近基线；微调仅带来适度整体提升。Qwen-VL配合高级提示在高危险案例的召回率显著高于其他模型。人物定位分析显示正确危险分类与更好的空间接地并不相关。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在细粒度空间推理和空间接地方面仍存在明显局限，但针对性的提示和微调可以在选定的模型中提升高危险检测能力，为未来在具身导航中部署VLM安全推理提供了方向。

Abstract: Assessing proxemic danger from a robot's egocentric perspective is critical for safe embodied navigation in human environments and requires both visual and contextual reasoning. We evaluate three opensource vision-language models (VLMs) (\textit{InternVL}, \textit{Qwen-VL}, and \textit{SmolVLM}) on the classification of egocentric robot images into four danger levels, comparing three prompting strategies and two rounds of QLoRA fine-tuning against a stratified random baseline. Without fine-tuning, all models perform near the baseline, while fine-tuning yields only modest overall improvements. However, \textit{Qwen-VL} with an advanced prompt achieves substantially higher recall for high-danger cases than the other models. An analysis of person localization further shows that correct danger classification does not correspond to better spatial grounding, indicating that a model may produce a useful safety label without attending to the relevant region of the scene. These results show that current VLMs remain limited in fine-grained proxemic reasoning and spatial grounding, although targeted prompting and fine-tuning can improve high-danger detection in selected models.

</details>


### [7] [Surface-to-Skeleton 3D Cephalometry: Estimating Hidden Skeletal Landmarks from CT-Derived External Soft-Tissue Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12537)
*Tomoki Abe,Taiki Kanaya,Kazuki Saita,Mao Noda,Chie Tachiki,Yasushi Nishii,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种坐标一致的表面对骨骼任务，利用CT获取的软组织点云推断内部骨骼标志点。在240例来自两家医院的CT扫描上，基于层次化点云模型在40例保留患者上实现2.97 mm的平均径向误差，证实了从外部软组织几何推断CT定义骨骼标志点的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D面部地标方法仅能定位皮肤可见表面的点，CT定义的内部骨骼标志点能否从外部软组织几何推断出来仍不清楚。作者希望回答这一受控可行性问题，从而为隐藏骨骼标志点推断奠定基础。

Method: 作者使用同一次CT采集获得的CT派生外部软组织点云与21个骨骼标志点及3个可见软组织标志点配对，构建锁定回顾性协议。将表面到骨骼的估计与光学到CT配准、扫描器域和采集状态效应分离，并单独分析覆盖率。采用集成层次化点云模型进行推断。

Result: 在40例保留患者上，骨骼标志点平均径向误差为2.97 mm，深层或表面不可见标志点误差为3.03 mm。患者不匹配对照实验支持超出固定群体配置或全局相似性的患者特定信号。覆盖率消融显示对非前部几何的依赖。光学迁移诊断揭示显著的覆盖率相关和全局配置成分，但可部署的光学推断仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该研究对受控可行性问题给出了肯定回答，证明从外部软组织几何推断CT定义的隐藏骨骼标志点是可行的，为后续隐藏骨骼标志点推断提供了基础。

Abstract: Existing 3D facial-landmark methods localize points on visible skin, but whether CT-defined internal skeletal landmarks can be inferred from external soft-tissue geometry remains unclear. We formulate a coordinate-consistent surface-to-skeleton task using same-acquisition CT-derived surfaces, separating estimation from optical-to-CT registration, scanner-domain, and acquisition-state effects, with coverage analyzed separately. From 240 clinical CT scans from two hospitals, we construct a locked retrospective protocol pairing CT-derived external soft-tissue point clouds with 21 skeletal landmarks and three visible soft-tissue landmarks. An integrated hierarchical point-cloud model achieves 2.97 mm mean radial error on skeletal landmarks and 3.03 mm on deep or surface-invisible landmarks in 40 held-out patients. Patient-mismatch controls support patient-specific signal beyond a fixed population configuration or global similarity alone, while coverage ablations indicate dependence on non-anterior geometry. Optical-transfer diagnostics reveal substantial coverage-related and global-configuration components, although deployable optical inference remains unresolved. These results answer the controlled feasibility question affirmatively and provide a basis for hidden skeletal landmark inference.

</details>


### [8] [StrAD: A Streaming Method and Benchmark for Audio Description Generation for Long-form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12549)
*Julian Spravil,Sebastian Houben,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StrAD基准和流式密集字幕生成方法，用于全长度视频的音频描述（AD）自动生成。通过滑动窗口处理完整视频，无需人工标注时间戳即可将AD插入现有转录文本中，支持微调模型和零样本VLM提示。StrAD-FT在CMD-AD上达到SOTA（36.3 CIDEr），在StrAD基准上建立参考点（51.0 CIDEr），是首个流式全视频AD生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动创建音频描述（AD）成本高昂，导致仅有极少数视频内容可访问。现有自动AD生成方法依赖真实时间戳和额外上下文线索（如角色数据库），被框架化为视频片段字幕生成任务，且现有基准由短片段和自动或任务不匹配的标注构成，无法衡量长视频全视频AD生成能力。这阻碍了无障碍内容规模化覆盖的进展。

Method: 首先引入StrAD基准，覆盖电影、纪录片、短片、表演和电子游戏等多类型全长视频。将AD生成重新定义为流式密集视频字幕生成：使用滑动窗口处理完整视频，将AD插入现有转录文本中而不需要真实时间戳。支持两种模型：微调模型StrAD-FT和零样本VLM提示的StrAD-Zero。在段级任务（给定时间戳）上训练和评估StrAD-FT，在全视频流式任务中评估两种方法。

Result: 段级任务中，StrAD-FT在CMD-AD上达到36.3 CIDEr（较Shot-by-shot提升+10.0），在StrAD上建立参考点51.0 CIDEr，在MAD-Eval上达24.9 CIDEr。全视频流式任务中，StrAD-FT的SODA分数为2.4，远高于StrAD-Zero的1.1。但两种方法在时间定位和叙事连贯性方面均存在局限。

Conclusion: 本文是首个流式全视频AD生成方法，无需真实时间戳即可实时生成AD，使全视频AD生成可衡量，为可访问性的规模化提供了基础。尽管目前SODA分数较低，且存在时间定位和叙事连贯性问题，但通过建立StrAD基准和基准结果，为后续研究提供了可比较的评估平台和改进方向。

Abstract: Visual content is the dominant medium of communication, yet without audio descriptions (ADs), it remains inaccessible to blind and low-vision people. ADs narrate context-relevant visual events during natural audio pauses. Manually creating ADs is expensive, limiting coverage to a small fraction of available content. Most existing automatic AD generation methods frame the task as video clip captioning, requiring ground-truth timestamps and additional context cues such as character databases. Current benchmarks reinforce this framing, consisting of short video segments paired with automatic or task-mismatched annotations. We introduce StrAD, a benchmark for long-form AD generation on full-length videos spanning diverse genres such as movies, documentaries, short films, performances, and video games. We reformulate AD generation as streaming dense video captioning. Our approach processes full-length videos with a sliding window, inserting ADs into existing transcripts without ground-truth timestamps, and supports both fine-tuned models and zero-shot prompting of vision-language models. On the segment-level task with given timestamps, our fine-tuned StrAD-FT sets the state of the art on CMD-AD with 36.3 CIDEr (+10.0 over Shot-by-shot), establishes a reference point on StrAD (51.0 CIDEr), and remains competitive on MAD-Eval at 24.9 CIDEr. On the full-video streaming task, StrAD-FT reaches a SODA score of 2.4 against 1.1 for our zero-shot baseline StrAD-Zero, though both exhibit limitations in temporal localization and narrative coherence. While prior work has tackled full-video AD generation in an offline, multi-stage fashion, ours is the first streaming approach, generating ADs on the fly without ground-truth timestamps. StrAD makes progress on full-video AD generation measurable, a prerequisite for scaling accessibility.

</details>


### [9] [Attribute-Conditioned Multimodal Slot Factorization for Controllable Fashion Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12570)
*Najmeh Forouzandehmehr,Topojoy Biswas,Evren Korpeoglu,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MM-slotgate，一种多模态槽编码器，将Fashion-CLIP的文本和图像嵌入分解为四个命名的属性槽（类别、颜色、图案、人群），每个槽学习独立的文本-图像门控。在H&M数据集上，结合槽相似度和槽逻辑回归的检索评分达到0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10，大幅超越基线，且槽可解释、可控制、无多余信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚检索通常需要同时满足多个属性（类别、颜色、图案、人群），但传统的单一嵌入将所有这些信号混在一个向量中，检索时难以对特定属性进行独立控制。已有的语义ID方法提供离散的物品编码，但这些编码作为物品级或残差地址优化，没有暴露具名、可独立控制的属性槽。因此需要一种能将多模态表示分解为独立属性槽的方法。

Method: MM-slotgate是一个多模态槽编码器，将Fashion-CLIP的文本和图像嵌入分解为四个命名的属性槽。每个槽学习独立的文本-图像门控（gate），使视觉基础属性（如颜色、图案）更多依赖图像证据，而分类学导向属性（如类别、人群）更多保持文本驱动。检索时使用组合的槽相似度（slot-similarity）和槽逻辑回归（slot-logit）评分。

Result: 在H&M数据集上，MM-slotgate达到0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10，优于等权多模态融合（0.7142）和fCLIP纯文本检索（0.4755）。颜色属性提升最大，从0.321提升至0.889（绝对提升+0.568），因为学习到的颜色门将57.4%的权重分配给图像证据。门控无需模态监督即可解释：颜色偏向图像，类别偏向文本，图案和人群居中。量化槽编码支持定向干预，颜色实现15.3倍提升；线性探针显示无超出标签相关基线的多余信息泄漏。

Conclusion: 可控的时尚检索受益于类型化、属性条件化的多模态槽，而非单一全局嵌入或不可解释的物品级语义ID。MM-slotgate证明将多模态表示按命名属性分解，既提升了检索性能，又保持了可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Fashion retrieval often requires satisfying multiple attributes at once, such as category, color, pattern, and demographic. Monolithic embeddings mix these signals into a single vector, making attribute-specific control difficult at retrieval time. Many existing semantic-ID methods provide discrete item codes, but these codes are typically optimized as item-level or residual addresses and do not expose named, independently controllable attribute slots.
  We introduce MM-slotgate, a multimodal slot encoder that factorizes Fashion-CLIP text and image embeddings into four named attribute slots. Each slot learns its own text-image gate, so visually grounded attributes such as color and pattern can rely more on image evidence, while taxonomy-oriented attributes such as category and demographic can remain more text-driven.
  On H&M, using a combined slot-similarity and slot-logit retrieval score, MM-slotgate achieves 0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10, outperforming equal-weight multimodal fusion (0.7142) and fCLIP text-only retrieval (0.4755). The largest gain is on color, which improves from 0.321 to 0.889 (+0.568 absolute), as the learned color gate assigns 57.4% weight to image evidence. The learned gates are interpretable without modality supervision: color is image-leaning, category is text-leaning, and pattern and demographic lie near the middle.
  The resulting slots also remain controllable: linear probes show no measured excess leakage beyond the label-correlation baseline, and quantized slot codes support targeted intervention, including a 15.3x lift for color. These results suggest that controllable fashion retrieval benefits from typed, attribute-conditioned multimodal slots rather than either a single global embedding or opaque item-level semantic IDs.

</details>
