However, existing cultural benchmarks primarily assess cultural knowledge or values biases, while overlooking whether LLMs can recognize and respect cultural taboos, especially when taboos are implicitly hidden in seemingly harmless questions. Besides, cultural taboos are implicit, and context-dependent, thus poss unique challenges for reliable evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce \textbf{CulShield}, the first public benchmark dedicated to evaluating and improving the cultural taboo safety of LLMs. CulShield spans 77 countries and territories, and includes over 2,020 taboos. It evaluates models along both explicit knowledge and implicit behaviors. Experiments on several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-pro) reveal a clear ``knowledge-behavior gap'': models often fail to apply known taboos during interaction. We further show that variations in linguistic context can significantly affect LLMs' cultural taboo safety. Code and data is accessible here: https://github.com/hedyHe/CulShield.

</details>


### [112] [Are Large Language Models Reliable Reviewers? A Benchmark for Error Detection in Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12342)
*Ying He,Zhouhong Gu,Zhecheng Hu,Yubo Zhou,Hao Shen,Jiaqing Liang,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Yanghua Xiao,Zhixu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinED-Bench，这是首个公开的金融文档错误检测基准，覆盖三个认知复杂度层级和九个真实金融场景，包含超过900份2025年报告。实验表明当前LLMs在此任务上表现欠佳，尤其是在高复杂度场景下，但监督微调可显著提升较弱模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在股票价格预测、金融分析等金融任务中表现良好，但识别金融文档中错误的能力尚未被探索。金融文档的准确性对经济分析、合规监管和企业决策至关重要，亟需一个系统性的基准来评估LLMs在该关键任务上的能力。

Method: 作者构建了FinED-Bench基准，涵盖三个认知复杂度层级（低/中/高）和九个真实金融场景，包含900多份2025年报告（现有LLM未见过的数据），并详细描述了基准构建流程。随后在GPT-4o、Qwen3-14B等先进LLM上评估其错误检测性能，并探索了监督微调对较弱模型的提升效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前主流LLMs在金融错误检测任务上仍存在明显困难，尤其在高复杂度案例中表现不佳。此外，监督微调能够显著提升较弱LLMs在此任务上的性能。数据和代码已公开。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在金融文档错误检测这一关键任务上能力不足，说明该任务需要同时具备金融领域知识和推理能力，现有模型仍有较大提升空间。FinED-Bench为该领域的评估和模型改进提供了标准化的基准平台。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy of financial documents is critical for economic analysis, regulatory compliance, and corporate decision-making. Several studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) perform well in many financial tasks, such as stock price movements and financial analytics. However, a critical task remains unexplored: the ability of LLMs to identify errors in financial documents. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{FinED-Bench}, the first publicly \textbf{Bench}mark for \textbf{Fin}ancial \textbf{E}rror \textbf{D}etection across three levels of cognitive complexity. FinED-Bench covers nine real-world financial scenarios, and includes over 900 documents reported in 2025 that are unseen by existing language models. We detail the benchmark construction process and evaluate several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Qwen3-14B) on this tasks, which requires both financial domain knowledge and reasoning capabilities. Experimental results show that current LLMs still struggle with this task, especially in high-complexity cases. Besides, supervised fine-tuning can significantly improve the performance of weaker LLMs on this task. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/hedyHe/FinED-Bench.

</details>


### [113] [Large Language Models Pass the History Exam But Miss the <<History>>: A Polish High School Exit Exam Matura Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12343)
*Adrian Trzoss,Kacper Dudzic,Wiktor Werner,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究首次基于波兰国家课程构建LLM历史推理基准，使用2023-2025年波兰高中历史结业考试（Matura）评估八种主流大语言模型，发现所有模型的表现均远超人类考生，但总体得分掩盖了能力差异：模型排名随任务类型、来源模态和地理范围而变动，且所有模型在波兰史内容上均有一致性劣势。定性错误分析揭示两种反复出现的失败模式——来源混淆（将来源内容当作推理对象而非分析客体）和时间迷失（回答中的历史时间错位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人被学生广泛用作知识来源，但现有LLM基准很少评估解释性历史推理能力，缺乏面向课程标准的严格的模型评价方法。

Method: 选取八种领先LLM，在2023-2025年官方波兰高中历史结业考试（含简答题和论述题）上进行评估，与人类考生成绩对比；按任务类型、来源模态和地理范围分解得分，并进行定性错误分析。

Result: 所有模型均大幅超越人类考生，但排名在不同任务类型、来源模态和地理范围上不稳定；模型在波兰史与全球史内容上存在一致性劣势；定性分析发现来源混淆和时间迷失两种典型失败模式。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个基于波兰国家课程的LLM历史基准，揭示了聚合分数掩盖的模型能力异质性问题，并指出了历史推理评估中需要关注的系统性错误模式。

Abstract: AI chatbots are widely used by students as knowledge sources, yet LLM benchmarks rarely assess interpretative historical reasoning. We evaluate eight leading LLMs on the Polish high school exit exams (Matura) in history - three official papers from 2023-2025, comprising short-answer questions and extended essays - comparing model performance against the human examinee population. Every model dramatically outperforms human examinees, yet aggregate scores mask distinct competency profiles: rankings are unstable across task type, source modality, and geographical scope, with a consistent penalty on Polish versus Global history content. Qualitative error analysis reveals two recurring failure modes - source conflation, in which models reason from source content rather than treating it as an object of analysis, and temporal disorientation, in which responses are historically misplaced. This study introduces the first LLM history benchmark grounded in Polish national curriculum.

</details>


### [114] [Predicting consumer-technology ownership without a diffusion history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12344)
*Irina Vartanova,Niels Selling,Jennifer Viberg Johansson,Pontus Strimling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验消费技术的感知属性是否预测其普及率。基于2022年对678名美国成年人的Prolific调查及两个前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7与GPT-5.5）的属性评分，使用带符号约束的惩罚回归与留一技术交叉验证，发现属性模型在预测拥有率方面优于"上市年数"基线（MAE降低17%以上），但短期窗口内不如无变化基线。文中还提供了2027年11款新产品的普及率预测示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者技术的采用与扩散是重要研究议题，但传统模型往往依据上市时间等简单变量预测普及率。作者希望检验更丰富的技术感知属性（基于UTAUT2理论）是否能为普及率提供更准确的预测，同时评估大语言模型能否替代人类评分来标注技术属性。

Method: 2022年对678名美国成年人进行Prolific调查，他们对65种消费技术在六个属性上评分；同时从Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5两个大语言模型获取相同评分。以普及率为因变量，以四个UTAUT2接受属性加对数年龄协变量为自变量，采用符号约束的惩罚回归，逐一留出单个技术进行交叉验证评估。比较属性模型与"上市年数"基线及"无变化"基线的预测表现，并对2025-2026年发布的11款产品做2027年预测。

Result: 属性模型在预测拥有率上优于上市年数基线：使用人类评分时MAE下降17%，使用任一语言模型评分时下降更多，其中Opus 4.7效果最佳。但在2022至2025的短期窗口内，由于拥有率变化很小，属性模型未能优于无变化基线。2027年预测结果已给出。

Conclusion: 感知属性（人类或语言模型评分的）能够改善消费技术普及率的横截面预测，且语言模型评分有效性强于人工基线。但该方法存在局限，包括语言模型评分可能反映其对技术的已有知识而非独立属性推理，以及短期预测中属性模型并不优于无变化假设。

Abstract: We test whether the perceived attributes of a consumer technology predict how widely it is owned. In a 2022 Prolific survey of US adults (n = 678), respondents rated 65 consumer technologies on six attributes. We then elicited the same ratings from two frontier language models, Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7 and OpenAI GPT-5.5. We regress ownership prevalence on four UTAUT2 acceptance attributes plus a log-age covariate with a sign-constrained penalized regression and evaluate it by holding out one technology at a time. The attribute model improves on a baseline of years-since-launch: mean absolute error falls by 17% with the human ratings, and by more with either model, most with Opus 4.7. Over the short 2022-to-2025 window, where ownership moved little, the same attributes do not improve on a no-change baseline. We set out the limitations of the approach, including the possibility that language-model ratings reflect prior knowledge of these technologies rather than independent attribute reasoning. We include a deployment illustration: 2027 ownership predictions for eleven products launched in 2025 and 2026.

</details>


### [115] [New Terms, New Toxicity: Consensus-based Chinese Neologism Toxicity Detection via Search-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12361)
*Shiyao Cui,QingLin Zhang,Di Wang,Yida Lu,Zhexin Zhang,Jinhua Gao,Jinglin Yang,Min He,Han Qiu,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何检测通过新词（neologisms）表达的隐性毒性内容，提出了一套分类法（taxonomy）、构建了毒性新词词典，并引入SeTox框架——一种搜索增强框架，使静态大语言模型能够结合实时网络上下文进行新词毒性检测。实验表明，即使使用3B规模的模型，SeTox也能超越近期的大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新词在形式或意义上不断涌现，可成为毒性表达的载体（如"country girl"被用作攻击女权主义的污名化标签）。这类毒性新词表面看似良性，但在公共共识中已演变为毒性用法，对现有内容审核系统构成挑战，且此前鲜有研究探索。因此，需要专门的检测方法来识别这类隐性毒性。

Method: 1. 提出一个分类法（taxonomy），刻画毒性新词的起源及共识验证标准；2. 基于该分类法构建覆盖广泛风险类别的毒性新词词典；3. 引入SeTox——搜索增强框架，使静态大语言模型能够融入实时网页上下文，从而捕捉基于公共共识的毒性含义。

Result: 实验表明，SeTox即使使用3B规模的模型，其性能也优于近期的大规模模型，展现了该方法在融入现实世界知识以进行毒性新词检测方面的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了毒性新词检测的空白，通过搜索增强的方式让静态大语言模型获得实时共识信息，有效提升了隐性毒性检测的准确性与可扩展性。论文包含可能令人不适的冒犯性内容，已作免责声明。

Abstract: Neologisms, emerging terms in meaning or form, can serve as new vehicles for toxic expression, like "country girl" as a stigmatizing label targeting feminism. Such toxic neologisms appear benign but have evolved into toxic usage in public consensus, posing challenges to moderation systems and remaining underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how to detect implicit toxicity expressed via neologisms. We first propose a taxonomy that captures the origins and consensus-verification criteria of toxic neologisms, followed by the construction of a lexicon spanning widely observed risk categories. To capture toxicity grounded in public consensus, we introduce SeTox, a search-augmented framework that enables static large language models (LLMs) to incorporate real-time web context for neologism toxicity detection. Experiments show that SeTox, even with 3B-scale models, outperforms recent large-scale models, demonstrating its scalability to incorporate real-world knowledge for toxic neologism detection. Disclaimer: this paper has offensive contents that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [116] [Are you Talking Logic to Me? Assessing Language Models Syllogistic Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12374)
*Hanna Abi Akl,Fabien Gandon,Catherine Faron,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同知识表示（KR）形式化符号对语言模型三段论推理能力的影响，扩展了FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，在小语言模型上通过SFT和零样本设置验证了KR符号的性能，并提出SEF分类方法增强推理，开源了CLGC框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在逻辑任务（如三段论推理）上表现不佳，而知识表示在表达输入信息以帮助模型解决问题方面至关重要。现有研究缺乏对不同形式化KR符号表示对三段论推理影响的系统探讨。

Method: 扩展FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，在不同KR形式化符号下构建三段论推理任务；在SLMs上分别进行有监督微调（SFT）和零样本（ZS）实验；提出SEF三段论分类方法，用逻辑定义丰富零样本提示以增强推理；开发并开源CLGC Python库，用于自动生成KR符号的三段论并定义其SEF类别。

Result: 实验表明，选择合适的输入符号表示可以在小语言模型上取得与自然语言相当的性能，同时实现更快的推理速度；使用SEF分类增强的零样本提示能够显著提升小模型的推理能力。

Conclusion: 知识表示符号的选择对语言模型的三段论推理有重要影响，KR符号化输入加上SEF逻辑定义增强是一种高效且有效的推理方案，适合资源受限的小模型场景。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) struggle with logical tasks like reasoning on syllogisms. It has been shown that Knowledge Representation (KR) plays a crucial role in expressing input information to help models solve tasks. This observation motivates our study of the impact of different formal KR notations on syllogistic reasoning by extending the FOLIO and P-FOLIO datasets. Our experiments on Small Language Models (SLMs) in Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Zero-Shot (ZS) settings show that the choice of input notation can yield performances competitive with natural language while enabling faster inference. We also propose a syllogistic categorization method (SEF) and use it to enrich ZS prompts with logical definitions, which boost reasoning in small models. We open-source our framework, Common Logic Grammar Construction (CLGC), as the first Python library for automatically generating syllogisms in KR notations and defining their SEF categories.

</details>


### [117] [Query Timing Produces Opposite Positional Biases Between LLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12387)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）中位置偏差（首因效应和近因效应）的底层机制，发现LLM与人类在证据呈现过程中的信念更新方式存在显著差异，且新模型的偏差比旧模型更为严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM中已被记录到诸如近因效应和首因效应等位置偏差，但其评估机制尚不明确。人类判断中也观察到首因和近因偏差，且最近研究表明"听众何时更新信念"（证据呈现期间还是结束时）会影响这些效应是否存在。作者希望探究LLM是否具有类似现象。

Method: 作者对比研究了LLM与人类在证据呈现过程中的信念更新时机对位置偏差的影响，具体考察LLM是在证据呈现过程中更新信念还是仅在结束时更新，并比较新旧模型的差异。

Result: 研究发现LLM在信念更新模式上与人类存在分歧（divergence），并且这些位置偏差在新模型中比其前辈模型中更加严重（more exacerbated）。

Conclusion: LLM的位置偏差机制与人类不同，且随着模型迭代，这些偏差反而加剧而非改善，这表明需要进一步研究模型评估的底层机制。

Abstract: Positional biases such as recency and primacy effects have been documented in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying mechanism by which these models make their evaluations remains poorly understood. Both primacy and recency biases have been observed in human judgments in response to evidence, but recent work suggest that \emph{when} the listener updates their beliefs -- during the presentation of evidence or only at the end -- influences the presence of such effects. We investigate whether a similar phenomenon holds for LLMs, finding divergence from human behavior. These biases are more exacerbated in newer models compared to their predecessors.

</details>


### [118] [Unified Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Complex Graph Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12391)
*Fali Wang,Ali Al-Lawati,Iliyas Bektas,Jinxuan Fang,Alek Melenski,Tianxiang Zhao,Yao Ma,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphGym，一个五阶段半自动框架，用于构建复杂图推理基准测试，在五个维度（图规模、任务复杂度、任务描述、图加载、任务来源）上扩展基准覆盖范围，并构建了包含202个任务的基准，在文本、代码和增强推理三种设置下评估LLM，揭示了现有基准难以发现的模型局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理基准存在三个关键局限：数据复杂度覆盖有限、过度依赖人工构建、缺乏跨文本和代码推理模式的统一评估。图推理虽然为评估LLM推理能力提供了理想测试平台（可程序化生成、结构可控、可自然扩展到长输入），但现有基准未能充分利用这些优势。

Method: 提出五阶段半自动框架，利用LLM-based数据生成器自动生成任务描述、图数据、参考解决方案、图加载脚本、问题形式和评估脚本，同时在关键质量控制阶段保留人工验证。基准覆盖五个复杂度维度：Graph Size（图大小）、Task Complexity（任务复杂度）、Task Description（任务描述）、Graph Loading（图加载方式）、Task Source（任务来源）。最终构建202个任务的基准，并在文本、代码和检索增强三种推理设置下评估LLM。

Result: 实验表明：复杂度维度揭示的模型缺陷在现有基准中不可见；现有微调模型难以泛化到GraphGym；检索增强方法表现出场景依赖的适应性——能改善文本推理但在编码推理上提升不一致。共评估了模型在文本、代码和增强推理三种设置下的表现。

Conclusion: GraphGym是一个具有挑战性和诊断性的图推理基准，能够发现现有基准掩盖的模型局限性，并为未来图推理增强方法提供经验性指导。代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Graph reasoning provides a promising testbed for evaluating the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), as graph instances can be programmatically generated, structurally controlled, and naturally scaled to long-input settings. However, existing graph reasoning benchmarks have limited coverage of data complexity, rely heavily on manual construction, and lack unified evaluation across text-based and code-based reasoning modes. To address these limitations, we propose {\dataset}, a five-stage \textit{semi-automatic} framework for constructing complex graph reasoning benchmarks. It expands benchmark coverage along five dimensions: \textit{Graph Size}, \textit{Task Complexity}, \textit{Task Description}, \textit{Graph Loading}, and \textit{Task Source}. The framework uses an LLM-based data generator to automatically produce task descriptions, graph data, reference solutions, graph-loading scripts, question forms, and evaluation scripts, while retaining human validation at key quality-control stages. Based on it, we construct a benchmark with $202$ tasks and evaluate LLMs under text-based, code-based, and augmented reasoning settings. Experiments show that the complexity dimensions reveal model limitations that are less visible in existing benchmarks; existing fine-tuned models struggle to generalize to GraphGym, whereas retrieval-augmented methods show scenario-dependent adaptability, improving textual reasoning but not consistently improving coding reasoning. These findings suggest that ours serves as a challenging and diagnostic benchmark for graph reasoning and provides empirical guidance for future enhancement methods. Code and dataset will be published soon.

</details>


### [119] [DIVE: Unlocking Self-Improvement in Frozen Language Models Through Diversity-Driven Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12486)
*Siheng Xiong,Ali Payani,Oguzhan Gungordu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DIVE提出了一种基于多样性驱动的自然语言技能演化框架，使冻结的大语言模型能够在不更新参数的情况下，通过从任务经验和验证器反馈中演化持久技能来自我改进，并在多个推理任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型无法在部署后仅凭经验进行改进而不更新参数。现有的推理方法、提示优化、技能开发框架和基于记忆的方法都无法有效解决这一问题。此外，自然语言技能演化是一个随机、非凸的搜索过程，单一技能轨迹容易过拟合或收敛到次优解。

Method: DIVE采用多样性驱动框架：从引导经验中独立演化多个技能种群，通过多样化的变换自适应地细化技能，并联合选择互补的技能集合。技能的编码内容涵盖可复用的推理过程、验证策略、常见失败模式和输出约束，由同一底层模型执行和修订，无需教师模型。

Result: 在六个数学和逻辑推理任务及多个模型家族上，DIVE一致优于现有推理方法、提示优化方法、技能开发框架和基于记忆的基线。它比SFT、GRPO和GEPA等参数型方法在更少的rollout中实现更大的性能提升。演化的技能可跨模型规模和家族迁移，使得GPT-5-nano等更小模型在常规提示下能匹配或超越更大模型。

Conclusion: 多样性驱动的技能演化被证明是一种有效、可解释且免参数的大语言模型自我改进方法，通过演化多样化的技能种群而非单一轨迹来规避优化方差，实现了持久学习和跨模型迁移。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) cannot retain post-deployment experience without parameter updates. We introduce DIVE, a diversity-driven framework that enables frozen LLMs to improve by evolving persistent natural-language skills from task experience and verifier feedback. These skills encode reusable reasoning procedures, verification strategies, common failure modes, and output constraints and are both executed and revised by the same underlying model without access to a teacher model. Since natural-language skill evolution is a stochastic, non-convex search process, optimizing a single skill trajectory can overfit to sampled experience or converge to a suboptimal solution. DIVE mitigates this optimization variance by independently evolving multiple skill populations from bootstrapped experience, adaptively refining them through diverse transformations, and jointly selecting a complementary set of skills. Across six mathematical and logical reasoning tasks and multiple model families, DIVE consistently outperforms existing reasoning methods, prompt-optimization approaches, skill-development frameworks, and memory-based baselines. It achieves rapid self-improvement from accumulated experience, obtaining substantially larger performance gains with fewer rollouts than parameter-based methods such as SFT and GRPO, and prompt optimization with GEPA. Further, the resulting skills transfer across model scales and families, enabling smaller models such as GPT-5-nano to match or outperform larger counterparts, i.e., GPT-5, under conventional prompting. These results establish diversity-driven skill evolution as an effective, interpretable, and parameter-free approach to LLM self-improvement.

</details>


### [120] [Intensional Anaphora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12598)
*Ezra Keshet,Steven Abney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出传统的将内涵算子处理为可能世界量词的做法无法正确处理内涵语境中的回指现象，并论证了基于描述而非基于值的回指账户才是正确的解释方式。作者提出了一个新的逻辑系统（PIP，多元内涵预设谓词演算），该逻辑可直接翻译为带集合抽象的标准一阶谓词演算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将内涵算子类比为限定词（个体量词），但内涵语境中引入的个体无法像普通量化语境中的个体那样作为后续代词的先行语。Stone (1999) 等人通过要求代词的值（指称对象）必须存在于评价世界来处理这一问题，但作者发现这一方法既不充分也不必要：有些情况下指称对象明确存在却仍不允许回指，有些情况下指称对象可能不存在却允许回指。

Method: 作者提出基于描述的（description-based）而非基于值的（value-based）回指分析：代词预设其对应的先行语描述在语境集的每个可能世界中都被实例化。这一分析用一个新的逻辑系统实现，即多元内涵预设谓词演算（PIP），建立在 Keshet (2018) 和 Abney and Keshet (2022) 的基础上。每个 PIP 公式可直接翻译为标准带集合抽象的一阶谓词演算。

Result: 该框架能够同时解释两类现象：（1）在指称对象明确存在但每个候选世界中先行语描述未被实例化时，回指被禁止；（2）在指称对象可能不存在但所有候选世界中描述都被实例化时，回指被允许。通过将内涵回指的条件放在"描述在语境集的所有世界中成立"上，而非"值在世界中真实存在"上，该分析更准确地刻画了内涵回指的语言事实。

Conclusion: 内涵回指应当从描述的角度来理解：代词预设的是先行语描述在语境集的每个候选世界中都被实例化，而非其指称对象必须在现实世界中存在。PIP 逻辑系统为这一分析提供了形式化基础和经典逻辑基础。

Abstract: Intensional operators are often treated as quantifiers over possible worlds, parallel to the treatment of determiners as quantifiers over individuals. Yet individuals introduced in intensional contexts cannot serve as antecedents to later pronouns as easily as those introduced in merely quantificational contexts. For instance, "Everyone is eating a cheeseburger" may be followed by "They are large", where "they" refers to the cheeseburgers being eaten. However, as Stone (1999) points out, the similar "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger" does not support later anaphoric references such as "It is large" or "They are large". Stone (1999), Stone and Hardt (1999), and Brasoveanu (2010) address this by requiring a pronoun's value (its referents) to exist in the world of evaluation, ruling out anaphora from non-veridical intensional contexts. We show, however, both cases where such anaphora is disallowed even when the pronoun's referents clearly exist and cases where it is allowed even though they might not exist. We argue that intensional anaphora is best captured using a description-based rather than value-based account. A pronoun presupposes that its corresponding antecedent description is instantiated in each world of the context set. Thus, there must be a cheeseburger being eaten by Andrea in every candidate world for "It is large" to be felicitous after "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger". We implement our proposal via a new logic, building on Keshet (2018) and Abney and Keshet (2022), called Plural Intensional Presuppositional predicate calculus (PIP). Each PIP formula translates directly into standard first-order predicate calculus with set abstraction, providing a classical foundation for this work.

</details>


### [121] [When Explanations Betray Backdoors: Black-Box Auditing for Language Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12623)
*Yang Liu,Ran Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Groundedness Drift，一种轻量级评分方法，用于检测语言模型分类器在面对触发器后门攻击时的恶意行为。该方法通过衡量答案摘要是否仍基于（grounded）于输入文本，在多种攻击家族下优于所有对比检测器，并进一步提出Unsupported Groundedness多探针升级方案应对解释伪装攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分类器被广泛应用于内容审核、路由、主题分类和低资源标注等场景，但这些分类器可能被后门攻击利用。现有的黑盒审计方法通常需要触发器信息或仅依赖标签，而许多实际场景中防御者只有干净的校准数据，且分类器会返回标签加简短解释（rationale）。如何在此受限设置下有效检测被植入后门的分类器是一个未充分研究的问题。

Method: 本文提出Groundedness Drift评分，用于衡量模型输出的答案摘要是否仍保持对输入文本的grounding（即摘要中是否有事实性或引用性的支撑来源于输入）。该方法仅需干净校准数据，无需触发器信息。进一步提出Unsupported Groundedness，一种多探针（multi-probe）升级策略，用于应对解释伪装（explanation-camouflage）攻击的极端情况。

Result: 在两个7B骨干模型、五个数据集和四种常见的非自适应OpenBackdoor风格攻击家族上，Groundedness Drift在5%干净误报率（FPR）预算下，所有案例的AUROC和残余目标ASR均优于所有对比检测器。Unsupported Groundedness进一步改善了检测信号，但未能完全消除自适应攻击带来的差距。

Conclusion: Groundedness Drift是一种有效且实用的后门审计方法，利用分类器解释中的grounding一致性来检测恶意行为。虽然Unsupported Groundedness增强了对抗解释伪装攻击的鲁棒性，但自适应攻击仍构成威胁，说明解释增强的防御方法存在基本局限，需要在未来的审计框架中进一步探索。

Abstract: Language model classifiers with explanations are used for moderation, routing, topic triage, and low-resource annotation. We study black-box auditing when the defender has only clean calibration data without trigger information but can ask the classifier for a label plus a short rationale or quoted evidence. We introduce Groundedness Drift, a lightweight score measuring whether the answer summary remains grounded in the input. Across two 7B backbones, five datasets, and four common non-adaptive OpenBackdoor-style attack families, Groundedness Drift achieves higher AUROC and lower residual target ASR than every compared detector in all cases at a nominal 5\% clean-FPR budget. We then evaluate Unsupported Groundedness, a multi-probe escalation for explanation-camouflage stress cases. Unsupported Groundedness improves signals but does not close the adaptive gap.

</details>


### [122] [LLMs Are Not Good Strategists, Yet Memory-Enhanced Agency Boosts Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12626)
*Yi Wu,Zhimin Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpicStar框架，通过将记忆作为策略（memory as policy）来解决LLM在长时间跨度环境中因有限注意力导致的策略漂移问题，在StarCraft II测试中显著优于基线方法，实现了更高的胜率同时降低了一个数量级的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时间跨度环境中进行战略推理时，由于有限的注意力资源无法在数千步中维持战略连贯性，导致策略漂移（strategic drift），即局部决策无法维持连贯的推理轨迹。现有方法缺乏有效的跨回合记忆机制来解决这一根本性局限。

Method: EpicStar框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 成功过往回合的记忆库作为启发式经验；(2) 工作记忆用于跟踪短期环境变化；(3) 动态门控机制在推理时决定是直接执行检索到的动作还是通过检索到的回合与当前工作记忆的上下文融合进行新推理。

Result: 在StarCraft II中针对多种对手风格进行评估，EpicStar显著超越基线方法，实现了更高的胜率，消耗的token数量减少了一个数量级，并且在难度级别和对手策略上保持一致的优势。

Conclusion: 结构化跨回合记忆对于使LLM智能体在动态、自主环境中执行稳健的长期战略是至关重要的，为长时序自主决策提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) within long-horizon environments is often limited by inconsistent subgoals. In these settings, finite attention resources prevent the model from maintaining strategic coherence over thousands of steps. This limitation leads to strategic drift, where localized decisions fail to sustain a coherent trajectory across reasoning. To address this, we introduce EpicStar, a framework that enables agents to learn memory as policy to tackle long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, the agent maintains a bank of successful past episodes as a heuristic alongside a working memory to track short-term environmental changes. During inference, a dynamic gating mechanism determines whether to execute a retrieved action directly or to perform new reasoning through a contextual fusion of the retrieved episodes and current working memory. Utilizing StarCraft II as the testbed, we evaluated EpicStar against diverse opponent styles. It significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving higher win rates while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and it maintains this advantage consistently across difficulty levels and opponent strategies. Our findings provide compelling evidence that structured cross-episode memory is essential for enabling LLM agents to perform robust, long-term strategic execution in dynamic, autonomous settings.

</details>


### [123] [Novels generated by language models show compressed formal variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12630)
*Mehdy Sedaghat Payam,Justin Quinn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了AI生成的40部小说（GPT-5.5 Thinking和Qwen3-14B各生成20部维多利亚风格和20部当代零风格小说）与270部人类小说在文档层面的文体多样性，发现AI反复生成的小说在句子结构、可读性、标点和句子长度变异性上均存在"压缩"现象，即AI作品之间文体差异远小于人类作品，提出了"方差过度封闭"与"相关性过度封闭"两个概念来区分这种多样性缺失现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单篇文本能否被识别为AI生成，但很少关注AI在多次随机生成中是否能复现人类语料库中存在的文体多样性范围。本研究旨在探讨反复AI生成能否产生与人类语料库同等的形式多样性水平。

Method: 构建六个语料库：GPT-5.5 Thinking生成20部十九世纪英国现实主义风格小说、Qwen3-14B生成20部同风格小说及两种模型各20部当代零风格小说，并以上下文相关的205部十九世纪人类小说和65部当代零风格人类小说为对照。在文档层面计算MATTR-500（词汇多样性）、香农熵、平均句子长度、可读性、标点密度等指标，并进行跨语料库比较和相关分析。

Result: 最稳健的结果是句子结构的压缩：AI生成小说之间的句子结构差异远小于人类小说之间的差异。可读性、标点和句内句子长度变异性也呈现压缩现象。词汇指标大多被压缩，但Qwen零风格小说的MATTR例外。GPT和Qwen虽然具有不同的平均文体特征，但缺乏稳定的跨指标相关性模式。

Conclusion: 单篇AI生成小说可能在文体上接近人类虚构作品，但AI生成小说的集合占据了更窄的形式空间。研究区分了"方差过度封闭"（小说间形式范围有限）和"相关性过度封闭"（指标间相关模式异常）两种现象，提示评估AI生成文本应关注语料层面的多样性而非单篇质量。

Abstract: While large language models can generate entire novels, there is little information about the level of formal variation in their output over many generations. Rather than asking whether individual passages can be identified as AI-generated, this study asks whether repeated AI generation can produce the same range of diversity which is found across human corpora. This paper contrasts six corpora based on generation source and target style: twenty novels generated using GPT-5.5 Thinking in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using Qwen3-14B in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using each of these models in a contemporary zero style, 205 nineteenth-century human-written British novels, and sixty-five contemporary human-written Zero-Style novels. At the document level, the research includes MATTR-500, Shannon entropy, average sentence length, readability, and punctuation rate measurements. The most robust and reliable result is compression of sentence structure. Repeated generations produce novels that vary far less from one another in sentence structure than human novels do. Compression is also present in the measures of readability, punctuation, and sentence length variability within novels. Lexical measures tend to be similarly compressed, with the exception of Qwen Zero-Style MATTR. Despite having distinct mean stylistic profiles, GPT and Qwen lack a stable pattern of cross-measure correlation. This article therefore distinguishes between variance overclosure, which represents a limited formal range between novels, and a more specific phenomenon of correlational overclosure. This means that an individual AI-generated novel may resemble human fiction stylistically, while a collection of AI-generated novels occupies a much narrower formal range.

</details>


### [124] [Excess Separability: Nuisance-Controlled Residual-Stream Probing for Benchmark Contamination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12652)
*Florian Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何从内部激活中读取基准污染（benchmark contamination），指出"在线性探针上读取污染"这一自然方法不可行，并提出了一套能经受测量的替代协议。该协议采用零和对仗设计（深度探针精度的深度剖面、水平匹配安慰剂基线、标签置换零假设），并证明各设计选择均优于被否定的更简单方案。在真实Transformer上，基线深度剖面并非平坦，且其非平坦程度与项目集表面差异显著相关（相关系数0.87），修正恰好发生在最需要的地方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准污染检测方法（n-gram重叠、基于似然的成员推理、金丝雀字符串）都依赖通常不可得的资源：训练语料库、精心选择的检验统计量、或在数据集发布时的先见之明。新近提出的方法尝试从内部激活的线性探针读取污染信息，但作者证明这种"自然方式"并不奏效，因此需要设计一种能够经受测量考验的替代协议。

Method: 提出一个零和对比协议：在探针精度的深度剖面上进行对比，以水平匹配的安慰剂基线为中心，针对标签置换零假设进行检验，参考集大小为可疑集的两倍。每个设计决策都替换并否定了更简单的替代方案。通过对照实验测量了各替代方案的失败模式：平坦深度剖面对比在两个方向都失败（真实零假设下0.72的拒绝率，深度可解码性上升时完全丧失功效）；项目自助法（bootstrap）固定已拟合探针，在置换零假设只拒绝0.02的情况下拒绝了0.09；半大小基线使错误率翻三倍。

Result: 在真实Transformer上，基线深度剖面明显不平坦，在时间分割上跨越高达29.1个准确率百分点，且其非平坦性追踪项目集之间的表面差异（6次审计相关系数0.87）。所有4个匹配良好的Pile分支均返回零结果，协议对时间分割拒绝给出判定而非报告结果。唯一的正结果出现在可交换性失效的分割上。

Conclusion: 水平匹配的安慰剂基线、充足大小的参考集以及适当的置换零假设是该协议的关键要素。该协议并未确立Transformer是否携带"熟悉度方向"（familiarity direction），因为唯一正值出现在可交换性失效的分割上。论文发布了完整的实现、测试和审计代码。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination is diagnosed today with n-gram overlap, with likelihood-based membership inference, or with canary strings, and each needs something usually unavailable: the training corpus, a well-chosen test statistic, or foresight at dataset release. A recent alternative reads contamination off a linear probe on internal activations. We show that the natural way to do this does not work, and specify one that survives measurement.
  The protocol reports a zero-sum contrast on the depth profile of probe accuracy, recentred on a level-matched placebo baseline, tested against a label-permutation null, with the reference set twice the size of the suspect set. Each choice replaces a simpler alternative we measured and rejected. Reporting the level of excess separability rather than its shape makes the false positive rate track the size of the analyst's own control set, from 0.03 to 0.99 under a true null. Contrasting against a flat depth profile fails in both directions, rejecting a true null 0.72 of the time when surface decodability rises with depth and losing all power when it falls. An item bootstrap holds the fitted probe fixed and rejects up to 0.09 of the time where a permutation null that refits it holds 0.02. A half-size baseline triples the error rate.
  On real transformers, baseline depth profiles are measurably not flat, spanning up to 29.1 accuracy points on a temporal split, and their non-flatness tracks the surface difference between the item sets (correlation 0.87 over 6 audits), so the correction is largest exactly where it is needed. All 4 well-matched Pile arms return null, and the protocol refuses a verdict on the temporal split rather than reporting one. What this does not establish is whether transformers carry a familiarity direction at all: the only positive sits on the split where exchangeability fails. Implementation, tests and audits are released.

</details>


### [125] [ERSkill: Evolving for Skill-Guided Adaptive Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12720)
*Haolong Chen,Liang Zhang,Zhuo Li,Lei Xue,Guanrxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ERSkill是一个面向LLM代理的检索中心框架，将长期记忆的检索行为建模为可执行技能，并让技能集与路由决策器协同进化，以动态匹配查询到最优检索策略，从而在异构记忆查询上显著超越静态和自进化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理日益依赖长期记忆进行持续交互，但控制记忆访问的检索机制很少被视为可进化的组件。这种静态方法在处理异构记忆查询时效果有限，因为不同查询往往需要不同的证据构建策略。

Method: ERSkill将交互历史编译为结构化记忆库，并将检索行为表示为由基础原语组成的可执行技能。推理时，训练好的路由器动态将每个查询匹配到最优技能以构建定制证据。训练过程中，ERSkill协同进化技能集和路由器，使用经验trie高效记录已探索的检索路径，并采用双前沿机制将新技能能力的扩展与面向路由器的稳定部署安全解耦。

Result: 在多个代理记忆基准上，ERSkill显著优于强非进化和自进化基线。使用Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct时，F1、BLEU-1和LLM-judge得分的整体平均值提升31.3%；使用GPT-5.4-nano时提升28.1%。

Conclusion: 将记忆检索视为可进化、可组合的技能，并通过协同进化方式持续优化技能集与路由策略，能够显著提升LLM代理在异构记忆查询上的表现，为长期记忆管理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on long-term memory for persistent interactions, the retrieval mechanisms governing this memory are rarely treated as evolvable components. This static approach limits performance on heterogeneous memory queries, which often demand diverse evidence construction strategies. To address this, we introduce \textbf{ERSkill}, a retrieval-centric framework for self-evolving, skill-guided memory access. ERSkill compiles interaction histories into a structured memory store and represents retrieval behaviors as executable skills composed of fundamental primitives. At inference time, a trained router dynamically matches each query to the optimal skill to construct tailored evidence for answer generation. To enable continuous improvement, ERSkill co-evolves the skill set and the router during training. It employs an experience trie to efficiently record explored retrieval paths, alongside a double-frontier mechanism that safely decouples the expansion of new skill capabilities from stable, router-facing deployment. Experiments across multiple agent memory benchmarks demonstrate that ERSkill substantially outperforms strong non-evolving and self-evolving baselines. Notably, it improves the overall average across F1, BLEU-1, and LLM-judge scores by 31.3\% with Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct and by 28.1\% with GPT-5.4-nano.

</details>


### [126] [PatientAct: Theory-Grounded Mental Health Client Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12750)
*Sahand Sabour,TszYam NG,Yaqian Chen,Guanqun Bi,Jialu Zhao,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PatientAct是一个基于临床理论（5Ps案例构建）的LLM客户端模拟框架，通过引入动态记忆层、信任阈值和分层抵抗机制，解决了现有模拟器过度配合、过早披露、单次会话内解决核心问题等缺陷，在40个临床情境中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM模拟客户端过于配合：过度披露信息、轻易接受治疗性重构、在单次会话内解决核心问题。作者将这些缺陷归因于两个方面：配置文件缺乏因果深度，以及行为机制将所有内容视为同等可访问。这限制了模拟在心理咨询师培训、LLM治疗师评估和合成数据生成中的应用价值。

Method: PatientAct框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 配置文件整合5Ps临床案例构建（呈现问题、易感因素、诱发因素、持续因素、保护因素），提供因果深度且不绑定单一治疗模式；(2) 模拟过程中，配置文件包含动态记忆层，各记忆项目带信任阈值（如症状早期可得，形成性记忆需长期治疗联盟），每轮对话先建模客户情绪反应和行为再生成回复，面对受限内容时在数量、内容、风格三个维度表达抵抗，而非默认合作或单一抵抗模式。

Result: 在40个临床情境上评估，PatientAct生成具有高临床可信度的多样化配置文件，并在抵抗质量和行为真实感方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于临床理论构建的模拟客户端框架能够生成更真实、更具临床意义的模拟行为，为心理咨询师培训和LLM治疗系统评估提供更有效的工具。

Abstract: LLM-based simulated clients are increasingly used to train novice counselors, evaluate LLM therapists, and generate synthetic data. However, current simulators produce overly cooperative clients that disclose too readily, accept therapeutic reframes without resistance, and resolve core issues within a single session. We trace these issues to profiles that lack causal depth and behavioral mechanisms that treat all content as equally accessible. We present PatientAct, a framework for client simulation grounded in established clinical theories. Our profiles integrate the 5Ps clinical case formulation, providing causal depth without tying the design to any single therapeutic modality. During simulation, profiles include a dynamic memory layer in which items carry trust thresholds (e.g., symptoms are available early, whereas formative memories require a sustained therapeutic alliance). At each turn, the client's emotional reaction and behavior are modeled before generating a response. If the therapist approaches gated content, PatientAct expresses resistance in terms of quantity, content, and style rather than defaulting to cooperation or a single resistance pattern. We evaluate our framework on 40 clinical situations and demonstrate that it generates diverse profiles with high clinical plausibility. Moreover, PatientAct significantly outperforms the baselines, yielding substantial gains in resistance quality and behavioral realism. Our code and data will be publicly available via github.com/Sahandfer/PatientHub.

</details>


### [127] [ReconSpan: Reconstruction-Guided Adaptive Latent Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12756)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReconSpan，一种自适应潜在分词方法。它通过将文本划分为可由单向解码器从单个上下文前缀码重建的块，并将每个块保留为一个潜在token，从而将细粒度输入压缩为更短的连续表示序列。训练得到的自编码器可产生6.5到12.2的平均块长度，且在相同平均长度下，重建引导的分割边界比随机边界保留更多文本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分词方法通常使用固定粒度或不依赖内容的切分，导致潜在token化后的表示未能充分利用文本的结构信息。作者希望实现输入依赖的自适应分词，使每个潜在token能够编码更多可重建的信息，从而提升压缩效率和信息保留能力。

Method: ReconSpan将文本划分为块，每个块由一个反向解码器从单个上下文前缀码重建。分割边界根据重建准则确定（即块形成时应用重建标准），并使用一个训练好的自编码器生成潜在token序列。该方法支持平均块长度在6.5到12.2之间灵活调整。

Result: 在匹配平均块长度的情况下，由重建引导确定的边界比随机边界保留了更多文本信息。然而，从所得潜在序列中，读者能够可靠地恢复主题信息，但在提取精确细节方面存在困难。

Conclusion: ReconSpan展示了自适应潜在分词在压缩文本表示方面的有效性，重建引导的分割优于随机分割。但其信息保留主要集中在主题层面，对精确细节的编码仍有局限，未来工作需在细节保真度上进行改进。

Abstract: Adaptive latent tokenization maps a fine-grained input to a shorter sequence of continuous representations associated with input-dependent spans. We introduce ReconSpan, which divides text into chunks that a backward decoder can reconstruct from a single contextual prefix code and retains one such code as the latent token for each chunk. The reconstruction criterion is applied when chunks are formed, allowing one trained autoencoder to produce average chunk lengths from 6.5 to 12.2. At matched average length, reconstruction-guided boundaries preserve more text than random boundaries. Readers of the resulting latent sequence recover topic information reliably but struggle to extract exact details.

</details>


### [128] [ViTOED: A Dataset for Target-Oriented Emotion Detection on Vietnamese Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12776)
*Chanh Vo,Son T. Luu,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTOED，一个面向越南语社交媒体文本的目标导向情感检测数据集，包含10,985条用户评论和21,244个手工标注的意见四元组（来源、目标、表达、极性），并提出了基于结构化情感图基线与多项预训练语言模型的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向情感检测在越南语领域缺乏专门的数据集和系统性研究，且越南语社交媒体文本存在隐式来源/目标和词汇歧义等特有现象，需要构建相应的数据集以支持该任务的研究与发展。

Method: 构建了包含21,244个严格标注的意见四元组的ViTOED数据集，提出了使用结构化情感图的基线方法，并评估了多种越南语预训练语言模型。

Result: 实验结果表明该任务在跨度检测和关系抽取方面存在显著挑战，当前模型在越南语目标导向情感检测任务上仍有较大的改进空间。

Conclusion: ViTOED数据集揭示了越南语特有的语言现象，为越南语目标导向情感检测提供了新的基准，但现有模型性能尚不理想，需要进一步研究和提升。

Abstract: This paper introduces ViTOED, a novel dataset for target-oriented emotion detection in Vietnamese social media texts. The ViTOED comprises 10,985 user comments and 21,244 manually annotated opinion quadruples (source, target, expression, polarity) that follow strict guidelines. The dataset reveals Vietnamese-specific phenomena, such as implicit sources and targets and vocabulary ambiguities, enabling deeper analysis of user emotions toward entities. We propose a baseline using structured sentiment graphs and evaluate various Vietnamese pre-trained language models. The empirical results highlight challenges in span detection and relation extraction and indicate substantial room for model improvement in Vietnamese Target-Oriented Emotion Detection tasks.

</details>


### [129] [CRAFT: LLM-Based Iterative Refinement for Temporal Reasoning over Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12779)
*Chengyang He,Tahreem Arif,Marko Zivkovic,Lijing Wang,Yue Ning,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，通过生成器与基于约束的验证器迭代配合，从锚点稀疏的临床叙述中重建分阶段症状时间线；在包含5,347篇疫苗不良事件叙述的新基准MedTempo上进行评估，四个LLM骨干模型的实验表明CRAFT持续提升时间排序准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床叙述中的时间信息推理对疾病监测、安全监控和因果关系评估至关重要，但现有方法主要聚焦于多访视、时间戳丰富的记录中的成对关系分类，未能解决从锚点稀疏的个体报告中重建结构化症状轨迹的问题。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，将生成器（LLM）与基于约束的验证器配对，通过针对性反馈迭代地生成和精炼分阶段症状时间线；同时构建MedTempo基准，包含5,347篇覆盖三种COVID-19疫苗类型的疫苗不良事件叙述，其中3,166篇带有专家验证的时间阶段注释。

Result: 在四个LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，CRAFT一致性地提升了时间排序准确性；消融分析进一步分离了生成器和验证器组件在不同模型能力水平下的各自贡献。

Conclusion: CRAFT在锚点稀疏的临床叙述时间线重建任务上展现了有效性，通过生成-验证迭代机制有效弥补了单一模型的不足，为临床事件时间推理提供了新的方法论范式。

Abstract: Understanding the temporal progression of symptoms in clinical narratives is critical for disease monitoring, safety surveillance, and causality assessment. Clinical narratives, however, rarely provide explicit temporal anchors. Current approaches to temporal information reasoning focus predominantly on pairwise relation classification across multi-visit and timestamp-rich records, leaving the reconstruction of structured symptom trajectories from individual anchor-sparse reports largely unaddressed. We propose CRAFT, an LLM framework that pairs a generator with a constraint-based verifier to iteratively produce and refine stage-wise symptom timelines through targeted feedback. We conduct evaluation on MedTempo, a new benchmark of 5,347 vaccine adverse-event narratives spanning three COVID-19 vaccine types, with expert-validated temporal stage annotations for 3,166 reports. Experiments across four LLM backbones demonstrate that CRAFT consistently improves temporal ordering accuracy, with ablation analysis isolating the contribution of generator and verifier components across model capability levels.

</details>


### [130] [FastThaiG2P: Lightning-fast Thai Grapheme-to-phoneme Conversion for Voice Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12814)
*Charin Polpanumas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FastThaiG2P是一个用于泰语文本转音素的亚毫秒级系统，基于PyThaiNLP分词和可扩展词典，在27,242条合成语料的基准上平均延迟仅0.15毫秒/句，并成功用于训练StyleTTS 2泰语语音合成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰语文本到音素（G2P）转换是语音合成（TTS）流水线的关键环节，需要高效且准确的转换以支持实时或近实时的语音合成应用。

Method: 采用PyThaiNLP分词、可扩展字典以及针对标准中部泰语发音的规范化规则，支持IPA和Kokoro-TTS两种音素约定；对未知词汇（OOV）使用回退机制处理。

Result: 平均延迟0.15毫秒/句（分词占30%、规范化占12%、OOV回退占58%，OOV率仅0.5%）；用其音素化20小时Som-TTS数据集后训练的82M参数StyleTTS 2模型，在CPU上以ONNX推理实现0.25实时因子（4倍实时）。

Conclusion: FastThaiG2P为泰语TTS提供高效、可扩展的G2P解决方案，生成的语音模型能输出可理解的泰语语音，适合原型开发场景。

Abstract: FastThaiG2P provides sub-millisecond Thai grapheme-to-phoneme conversion for text-to-speech pipelines (International Phonetic Alphabet and Kokoro-TTS conventions) using a PyThaiNLP-tokenized, extensible dictionary and normalization rules for common Central Thai speech. The approach achieves an average latency of 0.15 ms per utterance on a benchmark of 27,242 synthetically generated utterances, of which 30\% is spent on tokenization, 12\% on normalization, and 58\% on out-of-vocabulary fallbacks (0.5\% OOV rate). To demonstrate its effectiveness, we used FastThaiG2P to phonemize Som-TTS, an open dataset containing 20 hours of grapheme-and-audio pairs, then trained an 82M-parameter StyleTTS 2 model based on a Kokoro-TTS recipe. The resulting model vocalizes intelligible Thai speech suitable for prototyping and development at 0.25 real-time factor (4x real-time) with ONNX inference on CPU.

</details>


### [131] [From Atomic Evidence to Logical Composition: Structured Compositional Reasoning over Compound Answer Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12836)
*Obed Junias,Maria Leonor Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型在处理包含AND、OR、NEITHER/NOR等逻辑运算符的复合选项时经常失败的问题，提出了一种将复合选项分解为原子答案、分别打分，再通过受操作符约束的整数线性规划组合成最终预测的框架。在LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA和LOGICAL-SATA基准上，该框架将Macro-F1分别从48.3提升到77.0、从47.0提升到75.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型即使能正确判断单个原子判断，当选项要求在这些原子判断上应用显式逻辑运算符进行组合时，仍然经常失败。这说明模型缺乏对逻辑结构的系统化推理能力。

Method: 提出一个分解-组合框架：首先将每个复合选项分解为原子答案，对每个原子分别评估对比性假设并生成校准后的分数；然后使用受逻辑操作符（AND、OR、NEITHER/NOR）约束的整数线性规划（ILP）将原子分数组合为单一预测。框架确保模型从不直接看到复合选项。

Result: 在人工验证的LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA子集上，Macro-F1从48.3提升至77.0；在从SATA-Bench衍生的LOGICAL-SATA阅读理解基准上，从47.0提升至75.6。其中在NEITHER/NOR类型选项上的提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过将逻辑组合问题从模型内部推理中剥离，转化为可解析的优化问题，可以显著提升LLM在复合逻辑选项上的表现，为逻辑敏感的自然语言理解提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models often fail when answer options require combining atomic judgments under explicit logical operators, even when they judge the individual atoms correctly. We study compound options connected by AND, OR, and NEITHER/NOR, introducing a framework that decomposes each option into atomic answers and scores contrastive hypotheses about each one, so the model never sees a compound option. An operator-constrained integer linear program then composes the calibrated scores into a single prediction. We evaluate on LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA and introduce LOGICAL-SATA, a reading-comprehension benchmark derived from SATA-Bench. Our framework improves Macro-F1 from 48.3 to 77.0 on the human-validated LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA split and from 47.0 to 75.6 on LOGICAL-SATA, with the largest gains on NEITHER/NOR.

</details>


### [132] [AQuA: Recursively Self-Improving Quantitative Trading Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12841)
*Jiacheng Guo,Suozhi Huang,Yunlong Gao,Zihao Li,Jian Ge,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了量化投资研究中的递归自我改进问题，提出了AQuA系统，包含两个独立的大语言模型驱动研究系统：一个用于符号因子发现，一个用于可训练模型开发。两个系统各自在密封沙盒中闭环运行，利用已验证证据指导后续提案。因子系统在加密货币上达到约0.190的综合信息系数，模型系统在美国股票上取得+0.0843的每股信息系数，并转化为持有期Sharpe比率高达+2.50的多空策略，2021至2025年每年均为正收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自主系统能否在量化投资研究层面实现递归自我改进，即利用先前实验的证据来改进后续迭代中提出的假设和候选方案，从而验证AI系统在真实研究过程中的自组织能力。

Method: AQuA由两个独立的LM驱动研究系统组成：1)符号因子发现系统，采用管理者中介的多代理流水线，通过保留已验证证据并用于指导后续因子提议；2)可训练模型开发系统，采用配置驱动的循环迭代，基于混合时间序列架构。两系统不共享代理、记忆、候选空间或研究状态，各自在密封沙盒中运行，固定数据切分、特征和标签定义及评估器，模型只能通过受限的因子表达式或配置差异进行操作。

Result: 因子系统在加密货币领域中发现的组合信号达到约0.190的综合信息系数(IC)；模型系统在美国股票上达到每股+0.0843的信息系数，并将其转化为两腿成本下的阈值多空策略，持有期Sharpe比率高达+2.50，且2021至2025年每年均为正收益。

Conclusion: 两个独立系统均在各自封闭的研究循环中实现了有界意义上的递归自我改进，显著提升了信号质量和策略表现，证明了LM驱动的自主研究系统在量化投资领域具备实际有效的自我迭代能力。

Abstract: We study recursive self-improvement at the level of quantitative-investment research: whether an autonomous system can use evidence from earlier experiments to improve the hypotheses and candidates proposed in later iterations. We present AQuA, which comprises two separate language-model-driven research systems: one for symbolic factor discovery and one for trainable model development. The two systems do not share agents, memories, candidate spaces, or research state. Instead, each independently closes its own research loop by retaining validated evidence and using it to guide subsequent proposals. In this bounded sense, both systems implement recursive self-improvement at the level of the research process. Each system also uses its own sealed sandbox, which fixes the data splits, feature and label definitions, and evaluator while allowing the model to act only through constrained factor expressions or configuration diffs. The factor system, a manager-mediated multi-agent pipeline, discovers and combines factors into a signal that reaches a combined information coefficient of about $0.190$ on a crypto universe. The model system, a config-driven loop over a hybrid time-series architecture, reaches a per-stock information coefficient of $+0.0843$ on US equities and converts it into a threshold long/short strategy with a held-out Sharpe of up to $+2.50$ at a two-leg cost. The strategy is positive in every year from 2021 to 2025.

</details>


### [133] [Falsehood and Impossibility Are Different Directions in an AI's Representation of Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12852)
*Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对Gemma 3 4B IT多模态模型的探索性激活研究，发现该模型在回答中将偶然性虚假与矛盾混为一谈，但其内部激活空间中线性探针能有效区分不可能与真实陈述（AUC 0.93）以及必然性虚假与偶然性虚假（AUC 1.00）。研究还发现不可能性的神经表征与语义异常部分重叠但可区分，表明在该模型的激活空间中，必然性虚假更接近语义异常而非偶然性虚假的极端情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言可以描述虚假的事态，也可以描述根本不可能的事态。AI模型内部是否区分这两种失败方式尚不清楚。作者希望探究多模态模型在内部表征层面是否区分"虚假"（contingent falsehood）与"不可能"（impossibility）这两个哲学上本质不同的范畴，为经典的哲学区分提供经验层面的注脚。

Method: 使用85个来自17个哲学家族的提示词和15个话题匹配的模态集，每个话题以真理、偶然虚假、不可能主张、语义异常和必然虚假五种形式表达。采用线性真值探针和不可能性探针分析模型各层的激活模式，并使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征检验激活空间中的几何结构。通过保留话题家族的交叉验证评估探针泛化能力，并使用Bonferroni校正控制多重比较。

Result: 模型输出将12/15的虚假陈述标记为"矛盾"，但内部激活模式不同：真值探针以AUC 0.93区分不可能与真命题，但仅AUC 0.20区分不可能与虚假命题；不可能性探针在保留话题家族上以AUC 1.00区分必然性与偶然性虚假，在第15层峰值平衡准确率0.97（Bonferroni校正P=0.018）。真值与不可能性方向近乎正交，不可能性方向与语义异常方向部分重叠但可区分。SAE特征复现了此几何结构：对不可能性选择性的特征也激活于异常句，但很少激活于偶然性虚假。

Conclusion: 在该模型的激活空间中，必然性虚假并非偶然性虚假的极端情形，而是更接近语义异常这一实验性定义类别。这种表征上的邻近不代表不可能陈述本质上是无意义的，但为经典哲学区分提供了来自小模型的经验脚注。

Abstract: Language can describe states of affairs that are false and states of affairs that could not be the case at all. Whether an AI model internally distinguishes these failures remains unclear. I report an exploratory activation study of the multimodal open-weight model Gemma 3 4B IT using 85 prompts from 17 philosophical families and a topic-matched modality set of 15 topics, each expressed as a truth, contingent falsehood, improbable claim, semantic anomaly, and necessary falsehood. In its answers, the model conflates contingent falsehood with contradiction, labeling 12 of 15 false statements "contradiction." Its activations show a different pattern. A linear truth probe separates impossible from true statements (AUC 0.93) but not impossible from false statements (AUC 0.20). An impossibility probe evaluated on held-out topic families separates necessary from contingent falsehood at AUC 1.00, peaking at layer 15 with balanced accuracy 0.97 (Bonferroni-adjusted P=0.018). The truth and impossibility directions are close to orthogonal, whereas the impossibility direction partially overlaps a semantic anomaly direction while remaining distinguishable from it. Sparse autoencoder features at the same layer repeat this geometry. Features selective for impossibility also fire on anomalous sentences but rarely on contingent falsehoods. In this model's activation space, necessary falsehoods are not extreme cases of contingent falsehood but lie closer to the experimentally defined category of semantic anomaly. This representational proximity does not imply that impossible statements are intrinsically meaningless. These correlational observations from one small model offer an empirical footnote to an old philosophical distinction.

</details>


### [134] [The Embedder's Dilemma: LLMs Are Better, but at What Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12875)
*Adnan El Assadi,Niklas Muennighoff,Jinhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模受控对比实验（10个LLM×26个embedding模型×37个任务），发现LLM与embedding模型在整体性能上几乎持平（差距仅0.4分），但存在显著的任务分工差异：LLM在推理密集型检索上占优，embedding模型在分类上占优，两者在聚类/STS/对分类上相当。然而LLM的成本是其同等质量embedding模型的最高1431倍，开放LLM推理速度慢2.5-736倍。Pareto前沿由领先embedding模型和单一LLM（Gemini 3.1 Pro）占据，支持"分工使用"的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本嵌入管道（embedding模型）与直接使用大规模语言模型（LLM）进行文本表示一直是两种竞争范式，但此前缺乏系统的、成本感知的公平对比。作者旨在回答"是否应该用LLM替代文本嵌入管道"这一实际问题，通过控制变量和综合评估来提供可操作的决策依据。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖6个LLM家族共10个LLM、26个embedding模型（参数规模118M至14B）的大规模基准，在37个任务（分类、语义文本相似度、聚类、对分类、检索）上进行受控评估，同时纳入推理成本和延迟指标（每基准pass的美元成本、GPU上的token处理速度），并进行推理token预算的消融实验，基于考虑成本后的Pareto前沿分析。

Result: 两大范式在聚合性能上基本持平（最佳LLM Gemini 3.1 Pro得77.6分，最佳embedding模型77.2分）；LLM在推理密集型检索任务上领先，embedding模型在分类上领先，二者在聚类/STS/对分类上持平。但LLM成本最高为可比质量embedding模型的1431倍（每基准pass约154美元 vs 0.11美元），开放LLM推理速度慢2.5至736倍；推理token占LLM总推理成本的28%-81%，降低推理预算对大多数模型的检索质量无害甚至有益。

Conclusion: 建议采用分工策略：相似度检索、分类和聚类任务应使用embedding模型，而推理密集型的检索任务则保留给LLM。Pareto向前沿仅包含领先的embedding模型和一个LLM（Gemini 3.1 Pro），说明LLM作为通用嵌入替代方案在成本效率上不具普适优势。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Should you replace your text-embedding pipeline with a large language model? We answer this with a controlled, cost-aware comparison of ten LLMs across six families and 26 embedding models (118M to 14B parameters) on 37 tasks spanning classification, semantic textual similarity (STS), clustering, pair classification, and retrieval. In aggregate the two paradigms are effectively tied: the best LLM (Gemini 3.1 Pro, 77.6) and the best embedding model (77.2) differ by 0.4 points. Their strengths differ by task: LLMs lead on reasoning-heavy retrieval, embedding models lead on classification, and the two match on clustering, STS, and pair classification. Reaching that parity is expensive. An LLM costs up to 1,431x more than an embedding model of comparable quality (USD 154 vs. USD 0.11 per benchmark pass), and the open LLMs tested process tokens 2.5 to 736x more slowly on the same GPU. Reasoning tokens account for 28 to 81% of LLM inference cost; lower reasoning budgets preserve or improve retrieval quality for most models in our ablation. The Pareto frontier contains the leading embedding models and one LLM, Gemini 3.1 Pro. These results support a division of labour: use embedding models for similarity, classification, and clustering, and reserve LLMs for reasoning-intensive retrieval. Our code, datasets, and results are publicly available at https://github.com/embeddings-benchmark/embedders-dilemma.

</details>


### [135] [When Your Agent Opens the Chat App: Agent-Controlled Search over Raw Chat Logs Rivals Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12888)
*Ruizhe Li,Licheng Zhang,Benfeng Xu,Mingxuan Du,Zheren Fu,Weidong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReFind是一个不构建任何语义结构的智能体记忆检索系统，仅通过词法索引和智能体控制的迭代搜索循环，在对话记忆基准上超越了图/树结构记忆系统（HippoRAG 2等），表明精心设计的检索控制可以替代昂贵的记忆结构构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent记忆系统为了提升检索质量，普遍在提问前就将原始对话历史转换为摘要、嵌入、树或知识图谱等结构。作者质疑这些结构的收益究竟是来自结构本身，还是来自对原始历史的高质量检索控制——如果是后者，则可以省去昂贵的结构构建开销。

Method: ReFind不构建任何语义结构，保持对话存档原始不变，在轮次粒度上进行词法索引，并组合了通用迭代关键词搜索循环与四种基于实证的聊天原生控制：会话感知的排名融合、局部上下文扩展、时间收窄、以及跳过已检查的会话。独立的推理阶段基于收集到的证据回答问题。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench增量多轮设置下的约2,800个问题上，ReFind取得最高平均准确率58.2，超过最强图/树结构记忆系统HippoRAG 2（53.2），全部基线统一使用GPT-4o-mini骨干。在LongMemEval-S/M上分别达到93.2±3.3和89.3±6.0（GPT-5-mini）。消融实验分别验证了智能体控制、聊天原生控制和词法检索的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 对于聊天存档上的精确、基于证据的问题，归功于复杂记忆结构的大部分收益可以通过给智能体提供对未修改记录的可控搜索来获得，完全无需基于LLM的索引构建。

Abstract: Agent-memory systems increasingly buy retrieval quality with structure, transforming raw conversation histories into summaries, embeddings, trees, or knowledge graphs before any question is asked. We ask how much of that benefit comes from the structure itself, rather than from competent retrieval over the raw history. We present ReFind, an agent-controlled search interface that builds no semantic structure at all: it leaves the conversation archive unmodified, indexes it lexically at turn granularity, and combines a generic iterative keyword-search loop with four chat-native controls grounded in empirical refinding work: session-aware rank fusion, local context expansion, temporal narrowing, and skipping already-inspected sessions. A separate reasoning stage answers from the collected evidence. Across a broad suite of conversational-memory tasks (single- and multi-hop QA, event ordering, and fact consolidation), roughly 2,800 questions on precise-retrieval and fact-tracking capabilities evaluated under the incremental multi-turn setting of MemoryAgentBench, ReFind attains the highest mean accuracy (58.2) of any system compared, above the strongest graph- and tree-based memory systems (HippoRAG 2, 53.2), all under a GPT-4o-mini backbone matched to every reused baseline. Controlled comparisons to single-shot BM25, a matched generic-agentic BM25 control, component removals, and agentic dense/hybrid variants separately support the roles of agent control, chat-native controls, and lexical retrieval. On LongMemEval-S/M, the same interface reaches 93.2 +/- 3.3 and 89.3 +/- 6.0 with GPT-5-mini. The results indicate that for precise, evidence-grounded questions over chat archives, much of the benefit credited to elaborate memory structures is recoverable by giving an agent controllable search over the unmodified record, with no LLM-based index construction at all.

</details>


### [136] [BavGround: A Benchmark for Regional Cultural Grounding and Dialect Competence in Bavarian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12894)
*Jophin John,Michael Hoffmann,Jan Fillies,Michael A. Hedderich,Barbara Plank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍BavGround基准，用于评估大语言模型在巴伐利亚地区文化扎根和方言能力上的表现，涵盖英语、德语和巴伐利亚语三种语言，包含618个多平行实例。评估发现强多语言模型整体表现最佳，但在巴伐利亚语及本土来源知识上性能下降；研究还揭示评估协议（如答案字母评分、语义匹配等）对结论有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM的文化评估大多集中于高资源标准语言，忽视了地区文化和方言社区的多样性。为填补这一空白，需要构建覆盖地区文化和方言能力的评估基准。

Method: 构建BavGround基准，包含206道多项选择源题、覆盖八个文化领域，按英语/德语/巴伐利亚语三语对齐形成618个多平行实例。题目涵盖广泛可获取的文化知识和基于来源（新闻、历史文献、专业书籍）的地区知识。评估15个7B-10B开源指令微调模型及一个闭源模型，并比较五种评估协议（原始字母评分、打乱字母评分、选项文本似然、生成答案解析、语义匹配）。对GENBA-10B模型检查点进行探索性分析。

Result: 强多语言模型总体表现最好，但在巴伐利亚语题目和基于来源的地区知识问题上性能明显下降，反映模型在方言和本地化文化知识上的持续困难。不同评估协议产生不同的绝对分数和排名，尤其对地区适应模型影响显著。GENBA-10B的持续预训练对不同领域答案内容似然的提升不均衡，方言能力仍相对薄弱。

Conclusion: BavGround为LLM的文化表征提供本地化、协议感知的评估方法，弥补了地区文化和方言评估的空白，并强调评估协议设计对结论可靠性的关键作用。

Abstract: Cultural evaluation of large language models (LLMs) often focuses on high-resource standard languages, leaving regional culture and dialect communities underrepresented. We introduce BavGround, a benchmark for evaluating Bavarian regional cultural grounding and dialect competence across English, German and Bavarian. BavGround contains 206 multiple-choice source questions across eight cultural domains per language, yielding 618 multi-parallel instances, with items covering both broadly accessible cultural knowledge and source-grounded regional knowledge from journalism, historical sources, and specialist literature. We evaluate fifteen 7B-10B open-weight instruction-tuned models and one closed-model reference. Strong multilingual models perform best overall, but performance drops on Bavarian items and source-grounded questions, indicating persistent difficulty with dialectal and localized cultural knowledge. We further show that conclusions depend strongly on evaluation protocol: raw answer-letter scoring, shuffled-letter scoring, option-text likelihood, generated-answer parsing, and semantic matching can produce different absolute scores and rankings, especially for regionally adapted models. Finally, an exploratory analysis of GENBA-10B checkpoints suggests that continued pretraining improves answer-content likelihood unevenly across domains, while dialect competence remains comparatively weak. BavGround supports localized, protocol-aware evaluation of cultural representation in LLMs.

</details>


### [137] [Prompts in the Wild: A Large Analyzed Collection of Transactional Prompts in Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12905)
*Victoria Basmov,Yoav Goldberg,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为生成式大语言模型（LLMs）的行为由提示词（prompts）直接塑造，而提示词本身是值得研究的语言学对象。作者从GitHub收集了57.5K个独特的"事务性提示词"（可复现的自然语言指令，集成于软件中），构建了结构化本体论来捕捉提示词的属性及其形式和语义组件，将非结构化原始文本转化为结构化的语言学对象。分析揭示了不同语言、领域、任务和模态之间显著的使用模式多样性，呈典型的Zipf-like分布。通过对所有字段进行全面的错误分析验证了标注可靠性，并发布了数据集和浏览工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代生成式LLMs的行为直接由提示词塑造，提示词是描述期望输出和模型行为的非结构化文本。然而，提示词作为语言学对象本身尚未得到充分研究。作者主张提示词值得在其自身权利下进行系统性调查，并需要对大规模提示词进行经验性、定量化的研究。

Method: 作者从GitHub收集了57.5K个独特的事务性提示词样本（指集成到软件中的可复现自然语言指令）。他们引入了一个结构化本体论，用于捕捉提示词的属性及其形式（formal）和语义（semantic）组件。基于该本体论，他们将提示词从非结构化原始文本转化为结构化的语言学对象，并进行定量分析。

Result: 分析揭示了不同语言、领域、任务和模态之间显著的使用模式多样性，呈现典型的Zipf-like分布——某些提示词明显占主导地位，而更多样化的其他提示词出现在长尾中。此外，作者对本体论标注进行了全面的跨字段错误分析，提供了详细的注释质量评估。

Conclusion: 提示词作为语言学对象可以通过结构化本体论进行系统研究。作者发布了包含57.5K样本的数据集以及浏览和探索界面，为未来提示词的语言学研究提供了基础资源和方法论框架。

Abstract: The behavior of contemporary generative Large Language Models (LLMs) is directly shaped by prompts, unstructured texts that describe the desired output and model behavior. In this paper we argue that prompts are linguistic objects that merit investigation in their own right. To this end, we collect 57.5K unique samples of prompts from GitHub. Specifically, we focus on transactional prompts: reproducible natural language instructions that are integrated into software. To enable the empirical, quantitative study of prompts, we introduce a structured ontology, capturing the properties of prompts as well as their formal and semantic components. Based on this ontology, we transform prompts from unstructured raw texts into richly structured linguistic objects. Analysis of these structured data reveals significant diversity of usage patterns across languages, domains, tasks, and modalities, in a typical Zipf-like distribution where some clearly prevail and others, more diverse, appear in the long tail. To validate the reliability of the ontology-based annotation of the prompts, we perform a comprehensive error analysis across all fields, providing a detailed assessment of annotation quality. We release the dataset together with a browsing and exploration interface (https://github.com/OnlpLab/transactionalPromptsCollection ).

</details>


### [138] [Decoupled Contrastive Decoding via Expert-Aligned Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12913)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuanfu Wang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦对比解码（DCD），通过将轻量级草稿模型与专家对齐，仅在验证阶段使用业余模型，在保持标准对比解码输出分布的同时实现1.65-1.95倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比解码（CD）虽能提升生成质量，但其生成过程需要额外运行业余模型，导致解码成本高昂。即使采用投机解码加速，也面临一个关键问题：对比信号应塑造草稿模型还是仅保留在验证阶段？需要系统性探究该问题。

Method: 在轻量级特征级草稿模型框架下进行研究，设计了两个受控诊断实验（匹配Cross-alpha训练和近似对偶草稿分解），并据此提出解耦对比解码（DCD）：使用专家对齐的轻量级草稿器进行草稿生成，业余模型仅用于不变的CD验证阶段，标准投机验证可保持原始vanilla-CD输出分布。

Result: 在主要8B配置下，基于EAGLE3的DCD相比vanilla CD平均获得1.65至1.95倍的贪婪加速；相对于业余耦合的提案路径，MMLU提案路径延迟降低约5至12倍。

Conclusion: 对比感知的草稿生成并不一致地优于专家对齐的草稿生成，原因在于对比修正通常弱于草稿误差，且重建可能放大该误差。解耦的设计（草稿阶段用专家对齐模型、验证阶段用业余模型）既能保持分布一致性又能获得显著加速。

Abstract: Contrastive Decoding (CD) improves generation quality, but its amateur-model pass makes decoding expensive. Accelerating CD with speculative decoding raises a proposal-alignment question: should the contrastive signal shape the drafter, or should it remain only in verification? We study this question in the lightweight feature-level drafter regime. Two controlled diagnostics, matched Cross-alpha training and an Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition, give the same diagnosis: contrastive-aware drafting does not consistently improve over expert-aligned drafting because the contrastive correction is usually weaker than drafter error, and reconstruction can amplify that error. We introduce Decoupled Contrastive Decoding (DCD), which drafts with an expert-aligned lightweight proposer and applies the amateur only in unchanged CD verification. Standard speculative verification preserves the vanilla-CD output distribution. Across the main 8B settings, EAGLE3-based DCD achieves average greedy speedups of 1.65 to 1.95x over vanilla CD and reduces MMLU proposal-path latency by about 5 to 12x relative to amateur-coupled proposal paths.

</details>


### [139] [Unifying Depth and Width Pruning for LLMs via Binary Knapsack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12953)
*Palaash Goel,Ayan Sengupta,Akshay Nambi,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNIPER是一个两阶段结构化剪枝框架，通过背包优化与细粒度剪枝相结合，实现对大型语言模型的精确压缩。相比现有剪枝方法在目标压缩比上最高偏差33%，SNIPER达到了近乎精确的符合度（CRAFT分数0.98），在18个任务、4种架构上取得平均排名1.25的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法依赖贪心启发式算法，容易产生短视（myopic）的决策，且难以精确达到目标压缩预算。这一问题在大语言模型压缩场景中尤为突出，需要更优的参数分配策略与预算符合性保障。

Method: SNIPER包含两个阶段：(1) 粗粒度阶段：在粗粒度组件上求解背包优化（knapsack optimization），基于固定重要性估计获得条件最优的参数分配；(2) 细粒度阶段：执行细粒度剪枝以满足严格的预算约束。同时提出Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT)指标来量化预算符合度。

Result: 现有剪枝器偏离目标压缩比最高达33%，而SNIPER的CRAFT分数达到0.98，几乎精确符合预算。在4种架构、18个任务（覆盖5个领域）上，SNIPER在平均性能保持率和任务级稳定性上均优于6个SOTA剪枝器，平均排名1.25。

Conclusion: SNIPER通过两阶段优化框架有效解决了现有剪枝方法的短视决策和预算偏离问题，展现出跨架构的强泛化能力和高可靠性，是结构化剪枝领域的一项重要进展。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for compressing large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods rely heavily on greedy heuristics that produce myopic decisions, and often fail to precisely meet target compression budgets. We present SNIPER, a two-stage structured pruning framework that solves a knapsack optimization over coarse-granularity components to yield conditionally optimal parameter allocations with respect to fixed importance estimates, followed by a fine-grained pruning stage to meet strict budget constraints. We introduce the Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT) to quantify budget fidelity, showing that while existing pruners deviate from target compression ratios by up to 33%, SNIPER achieves near-exact adherence with a CRAFT score of 0.98. Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [140] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2 提出了一种高效的长期记忆框架，通过语义分段级整合（而非逐轮整合）来降低 LLM 记忆构建成本，同时保持细粒度上下文证据，在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上达到 SOTA 性能，并将构建 token 开销降低 86.0% 和 75.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体长期记忆系统依赖 eager consolidation，即每次交互后调用 LLM 提取、总结或更新记忆，导致对话增长时记忆构建成本显著上升。粗粒度摘要可降低成本但丢弃细粒度证据，而更大检索上下文或多跳推理将开销转移到查询阶段。因此需要一种在记忆构建成本与证据保留之间取得更好平衡的记忆框架。

Method: LycheeMemory V2 将逐轮级整合替换为语义分段级整合。具体做法是：1) 将多次交互批处理为语义段，对每个完成的段编码为上下文无关的类型化记忆记录；2) 通过语义边界检测保留连贯的事件级和时间级证据，相比固定窗口批处理更优；3) 使用轻量级结构化索引组织记忆记录，用于查询规划的证据检索。

Result: 使用 GPT-4.1-Mini 评估，LycheeMemory 在 LoCoMo 上达到 89.22% 准确率，在 LongMemEval-S 上达到 92.20%，均为 SOTA。相比 A-Mem，在 LoCoMo 上构建 token 减少 86.0%，在 LongMemEval-S 上减少 75.9%，且未增加查询阶段 token 用量。

Conclusion: 长期智能体记忆的准确率-成本权衡不仅取决于保留什么信息，还取决于信息整合的粒度。语义分段级整合能够在降低记忆构建开销的同时保留细粒度上下文证据，为高效长期记忆系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [141] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridRAG-BN，一个针对孟加拉语知识库问答（KBQA）的检索增强框架。该框架整合了BM25和BGE-M3的混合检索、基于GGUF版本的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct进行答案生成，并通过LoRA微调的模型进行答案验证与精炼，同时引入后处理阶段处理未解决案例。在公开和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，荣获竞赛第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的KBQA系统开发面临三大挑战：检索相关研究匮乏、语言资源稀缺、生成响应难以在外部知识中进行接地（grounding）。现有KBQA系统主要面向高资源语言，缺乏专门针对孟加拉语的可靠检索增强方案。

Method: 提出HybridRAG-BN框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) 混合检索：结合BM25与传统检索和BGE-M3密集检索；(2) 答案生成：使用GGUF量化的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct模型生成答案；(3) 验证精炼：通过LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct模型对生成答案进行验证和精炼；(4) 后处理：针对未解决的案例采用fallback答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索来增强鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果显示，该框架在公开排行榜上取得0.71654的token级F1分数，在私有排行榜上取得0.72912的token级F1分数，在竞赛中荣获第一名。

Conclusion: HybridRAG-BN通过混合检索策略、LoRA微调验证模块及多级后处理机制，有效解决了孟加拉语KBQA中检索与知识接地的问题，验证了检索增强框架在低资源语言场景下的可行性，并达到了竞赛最优性能。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [142] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviReform是一种多跳检索方法，通过将检索请求的修正与图证据聚合分离，利用已检索到的源段落生成残差查询，补充原始问题未充分指明的证据需求，在多个多跳问答数据集上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能共同提供充分证据的段落，但初始段落往往解析了问题中隐含的实体或关系，仅在检索开始后才能更容易地描述缺失证据。传统图检索的检索信号通常来自原始问题，导致补充证据必须通过存储的关系到达，即使已观察到的段落提供了更直接的语义线索。

Method: EviReform将修正检索请求与在图中的证据聚合分开处理。已检索到的源段落为未解决的信息需求构建残差查询。原始检索信号与残差检索信号分别归一化后合并，并在共享实体的命题之间传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，EviReform相比最强基线在Recall@5上最高提升5.59个百分点，在F1上最高提升4.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，观察到的证据可以引导图检索朝向原始问题未充分指定的支撑链部分，证明了以证据驱动的残差查询策略在多跳检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [143] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve是一个自参考的检索增强生成(RAG)中毒攻击检测框架，包含查询端(RSQ)和语料端(RSG)两个模块，无需可信参考语料或中毒标签即可在语料引入和查询时刻检测注入文档，联合部署可将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成将外部语料作为推理证据，攻击者可通过注入文档来传播其选定的主张。现有检测方法依赖可信参考、特定攻击痕迹或对语料拓扑敏感的全局阈值，缺乏一个无需外部参考的通用检测框架。

Method: RAGSieve提出自参考检测框架，从被检测系统自身构建参考。包含两个模块：(1) RAGSieve-Query (RSQ)执行查询局部对比，对检索结果前5名候选与同次检索第6-20名进行对比评分，检测答案锚点集中和载体转换现象；(2) RAGSieve-Graph (RSG)执行语料局部对比，将每个文档的语义相似但词法不同的邻居与其局部基线比较，在查询到达前检测协调性密度异常。

Result: 在三个QA数据集和六种中毒构造下，RSQ在5%干净文档移除时达到95.2% AUROC并检测82.2%的中毒，超过GMTP的81.1%/52.5%；RSG达到93.3%/79.8%，超过CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%。联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%，同时在无中毒检索上保持41.3%的F1分数。

Conclusion: RAGSieve在语料摄入和查询时均提供实用保护，无需中毒标签或可信语料，可有效检测RAG系统中的文档注入攻击，代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [144] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA，一种结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B的简单架构，在自动口语评估（ASA）中取得最先进性能，同时在语音传递与内容之间提供更可解释的分离，并在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上以约一半的推理参数实现RMSE 0.358。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动口语评估研究虽然越来越多地采用多模态语音大语言模型，但对声学信息和内容信息如何贡献于预测、以及所产生性能的稳定性缺乏充分分析。

Method: 提出CASA架构，由Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B组成，采用三个手工设计的流利度特征，实现语音传递与内容之间的可解释分离；通过消融实验和重复运行分析声学与内容信息的个体及互补贡献，并利用大语言模型推理实现免训练的内容验证。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上，CASA取得0.358的RMSE，优于此前最佳结果，且推理参数约为现有方法的一半。

Conclusion: CASA证明了一个更简单、更可解释的架构能够在ASA中达到最先进性能，同时该通用架构无需结构改动即可适配其他ASA语料库；声学与内容信息的互补性以及性能变异性分析为理解多模态ASA模型提供了深入见解。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [145] [LigBench: A Unified and Human-Aligned Benchmark for LLM-based Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13136)
*Chenrun Wang,Mingxuan Zhu,Tiancheng Huang,Wenjie Li,Yujie Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Zhiying Zou,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LigBench，一个用于自动化、细粒度、可靠地评估AI研究思路的基准，以及PAIR-IQ数据集用于训练成对思路判断模型，实现与专家判断更好对齐的客观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM研究思路生成方法的评估实践碎片化、缺乏客观标准，通常依赖直接的LLM打分，难以在生成思路的一致分布上提供统一可靠的评估。

Method: 提出LigBench自动化评估基准，支持跨不同生成分布的细粒度可靠评估；同时引入PAIR-IQ数据集，用于训练成对思路判断模型并作为辅助参考支持更客观的比较评估。

Result: 实验表明LigBench实现了稳定且可解释的评估，显著提升了与专家判断的一致性；在PAIR-IQ上训练的模型展现出更高的排序准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 建立了可扩展且客观的研究思路评估的原则性标准，为解决LLM思路生成评估缺乏统一标准的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), research idea generation has attracted increasing attention. Existing approaches enable LLMs to retrieve relevant literature and propose novel ideas for research areas. However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [146] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Syfer框架，一种适用于多语言多跳问答的"合成-折叠"方法，通过延迟翻译、格式受限的子问题图分解以及质量检查，在保持竞争性准确率的同时，在性能和计算成本之间取得了良好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言检索增强生成（mRAG）方法存在两个主要局限：一是"一刀切"的翻译对齐会丢弃目标语言独有的文化和语言原生信息、引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；二是贪婪的分解与聚合会产生冗余子问题，在逐步推理中累积错误，最终聚合进一步放大这些错误。

Method: Syfer框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 格式受限的分解器在原始语言中生成子问题图；(2) 对分解质量进行检查；(3) 当检查通过时，子问题在目标语言下按"先检索后回答"策略顺序执行；当检查失败时，才激活英文翻译路径，并进行双语子问题图对齐。

Result: 在多种语言上的实验表明，Syfer在达到具有竞争力的准确率的同时，在性能和计算成本之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译而非默认应用翻译是一种有效的策略，可以在mRAG中保留语言原生信息、减少噪声和成本，同时通过质量检查和条件性翻译路径来保证回答的准确性。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [147] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECBench情感陪伴基准，引入成人依恋理论（ECR-R量表）评估32个LLM在亲密关系场景中的依恋焦虑/回避倾向，涵盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交引导四种场景，通过11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法检验模型行为，并探究这些倾向能否通过提示词进行塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被应用于情感陪伴场景，但现有评估主要刻画通用人格特质，缺乏对模型在亲密关系和情感敏感场景下行为的深入洞察，需要引入心理学理论框架来系统评估模型的情感陪伴能力。

Method: 将成人依恋理论引入LLM评估，使用ECR-R量表表征依恋焦虑和回避维度；构建ECBench基准，覆盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决和社交引导四个场景，涵盖友谊与浪漫关系；采用11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法，对32个LLM进行依恋倾向评估，并选取代表性模型研究依恋倾向在多轮交互中的表现及可通过提示词定制的程度。

Result: 评估了32个LLM的依恋倾向，识别出代表性模型，揭示了依恋倾向在情境化多轮交互中的具体表现，并验证了通过提示词塑造模型依恋倾向的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究为理解和选择适合情感陪伴的LLM提供了心理学视角的理论框架和实用评估工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue.
