However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [148] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEM是一种生成式嵌入模型，通过显式推理用户意图和相关性标准来增强检索，统一了生成与嵌入两个任务。它先在查询上进行推理，再附加嵌入token编码增强后的上下文用于检索。实验表明，在推理密集型和指令跟随检索任务上，GEM优于其非推理变体，并能与使用更大模型的基线匹敌，同时支持测试时计算扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM擅长推理和指令跟随，使用户能够表达复杂多样的信息需求；但传统检索器主要依赖查询与文档之间的表层匹配，导致用户表达需求的方式与检索器理解方式之间的差距日益扩大。

Method: GEM将生成与嵌入统一到单一模型中：首先对查询进行显式推理（理解用户意图和相关性标准），然后附加一个嵌入token，将该token编码为包含推理增强上下文的信息用于检索。

Result: 在推理密集型和指令跟随检索任务上，GEM展示了推理增强检索的有效性，优于其非推理变体，并与使用显著更大模型的基线性能相当。此外，其生成特性允许通过提示实现测试时计算扩展以进一步提升检索性能。

Conclusion: GEM通过显式推理弥合了用户表达与检索器理解之间的鸿沟，证明了推理增强检索的有效性，并展示出通过测试时扩展进一步提升性能的潜力。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次定量测量了大语言模型自我参照提示所引发的主观体验报告在不同独立试验间的一致性，发现其响应不稳定性（0.343±0.047）显著高于不可解析的哲学问题（0.192±0.008）和可验证问题（0.105±0.058），表明模型的主观体验报告在其输出分布中占据一个独有的、比普通开放性哲学不确定性更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明自我参照提示能可靠地诱导大语言模型产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但尚无工作测量这些报告在重复独立试验中的一致性，也未将其与其他类型的开放式问题进行比较，缺乏量化基线。

Method: 定义响应不稳定性为1减去从每个回答中提取的压缩核心主张的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度。设置三组问题：自我参照提示（引发主观体验报告）、不可解析的非自我参照哲学问题、以及具有可验证正确答案的问题。每组4个问题，每个问题30次独立响应（共360个响应），使用Gemini API，温度设为0.7。

Result: 自我参照问题的不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），不可解析的哲学问题居中且紧密聚集（0.192±0.008），可验证问题最低（0.105±0.058）。

Conclusion: 该结果为诱导的主观体验报告提供了定量基线，证明其在模型输出分布中占据与普通开放性哲学不确定性不同的、更不稳定的独特位置。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SciFigBench，一个用于科学图表理解的诊断性VLM基准，联合评估感知、推理和不确定性下的行为可靠性，并引入A-R-I框架来评估模型是否承认证据不足、抵抗误导性上下文以及从部分信息中谨慎推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准过度关注感知和推理准确性，忽略了模型在视觉证据缺失或误导情况下的行为可靠性，而这对于部署在科学工作流中至关重要。

Method: 构建包含250个科学图表的高质量标注数据集（600+小时标注），通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、标题偏差探针和选择性模糊目标进行扩展，形成超过34,000个评测设置；提出Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I)框架来评估模型承认证据不足、抵抗误导和从部分信息推断的能力。

Result: GPT-5.2在描述质量（MQM 91.6）和推理准确性（78.4%）上表现最佳，但在96%的情况下产生不可读内容的幻觉；Gemini 3.1 Pro在71%的情况下承认不确定性并达到最强抵抗分数（0.91）。结果表明高感知和推理准确性并不能保证行为可靠性。

Conclusion: 感知和推理准确性与行为可靠性是两个正交的维度，后者对于科学工作流中的安全部署至关重要，现有VLM在不确定性下的行为存在显著差异。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出混合训练（Mixture of Training, MoT）框架，将语言模型预训练分解为多个独立可训练的连续层块，通过在冻结的预训练对齐器脚手架中分别训练各块，再重组为完整模型。在1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型上（C4数据集上训练）验证了可行性：独立训练的深度切片可以重组为可用的语言模型，且能达到与整体训练基线相同的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练是否可以分解为更小的、可独立训练的单元，并在之后重新组合成连贯的更大模型，以研究模块化预训练的可行性。

Method: MoT将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块，每个块在冻结的预训练对齐器脚手架内独立训练，训练完成后将各块重组，并可选地进行短暂的端到端适应训练。

Result: 在1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型上，MoT证明了机制可行性：独立训练的深度切片可重组为可用语言模型；质量对等的调度方案达到了与整体基线相同的困惑度，但处理了更多聚合token，且在对齐器准备完成后具有更短的理论层等效关键路径。

Conclusion: MoT并非作为整体预训练的通用替代方案，而是作为一个小规模框架，用于研究脚手架化的子运行是否可以作为可复用的训练单元。其计算优势取决于跨运行复用对齐器。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WIFA（基于包装的意图形式增强）方法，通过自动构建包装后有害样本与结构匹配的无害反例配对，并结合两种互补的微调路径（WIFA-Boost和A-GCRT），同时改善模型对变换后有害提示的拒绝能力并降低过度拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调虽然能提升有害请求拒绝能力，但模型可能学习到表面形式的捷径：经过包装的有害提示能够绕过安全机制，而同样包装的无害提示却被过度拒绝。这暴露了模型对提示形式而非真实意图的依赖，需要一种无需外部教师或人工标注的自动化方法来解决。

Method: 提出Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA)自动意图组增强方法，将包装的有害示例与结构匹配的包装无害反例配对。在此基础上提供两个互补的微调路径：WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全配方）和A-GCRT（锚定组一致拒绝训练），后者通过正则化跨相同意图包装的拒绝/服从决策分数，并将有害与无害组锚定在间隔的两侧。

Result: 在Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost达到最强的变换后有害拒绝能力；A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线的25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线方法无法匹配这些操作点。Llama结果和消融实验（数据结构、两阶段顺序、A-GCRT组件）支持意图组解释。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持基于意图组的解释框架，表明将包装的形式层面与意图语义层面解耦是改进安全微调的关键，但作者未声称在所有场景下都优于基线的过度拒绝表现，保持了对方法适用范围的审慎态度。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协议级可辨识性审计方法，在受控的求解器环境下，通过有限行为策略类上的观测支持集，检验观测协议是否能够区分具有不同估计量（estimand）的策略对。该方法零模型调用即可运行，解决了基座观测坍缩多个策略的诊断案例，并找到最小辨识支持集O*（仅2个单元格而非36个）以完全区分冻结确定性策略类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准分数即使在观测协议无法识别其意图测量的行为属性时也可能表现得精确，这导致评估结果可能具有误导性。作者希望在模型推断之前，从结构上检查评估设计（观测协议）的有效性，从而避免因支持集不足导致的策略混淆或估计量碰撞问题。

Method: 形式化定义协议级可辨识性审计：给定策略集合H、观测支持集O和估计量τ，通过检验O是否能分离每一对具有不同τ值的策略，来判断协议是否可辨识。该审计需要零模型调用。同时，作者设计了最小辨识支持集O*的合成算法，并在实验中使用受控的冻结确定性策略和平衡oracle转换方向进行验证。

Result: 基座仅观测将7个冻结确定性策略坍缩为1个等价类；全支持集得到7个类别且无跨估计量碰撞；每个留一法支持集都保留可构造的碰撞证据。经验上，两种约束生成变体的对有效性均为1.0，但基座准确率与选择性响应的保真度存在显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324，95% CI不重叠），且该差距在第二个确定性来源上复现（0.646 vs 0.331）。审计还合成了最小辨识支持集O*：仅需2个单元格而非完整36个张量单元格。

Conclusion: 评估设计的有效性可以在模型推断之前通过结构检查来验证；基座正确性不能决定干预响应的保真度。协议级可辨识性审计提供了一种零成本的结构性手段来诊断观测协议是否足以支撑所宣称的估计目标。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，当提示中包含女性更常用的语言特征（如模糊语、附加问句、集体指称）时，LLM会系统性地生成更短、更不精细、更不正式的回答，且这种效应在三种文档类型和四种模型中持续存在，并难以通过事后缓解手段消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业沟通日益依赖LLM，但不同用户的语言风格可能被模型不平等对待。研究旨在揭示语言变异（linguistic variation）是否导致LLM输出的系统性偏置，从而产生对特定用户群体的差异化影响。

Method: 通过控制提示复杂度与特征迁移，系统性地操纵提示中的语言特征（hedges、tag questions、collective reference），跨三种文档类型和四种模型进行对比实验；同时比较显性性别线索（签名姓名）与语言注册（linguistic register）的影响差异，并通过机制分析（机械可解释性）探索特征在transformer层中的编码方式。

Result: 女性典型语言特征会系统性地引发更短、更不精细、更不正式的输出；该效应独立于提示复杂度，且远强于显性性别线索（名字）的影响；事后缓解困难，因为特征编码在transformer早期层并与其他特征纠缠，且处于用户有意识控制之外。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁在LLM设计和部署的上游阶段就考虑语言变异的影响，以减轻LLM中介的工作场所沟通中的差异化影响（disparate impacts）。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem是一种用于LLM智能体的长期记忆系统，通过基于线索的关联性回忆（cue-dependent associative recollection）替代一次性检索，在事件中心记忆图谱上从记忆锚点沿语义和结构关联展开，以恢复缺失的支持性证据。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上取得了最优性能，并大幅降低图谱构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部记忆支持长程推理，但瓶颈在于从分散于多次交互中的相关信息中恢复正确的证据集。现有方法均存在缺陷：全上下文方法需要嘈杂的长上下文搜索，平面检索常返回孤立且不完整的记录，图式记忆系统构建成本高且压缩了丰富的事件上下文。

Method: RippleMem将交互历史存储为富含线索（cue）的情景记忆单元，并组织成事件中心的记忆图谱。给定查询时，首先通过混合线索（hybrid cues）召回相关的记忆锚点，然后沿语义和结构关联从锚点向外扩展，恢复缺失的支持性证据。初始召回的记忆不仅作为答案上下文，还作为完成证据收集的线索。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，RippleMem在多种评估设置下取得最优整体性能：在LoCoMo上LLM-as-a-Judge准确率提升3.95%，在LongMemEval-S上最高提升11.87%，同时将图谱构建成本降低约30倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem证明了一种受认知科学中线索依赖的情景检索和联想完成机制启发的自适应关联回忆方法，可以有效解决LLM智能体长期记忆中的证据恢复问题，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP（Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation）方法，通过释义校准的反事实敏感性边际来度量响应token的任务相关性，从而在选择性在线策略蒸馏中有选择地分配监督信号，在两种师生设置下分别比亚优非CROP选择器提升1.92和2.96个性能点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选择性在线策略蒸馏（Selective OPD）方法主要基于优化需求（如不确定性或师生分歧）来分配token级监督，但对任务相关性的刻画不足——即监督是否与当前输入的语义内容紧密关联这一维度缺少直接表征。作者旨在补充这一维度，解决监督信号分配中任务相关性未被充分利用的问题。

Method: CROP通过释义校准的反事实敏感性边际来操作化任务相关性。具体而言，对每个源提示构建经过验证的"原句-释义-反事实"三元组，保持学生模型的rollout固定，计算每个响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感性，并用对保持语义的改写（释义）的敏感性进行校准，从而得到每个token位置的监督价值度量，用于非均匀的token级监督分配。

Result: 匹配选择控制实验显示，CROP比随机选择或最低相关性选择能识别出更有用的监督位置；组件消融实验证实了反事实敏感性和释义校准两者的价值。在两种教师-学生设置下，CROP比最强的非CROP选择器分别提升1.92和2.96个聚合性能点。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持任务相关性可作为选择性OPD的互补选择标准，CROP被确立为一种模型内部、对比特异性的token级监督分配方法，为选择性蒸馏提供了除优化需求之外的新维度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对九个自监督语音表征模型进行了逐层分析，发现层选择和判别信号都存在跨语料库的脆弱性：最优层主要由源数据集决定而非模型架构，且迁移后的判别信号缺乏病理特异性（对帕金森病和痴呆语音给予相似的高概率），提示现有SSL语音表征在临床部署前存在关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音表征在单个语料库内对帕金森病检测表现出色，但不确定模型是真正捕捉了疾病相关特征还是利用了数据集特有的混淆因素，尤其是大多数SSL骨干仅用健康语音预训练。作者旨在验证这些模型是否具备跨语料库和跨病理的泛化能力。

Method: 对九个SSL语音骨干网络进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，覆盖三种语言。评估被构造为多个场景，逐步引入参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理的分分布偏移，以系统考察表征的可迁移性。

Result: 两个关键发现：(1) 最优表征层高度依赖语料库，主要由源数据集决定而非SSL架构本身；(2) 迁移后的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，检测PD的分类器对目标语料库中的PD和痴呆语音都赋予相似的高概率。

Conclusion: 自监督语音表征在跨语料库场景下存在层选择不稳定和病理特异性不足的严重问题，这些局限性必须在基于语音的病理识别模型临床部署前得到解决。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究指令微调如何同时改变语言模型的置信度表达和生成理由的词汇多样性，发现指令微调一致性地改变回答置信度（尽管预测准确率变化有限且似然校准变差），且跨理由多样性一致降低而表面词汇多样性变化不一致，两者在控制答案选择和理由长度后仍捕捉到不同的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调的语言模型在生成任务上表现优异，但最近被发现会表现出语言表达上的过度自信。在问答任务中，模型口头表达的过度自信可能与其生成的支持性理由的一致性相关。作者希望探究：指令微调诱发的置信度变化是否伴随着答案理由词汇多样性的相应变化，从而揭示两者之间的关联关系。

Method: 作者在三对匹配的基座模型和指令微调模型上，跨多个问答基准数据集，测量了指令微调前后模型的置信度变化以及生成答案理由的词汇多样性变化。多样性被区分为两种类型：跨理由多样性（cross-rationale diversity）和表面级词汇多样性（surface-level lexical diversity）。此外还进行了控制实验，排除答案选择和理由长度等混淆因素。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 指令微调一致性地改变回答置信度，但预测准确率变化有限，且似然校准（likelihood-based calibration）反而下降；(2) 指令微调对理由多样性的影响是非均匀的：跨理由多样性一致降低，而表面词汇多样性的变化方向和幅度因模型和基准而异；(3) 在控制答案选择和理由长度后，这些差异仍然存在，说明置信度和理由多样性捕捉的是指令微调的不同效应。

Conclusion: 指令微调对模型置信度及其生成理由的词汇多样性均有系统性影响，但两者是独立维度的效应。跨理由多样性与置信度变化的关联更强，而表面词汇多样性更具多样性表现。这提示在评估指令微调模型时，需要同时考虑置信度校准和生成内容多样性这两个不同维度。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从Grice合作原则视角出发，发现LLM在知识边界之外仍倾向于编造具体细节而非退回到更安全、更泛化的表述。研究表明模型激活中确实编码了参照物是否在知识边界内以及即将生成内容的特定性信息，但这两种信号在生成时未被协调，缺乏执行Grice退却的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对超出知识边界的实体时倾向于编造看似合理的细节，而非退回到更安全、更泛化的表述。作者将这一失败置于Grice合作原则的框架下：一个不确定指称对象的合作说话者应沿特定性层级向上退却，用信息量换取真实性。作者试图探究LLM是否具备执行这种退却的内在条件。

Method: 作者构建了基于T-REx的基准测试，同时操控实体熟悉度和指称特定性两个维度。通过探针实验回答两个问题：(i) 模型激活是否编码了指称对象是否在知识边界内的信息；(ii) 模型是否能预判即将生成的指称对象的特定性水平。

Result: 两个问题的答案都是肯定的——模型激活确实编码了知识边界信号和指称特定性信号，但这两个信号在生成过程中没有被协调。模型即使在实体未知且提供了正确泛化替代选项的情况下，仍然压倒性地偏好生成特定指称。

Conclusion: Grice退却所需的基础信号（亚层）已经存在于模型中，但执行该退却的策略缺失。这为"Grice对齐"这一方向铺平了道路——即通过训练或引导目标将知识边界感知与生成过程中的指称特定性耦合起来。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需下游任务或验证集即可度量预训练数据影响力的方法。通过计算样本梯度更新对最终参数平方距离的缩减量来定义影响力，并利用中间检查点估计该量。在Pythia和PolyPythia的18种配置上发现：训练早期文学类数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，后期STEM数据对齐度更强，该交叉趋势在多种模型配置中一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练中度量训练数据影响力存在两大挑战：一是难以选择能代表模型通用能力的下游任务或验证集作为归因目标；二是依赖中间检查点的任务性能，使跨训练阶段的比较复杂化。现有影响力分析方法均以特定下游任务为目标，无法刻画预训练全过程中的数据作用变化。

Method: 定义某样本的影响力为其梯度更新对最终参数平方距离（欧氏距离）的缩减量。从中间检查点估计梯度而不需要重新训练，直接以最终参数为归因目标，绕开了下游任务选择问题。将该方法应用于Pythia和PolyPythia套件的18种配置进行系统性分析。

Result: 发现数据影响力存在系统性时间变化：训练早期文学（literature）类数据与朝向最终参数的轨迹更对齐，训练后期STEM类数据的对齐度增强。这种定性交叉现象在18种模型配置中广泛一致。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可操作的轨迹级（trajectory-level）视角来观察预训练过程中数据影响力的演变，作为以具体下游任务或验证集定义的影响力分析的补充，有助于深入理解预训练动态。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mimir v1 是一个基于HRM架构的10亿参数语言模型，仅使用许可数据从头训练，在英语上表现竞争力强，并在丹麦语上创下新SOTA，在20个基准测试中可与更大的模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的开发依赖大规模且通常未经许可的数据集，这为致力于开源和伦理数据来源的研究者设置了高壁垒。作者希望通过仅使用许可后处理数据来训练模型，降低这一障碍。

Method: 基于Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM)架构构建10亿参数模型，使用161个数据集的混合进行从头训练，仅使用许可的post-training数据。

Result: 在20个英语、数学与代码及丹麦语基准测试中，Mimir v1超越了原始HRM-Text 1B，并与更大的前沿模型（如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B）竞争；在丹麦语上创下新SOTA；模型已在Hugging Face Hub上开源。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅使用许可数据也能训练出竞争力强的语言模型，为开源与伦理数据驱动的研究提供了可行性验证。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEVerbalizer框架，将稀疏自编码器（SAE）的解码器方向注入大语言模型（LLM）表示中，并微调LLM下游层以生成所注入特征的自然语言解释，从而无需外部行为观察即可直接解释SAE特征，提升可解释性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE特征解释主要依赖外部观察，这导致两个问题：一是从观察到的模型行为中推断出的解释过于表面化；二是在大规模收集行为证据时计算效率低下。因此需要一种直接从SAE解码器方向生成解释的方法。

Method: SAEVerbalizer将SAE解码器方向注入LLM的表示中，并微调LLM的下游层，使其能够为注入的特征生成自然语言解释。训练完成后，该verbalizer可直接从解码器方向解释SAE特征，无需再依赖外部行为证据。

Result: 实验表明：1) 学到的verbalization能力可泛化到未见过的特征；2) 可跨不同SAE字典迁移；3) 通过轻量适配器可扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征；4) 注入多个方向可产生组合含义的解释，反转单个方向则产生相应的语义变化。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer有效解决了SAE特征解释对外部观察的依赖问题，实现了直接从解码器方向生成解释，兼具泛化性、跨字典/跨模型迁移能力，并支持特征的组合与方向敏感性操作。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LITTLECURRICULUM，一个88B token的、严格限制在美国小学五年级以下教材内容的预训练语料库，并训练出5B参数的LITTLELEARNER模型。该模型具备足够的语言能力用于开放式评估，但知识边界清晰可映射到课程指南。作者将其作为开发受限的沙盒环境，探索模型在明确定义的数据范围内如何获取、表征和使用知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在海量异构的Web级文本语料上训练，导致难以刻画模型对相关内容的先验暴露情况，从而难以研究知识的获取与技能习得过程。缺乏一个数据范围明确、可控的实验环境来系统性地研究这些问题。

Method: 构建一个经过严格筛选的88B token预训练语料库LITTLECURRICULUM，内容限定为美国小学教材材料，明确排除五年级以上教授的概念、事实和词汇；在此基础上从头训练5B参数的LITTLELEARNER模型；通过后续注入新知识的实验（post-training和in-context learning）验证该沙盒的效用。

Result: LITTLELEARNER在开放评估中展现出足够的语言能力，但知识和能力边界清晰且与课程指南对齐。后训练和上下文学习能让模型更好地利用已有知识，但无法提升超出范围的（out-of-scope）能力。

Conclusion: 开发受限的训练环境对于研究模型知识获取与表征机制具有重要价值，为未来关于数据范围与模型能力边界的深入研究提供了可控的基准平台。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [A Repeated-Game Framework for Incentives in Decentralized Infrastructure Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12576)
*Mustafa Qazi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个重复动态激励框架，用于刻画去中心化物理基础设施网络（DePIN）中"合规"（即全努力诚实服务）的激励相容条件。核心贡献是"威慑比率"Γ，即偏离行为的私人收益与边际检测概率之比的最坏情况比率。研究发现，若任何有利可图的偏离行为不增加相对合规的失败概率，则二元公开结果协议无法威慑该类偏离；当所有有利可图的偏离都具有正检测差距，且没有弱昂贵行动更不可能失败时，合规在每个声誉层级上是序贯激励相容的，当且仅当即时罚没加上折现声誉损失至少等于Γ。该条件被用于构建协议设计问题，将服务原语映射到抵押要求、奖励计划、缓刑规则和审计频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DePIN作为去中心化物理基础设施网络，其质量控制面临核心挑战：在缺乏中心化监管的分布式环境中，如何确保服务提供者（provider）诚实履行职责（即"合规"）而非偏离以谋取私利。这是一个重复道德风险问题，需要严格的理论框架来刻画合规的激励相容条件，从而为协议设计提供理论依据。

Method: 本文将DePIN中的质量控制建模为协议与每个提供者之间的重复道德风险问题，合规通过两种机制共同执行：(1)罚没已质押的抵押品；(2)降级到声誉阶梯中缓刑层的折现威胁。作者引入"威慑比率"Γ作为关键理论工具，分析违约的私人收益与边际检测概率的最坏情况比值，并推导出序贯激励相容的充要条件，进而构建协议设计优化问题。

Result: 核心发现包括：(1) 如果任何有利可图的偏离不增加相对合规的失败概率，则二元公开结果协议无法威慑此类偏离；(2) 当所有有利可图的偏离具有正检测差距且无弱昂贵行动更不易失败时，合规序贯激励相容的充要条件是：在每个声誉层级上，即时罚没加上折现声誉损失至少达到威慑比率Γ；(3) 该条件可被形式化为协议设计问题，将服务原语映射到具体的抵押、奖励、缓刑和审计参数。

Conclusion: 该框架为DePIN协议设计提供了可操作的理论工具，明确了在何种条件下合规是激励相容的，并能够据此推导出抵押金额、奖励安排、缓刑规则和审计频率的最优设计。这为去中心化物理基础设施网络中的激励与质量控制提供了一套系统化的理论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce a repeated dynamic incentive framework for characterizing when "compliance", or full-effort honest service provision, is incentive compatible in Decentralized Physical Infrastructure Networks (DePIN). We model quality control in this setting as a repeated moral-hazard problem between the protocol and each provider, where compliance is enforced by both slashing posted collateral and the discounted threat of demotion to probation tiers on a reputation ladder. Our main contribution is the "deterrence ratio" $Γ$, the worst-case ratio of a deviation's private gain to its marginal probability of detection. We find that if any profitable deviation does not increase the fail probability relative to compliance, then binary public-outcome protocols cannot deter it. When all profitable deviations have positive detection gaps and no weakly costlier action is less likely to fail, compliance is sequentially incentive compatible if and only if immediate slashing plus discounted reputation loss is at least $Γ$ at every reputation tier. We use this condition to formulate a protocol design problem mapping service primitives to stake requirements, reward schedules, probation rules, and audit frequency.

</details>


### [165] [Error-Aware Reverse Auction Mechanism for Large Language Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12719)
*Haolong Chen,Zhengyuan Xin,Liang Zhang,Lei Xue,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于市场的LLM路由范式EA-RAM（错误感知反向拍卖机制），通过反向拍卖让提供商自我预测成功率并出价，显式建模固有双误差，证明了贝叶斯激励相容、个体理性等关键性质，并在仿真和真实基准上优于集中式路由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由依赖集中式任务中心预测模型性能，存在信息-风险不匹配问题（中心缺乏提供商内部信息却承担预测风险）以及模型池扩大时的可扩展性瓶颈，需要更高效的路由机制来平衡质量与成本。

Method: 提出基于市场的路由范式：将事前预测从中心转移到LLM提供商，通过反向拍卖机制让其以自预测成功概率和执行成本进行竞价。引入EA-RAM机制显式建模提供商预测误差与中心评估误差这一双误差（Dual Error），并分析误差抵消、饱和效应、噪声平滑等鲁棒性效应。

Result: 证明了EA-RAM在双误差下满足贝叶斯激励相容和个体理性，给出了中心理性成立的充分条件及显式福利损失界；仿真与真实基准实验表明EA-RAM对双误差具有鲁棒性，比集中式基线取得更优的成本-性能Pareto前沿，且提供商贡献本地信息时收益更大。

Conclusion: 将LLM路由从集中式预测范式转向市场化竞拍范式是有效且可扩展的，EA-RAM在理论上保证了机制的核心经济学性质，在实践中展示了成本与质量之间更优的权衡，为大规模LLM路由提供了一种去中心化的新思路。

Abstract: Routing each query to a cost-effective large language model (LLM) is critical for balancing quality and cost, yet most routers rely on a centralized task center to predict model performance, creating an information-risk mismatch and a scalability bottleneck as the model pool grows. We propose a market-based routing paradigm that shifts ex-ante prediction to LLM providers via a reverse auction, where providers bid with self-predicted success probabilities and execution costs. To account for inherently noisy provider predictions and center evaluations, we introduce the \textit{\textbf{E}rror-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}everse \textbf{A}uction \textbf{M}echanism} (EA-RAM), which explicitly models this inherent Dual Error. We prove that EA-RAM is Bayesian incentive compatible and individually rational under the Dual Error, establish sufficient conditions for center rationality, and derive an explicit welfare-loss bound. We further identify robustness effects: opposite-signed errors can cancel, vanishing-tail link functions (e.g., logistic) stabilize clear-cut cases via saturation, and extra noise smooths belief maps, reducing the gains from marginal manipulation. Experiments on simulations and real-world benchmarks show that EA-RAM is robust to the Dual Error and achieves a better cost--performance Pareto frontier than centralized baselines, with additional gains when providers contribute local information, validating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [166] [Representation in Peer Selection: A Liquid Democracy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13085)
*Davide Grossi,Grzegorz Lisowski,Georgios Papasotiropoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的偏好分布——"液体偏好分布"（liquid profiles），其灵感来源于流动民主中的委托图和信任的传递性。在该分布下，每位选民指定一个信任的代理人，并认可该代理人信任的所有人。文章研究了该域与标准受限偏好分布的关系、计算性质，以及在此域中各种比例代表制公理的满足情况，并提出了受液体分布结构启发的新投票规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行选择问题（peer selection）中，群体需要从自身成员中选出固定规模的委员会。现有研究缺乏能够反映流动民主（liquid democracy）中信任传递性结构的偏好模型，需要一种新的域限制来捕获这种传递信任关系，并在此框架下研究比例代表制的理论保证。

Method: 首先引入"液体偏好分布"的定义，其中每位选民指定一位信任的代理人，并认可该代理人信任的所有人。接着：建立该分布与标准受限偏好分布（如单峰、单交叉等）之间的关系；研究液体分布的计算复杂性（如识别、操作复杂度）；分析多种既有和新提出的比例公理在此域中的行为及相互关系；提出受液体结构启发的新投票规则并评估其比例保证。

Result: 研究发现，在液体偏好分布这一新域限制下，可以获得特别强的比例代表制保证（proportionality guarantees），并表明这些保证在既有及新提出的投票规则下均可实现。该结果表明液体分布在真实世界的同行选择问题中具有应用前景。

Conclusion: 液体偏好分布为同行选择问题提供了一个在理论上具有可操作性、并且在比例性上具有强保证的新框架。通过传递委托机制，该框架展现出在现实场景中应用的潜力，同时为比例代表制理论在这一受限域中的深化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the peer selection problem in which a group of peers must select a fixed-size committee from among themselves. To this end, we introduce a class of approval-based preference profiles, inspired by delegation graphs in liquid democracy and the transitivity of trust therein, which we call ``liquid profiles''. Under this new domain restriction, each voter designates a single trusted agent and, in turn, approves all those trusted by that individual. After establishing relationships to standard restricted preference profiles and examining computational properties of liquid profiles, we focus on understanding how novel and established proportionality axioms behave in this domain and how they relate to one another. Then, we study their satisfaction under various voting rules, including new ones that are directly inspired by the structure of liquid profiles. Our findings reveal that particularly strong proportionality guarantees are attainable in this new domain, showing promise for applications to real-world peer selection problems via transitive delegations.

</details>


### [167] [Power in Liquid Democracy: A Network Centrality Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13188)
*Davide Grossi,Andreas Nitsche,Georgios Papasotiropoulos,Oskar Skibski,Piotr Skowron,Tomasz Wąs*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个计算上可处理的框架，用于衡量液体民主（支持可传递和可暂停委派的集体决策平台）中投票者的权力，采用随机游走衰减中心性来捕捉影响在委派网络中的传播，并给出了公理化刻画、实用工具扩展以及有影响力参与者代表名单的选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体民主平台中委派网络日益复杂，需要一种计算上可行且概念上合理的度量标准来衡量投票者的实际影响力，而非传统简单的投票计数或PageRank等通用指标。

Method: 提出Random Walk Decay centrality作为权力度量，论证其直觉吸引力优于PageRank等替代方案，在权力度量类别内推导了自然公理化特征；进一步将该框架扩展为分析权力分布的实际工具，并从公理化和算法两个角度研究有影响力参与者代表名单的选取方法。

Result: 建立了计算上可处理的权力度量框架，给出了该度量在公理化体系下的自然刻画，并提出了选择代表性影响力者名单的有效算法方法。

Conclusion: Random Walk Decay central提供了一种理论上合理且计算上可行的液体民主中投票者权力度量方案，并可作为实用工具用于分析权力分布和识别关键参与者。

Abstract: This paper develops a computationally tractable framework for measuring voters' power in collective decision-making platforms that support transitive and suspendable delegations---commonly known as liquid democracy. We employ Random Walk Decay centrality to capture how influence propagates through delegation networks, argue for its intuitive appeal in this context and its advantages over alternatives such as PageRank, and derive a natural axiomatic characterization within the class of power metrics. Moreover, we conceptualize how the framework can be extended into a practical tool for analyzing power distributions in such platforms, and propose and study, both axiomatically and algorithmically, methods for selecting representative slates of influential participants.

</details>


### [168] [Keep, Customize, or Exit: Default Design and Token Pricing in LLM Reasoning Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13315)
*Ahmet Bugra Gundogan,Yigit Turkmen,Melih Bastopcu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型服务中提供商设定每token价格与默认推理token分配量的博弈问题，构建Stackelberg博弈模型，推导出用户最优定制分配量的闭式解，并通过三区域规则刻画提供商最优默认值，证明均衡存在性，最后在五个数学与科学基准上用两个紧凑推理模型验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中推理token分配量（默认配置）与定价是提供商的核心决策，而用户可在接受默认、定制分配或退出三者间选择。这项工作旨在从博弈论视角提供可解析的均衡刻画，理解默认资源配置如何影响定价、推理分配与服务效率。

Method: 以Stackelberg博弈建模提供商与用户的互动：提供商先选每token价格和默认推理分配量，用户随后选择接受默认、定制分配或退出。推导用户最优定制分配的闭式解；对任意价格，将可接受默认集刻画为空集或紧区间；通过三区域规则刻画提供商最优默认；将均衡计算降为一维价格优化并证明均衡存在性。

Result: 推导了用户唯一最优定制分配量的闭式解；可接受默认集为空集或紧区间；提供商最优默认遵循三区域规则；均衡计算可简化为一维价格优化并证明均衡存在；实验表明默认设置仅在用户重视免定制便利性时影响最终推理分配，否则服务结果均实现用户最优定制分配。两个紧凑推理模型在五个基准上的实验支持精度-token模型，揭示了模型与任务特征如何决定均衡价格、默认值与推理分配。

Conclusion: 默认推理token配置在LLM服务均衡中扮演关键角色，但其实际影响取决于用户对免定制便利性的偏好。研究提供了完整的解析性均衡框架与实证验证，为推理服务提供商在定价与默认资源配置策略上提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a large language model (LLM) service in which a provider chooses a per-token price and a default reasoning-token allocation, while a user may accept the default, customize the allocation, or exit. Larger allocations can improve accuracy but increase token cost and latency. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg game and derive the user's unique optimal customized allocation in closed form. For any price, the acceptable defaults form either an empty set or a compact interval. We characterize the provider's optimal default through a three-regime rule, reduce equilibrium computation to a one-dimensional price optimization, and prove the existence of the equilibrium. We further show that defaults affect the implemented reasoning allocation only when users value the convenience of avoiding customization; otherwise, every service-providing outcome implements the user's optimal customized allocation. Experiments with two compact open-weight reasoning models on five mathematics and science benchmarks support the accuracy-token model and show how model and task characteristics determine equilibrium prices, defaults, and reasoning allocations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Matrix-Driven Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) Surfaces Structural Framework: Continuity Limitations, Computer Graphics Algorithms, and Software Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12697)
*Hakan Üstünel*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四次Overhauser（QOVR）曲面生成框架，该框架通过变量参数的四次架构实现跨正交坐标轴的精确参数隔离，在满足C^0位置闭合和C^1切向连续性的同时实现局部形状修改，且不引发全局曲率传播或边界变形，但无法维持C^2曲率连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲面生成方法在处理边界对齐与局部形状修改时存在耦合问题，即局部调整会引发全局形变，难以满足制造和逆向工程中的密封性要求。作者旨在构建一种既能精确控制局部形状又不破坏全局边界不变性的曲面生成框架。

Method: 该框架采用变量参数的四次（第四度）新颖架构，实现跨正交坐标轴的精确参数隔离。分析管道集成方向性样条混合函数与对称空间控制矩阵，使内部节点矢量变化允许局部曲面调整，同时保持全局边界的绝对位置不变性。通过三维网格模型和色图可视化验证空间强度场的局部化特性。

Result: 计算验证确认该框架满足C^0位置闭合和C^1切向连续性条件，不触发全局曲率传播、边缘接头分离或波浪状伪影，但C^2曲率连续性未被维持。空间强度场严格限制在被修改元素的紧邻区域内，位移效应随距扰动控制点距离增大而指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该显式解耦方案保持了相邻几何补片间的结构对称性和边界不变性，满足制造和逆向工程的密封性标准，为CAGD领域提供了兼顾局部形状控制与全局边界稳定性的实用曲面生成方案。

Abstract: This study introduces the spatial and analytical construction of the Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) surface generation framework designed to resolve boundary alignment and localized shape modification constraints. This framework implements a variable parameter fourth degree novel architecture to achieve exact parameter isolation across orthogonal coordinate axes. The analytical pipeline integrates directional spline blending functions with symmetric spatial control matrices, ensuring that internal knot vector variations allow localized surface adjustments while preserving the absolute positional invariance of global edge boundaries. Computational verification confirms that while the current formulation satisfies explicit C^0 positional closure and C^1 tangent continuity conditions across the internal and boundary interfaces without triggering global curvature propagation, edge joint separation, or wave-like artifacts, C^2 curvature continuity is not maintained. As demonstrated by three-dimensional mesh models and colormap visualizations, the spatial intensity fields condense strictly within the immediate neighborhood of the modified element, verifying that the displacement effect decreases exponentially as the distance from the perturbed control points increases. This explicit decoupling preserves structural symmetry and boundary invariance across adjacent geometric patches, satisfying manufacturing and reverse engineering sealing criteria.
  Keywords: Quartic Overhauser surface; computer aided geometric design (CAGD); local shape control; geometric continuity; symmetric tensor products; computer graphics algorithms; software implementation framework.

</details>


### [170] [Blue Noise as a Lattice Gibbs Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13446)
*Zhuoran Yi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的蓝噪声采样生成方法，将蓝噪声生成形式化为二元晶格占用率上的吉布斯分布采样，通过马尔可夫链回溯采样（Coupling Towards The Past）实现有界成本的并行/分块生成，使每个瓦片独立生成且与全局生成结果逐位一致，内存仅取决于瓦片大小而非输出大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蓝噪声方法将统计建模与可扩展生成分离：优化和传输方法生成高质量点集但耦合所有样本，成本高昂且难以并行；程序化与分块采样器是局部的，但输出仅隐式定义，缺乏理论保证。作者希望统一建模与生成，使蓝噪声生成既具备统计建模的精度又有分块生成的可扩展性，同时可证明地控制误差与内存。

Method: 将蓝噪声生成为从带有成对排斥相互作用的二元晶格占用率吉布斯分布中的采样。密度、排斥强度、交互尺度、核硬度均为分布参数。由于能量为成对求和，远距离交互可被截断且分布变化有界，从而形成有界度的马尔可夫随机场。采样采用Coupling Towards The Past方法从目标状态反向追踪马尔可夫链，并在固定深度截断，从而限制成本与每个样本的依赖区域。足够的halo下，独立生成的瓦片与任何更大域上生成的相同区域逐位一致。

Result: 实验验证了模型、采样器与理论保证：生成的集成复现标准蓝噪声频谱，且参数变化时频谱连续过渡；采样器的工作量与内存与预测一致；分块输出与全域生成逐位一致。演示了14K分辨率的自适应模板化（stippling），其中现有方法需要与输出成比例的内存，同时支持多类扩展。

Conclusion: 通过将蓝噪声生成建立在吉布斯分布与MRF框架之上，该工作首次实现了统计建模与可扩展生成的统一，提供了有界错误保证的计算成本与内存，使大规模、并行、分块的蓝噪声采样成为可能，并支持多种实际应用扩展。

Abstract: Blue-noise sampling is widely used in computer graphics, but existing methods separate statistical modeling from scalable generation. Optimization and transport methods produce high-quality point sets by coupling all samples together. Procedural and tile-based samplers are local, but define their output only implicitly.
  We formulate blue-noise generation as sampling from a Gibbs distribution over binary lattice occupancies with pairwise repulsive interactions. Density, repulsion strength, interaction scale, and kernel hardness are parameters of this distribution. Because the energy sums over pairs, distant interactions can be dropped with a bounded change to the distribution, leaving a Markov random field of bounded degree.
  To sample it, we trace the Markov chain backward from the state we want, following Coupling Towards The Past, and cut the trace at a fixed depth. This bounds the cost, and it bounds the region each sample depends on. A tile generated on its own, with a sufficient halo, is then bit-identical to the same region generated on any larger domain, in any order and with no communication between tiles. Memory is set by the tile size, not by the output size, and accuracy is traded against cost through parameters with a proven error bound rather than by switching algorithms.
  We validate the model, the sampler, and these guarantees separately. The ensemble reproduces standard blue-noise spectra and moves continuously between them as its parameters vary. The sampler matches its predicted work and memory. Tiled output is verified bit-identical to full-domain generation. We demonstrate adaptive stippling at 14K, where existing methods need memory proportional to the output, along with multi-class extensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI社区缺乏对"推理"的操作性定义，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的规则化过程），并提供了一套AI推理研究交流的最佳实践检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自主推理是AI领域最受关注的主题之一，但生成式AI社区在"推理"的界定上存在严重歧义，且常隐含地拒绝逻辑学和可验证自动推理的历史传统。这种定义模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度不可验证，从而无法可靠地衡量向可信自主推理的进展。

Method: 本文采用立场论文（position paper）的方式：(1) 综合现有文献，提出操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的规则化过程；(2) 提出一套用于AI推理研究交流的最佳实践检查清单。

Result: 作者提供了两个主要产物：基于文献综合的操作性定义框架，以及一套面向AI推理研究社区的最佳实践检查清单，旨在改善推理研究的定义清晰性和可验证性。

Conclusion: 定义歧义是可以解决的。通过建立清晰的操作性定义和最佳实践沟通准则，可以使推理评估具备可验证的构念效度，从而推动可信自主推理的量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [172] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在研究诚信（科研诚信）方面的表现，发现前沿模型在高压下约三分之一的诚信关键决策会失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"（co-scientists），但其在制度压力下维护研究诚信的能力尚未被量化测量。现有评估缺乏对违规行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于证据的决策等多维度的系统测试，也缺乏对显式与隐式压力场景的区分。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，采用5级显式-隐式压力协议，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，评估3个维度：违规行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于证据的决策。对18个前沿模型变体进行全面评测。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败，模型规模和推理能力均不能可靠缓解此问题；显式压力导致模型顺从违规行为，隐式情境重构则更易造成对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；有趣的是，在违规分类上表现差的模型在基于证据的决策上表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度在结构上是分离的。

Conclusion: 正确的伦理行动并不需要准确的分类能力，前沿模型可能表面上很有帮助但潜藏诚信缺陷，产生两种部署风险：助长研究不端行为和侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [173] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观点：AI对齐方法（原本用于防止有害输出）是一种双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用于信息审查和操纵。随着AI作为信息提供者的迅速普及、经济权力不对称以及政治环境趋向威权，这种滥用风险正在加剧，应尽早讨论并采取缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术初衷是确保模型行为安全，但其底层机制（控制输出、引导行为）同样可被恶意行为者利用来实施信息审查和舆论操纵。这种双重用途风险尚未被充分讨论。

Method: 通过将当前对齐技术映射到可能及实际发生的滥用案例，论证对齐技术存在双重用途属性，并分析风险加剧的三个因素（AI用户普及、经济不对称、政治威权化趋势）。

Result: 研究表明对齐技术客观上为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息主导工具，追求"完美对齐"的过程同时增强了滥用潜力。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制被故意滥用的可能性，并提出缓解策略以防范这一双重用途风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [174] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过ETHICS派生基准（500条目，五个道德领域），比较人类标注者与LLM在最终标签和支撑理由两个层面的道德判断一致性。发现模型在标签层面与人类多数派高度一致，但在理由层面系统性偏离人类道德基础（如伤害、尊重、承诺、正义、应得、借口相关性等类别的注意力再分配），据此论证标签一致性不应等同于模型与人类的道德对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用"与人类判断一致"作为评估LLM道德对齐的代理指标，但最终标签一致并不保证模型与人类依赖于相同的道德理由、原则或情境假设——两个智能体可能在得出相同结论的同时诉诸不同的道德依据。这种标签层面的虚假安慰掩盖了模型在道德推理层面的实质偏差，需要更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 构建一个包含500个条目的ETHICS派生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域；同时收集人类标注者和LLM（前沿闭源与开源模型家族）的最终标签及支撑理由标注；在标签层面计算与人类多数派的一致性，在理由层面通过类别注意力分配分析来检验模型与人类在道德基础（harm、respect、promise-keeping、justice、desert、excuse relevance等）上的系统性差异。

Result: 尽管前沿和开源模型在最终标签上与人类标注者多数派的一致性通常很高，但理由层面的分析揭示了人类与模型在道德基础上存在系统性分歧：即使最终标签匹配人类多数派，模型在各类道德范畴上的注意力分配明显重排，说明模型使用了与人类不同的道德推理路径。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应被视为道德对齐的等价指标；仅基于标签的评估会产生误导性的乐观结论，必须辅以对模型判断中所表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能真正评估LLM与人类道德对齐的程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [175] [BoardroomAI: Dependency-Aware Human-Steerable Multi-Agent Deliberation through Evolving Decision Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13046)
*Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoardroomAI框架，将人类作为多智能体决策系统中的持续参与者，而非仅提供初始问题后被动接收结果。系统通过类型化决策图、干预编译器、依赖感知传播和评估框架四个组件，实现人机共存的决策过程，实验表明其传播效率高、选择性修复有效，但也暴露出决策上下文不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于转录的多智能体系统中，人类仅提供初始问题后便不再参与，无法在决策过程中实时介入（如挑战假设、修改约束、引入证据或调整优先级）。然而现实中的组织决策是演进的，需要人类作为持续参与者与人机共享决策过程。

Method: 提出四个核心组件：(i) 类型化决策图，编码证据、假设、约束、主张、异议、替代方案、风险、决策及其语义依赖和专家责任；(ii) 干预编译器，将确认的人类行动转换为显式图更新；(iii) 依赖感知传播，识别受影响的子图、保留未受影响的工件、选择性重新激活相关专家；(iv) 评估框架，衡量干预影响、修复覆盖率、保留度、重计算量和决策有效性。

Result: 在600个生成的决策DAG干预中，传播结果与穷举影响计算完全一致，且仅检查14.59%的节点。在12个案例的探索性试点中，选择性修复重计算了62.11%的规范节点，保留了所有金标准中未受影响的节点，6个案例产生有效更新决策，其余6个案例选择弃权。

Conclusion: 弃权结果表明，即使干预路由正确，也可能缺乏足够的上下文进行综合，这推动了对人类操控的多智能体协商中"决策充分上下文闭合"的探索。所有结果均为合成数据和原型级别，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Organizational decisions are co-created while evidence, constraints, and human priorities continue to evolve. In conventional transcript-based multi-agent systems, humans typically provide an initial problem, agents deliberate internally, and the system returns a final response. BoardroomAI instead treats the human as a persistent participant who can intervene by challenging assumptions, modifying constraints, changing priorities, introducing evidence, or redirecting the decision process. We operationalize this human--agent coexistence through four components: (i) a typed decision graph representing evidence, assumptions, constraints, claims, objections, alternatives, risks, decisions, semantic dependencies, and specialist responsibility; (ii) an intervention compiler that converts confirmed human actions into explicit graph updates; (iii) dependency-aware propagation that identifies affected subgraphs, preserves unaffected artifacts, and selectively reactivates relevant specialists; and (iv) an evaluation framework measuring intervention impact, repair coverage, preservation, recomputation, and decision validity. Across 600 generated decision-DAG interventions, propagation matched exhaustive impact computation while inspecting only 14.59% of nodes. In a 12-case exploratory pilot, selective repair recomputed 62.11% of canonical nodes, preserved all gold-unaffected nodes, and produced valid updated decisions in six cases while abstaining in the remaining six. These abstentions show that correct intervention routing may still provide insufficient context for synthesis, motivating a \emph{decision-sufficient context closure} for human-steered multi-agent deliberation. All results are synthetic and prototype-level.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream系统，其核心是LLM-MR-CNP协议——扩展经典合同网协议（CNP），引入语义CFP（招标书）表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，以应对移动边缘-云异构流处理系统中的去中心化调度挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统正越来越多地运行在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格的QoS要求，这使得去中心化调度变得复杂，现有方法难以同时处理动态环境与硬性约束。

Method: MAS-DecStream的核心是LLM-MR-CNP协议：边缘集群代理基于本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，利用自然语言细化卸载提案；硬性资源和QoS约束保持确定性，同时通过语义CFP、渐进式信息披露、多轮提案修订和协商记忆增强协商能力。

Result: 基于阿里ASI流量轨迹的实验表明：延迟违规降低至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理时冲突解决率达0.91，效用相比多轮基于规则的基线提升最多22%。25个案例的评估显示模型和提示词相关的准确率-成本权衡。多轮CNP修订是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在定性和不确定运行时上下文场景下增值。

Conclusion: 实验初步证明多轮CNP修订是协议级别的主要收益来源，LLM辅助主要针对定性和不确定的运行时上下文提供额外价值，为异构边缘-云流处理中的去中心化调度提供了新的方案方向。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [177] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅需要行为对齐，还应与其用户以相似方式推理并忠实传达推理过程，并提出了实现该目标的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地扮演决策辅助、决策委托乃至自主决策者的角色，现有的对齐方法（如LLM对齐）主要关注行为层面的一致性，而忽视了AI推理方式与用户认知方式的对齐。认知错位可能阻碍AI在众多应用场景中的采用，特别是在高风险的决策场景中，用户既需要愿意信赖AI，也需要有充分理由信赖AI。

Method: 论文采用立场论文（position paper）的方式，综合评述了认知对齐如何提升AI的可理解性和可信度的现有证据，并提供了新的调查数据表明许多用户在AI判断或行动的推理过程对其重要时，认为认知对齐是"必不可少的"。随后识别了现有对齐方法与认知对齐之间的差距，并提出研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示许多用户将认知对齐视为"必不可少"的要素；论文指出现有对齐方法（主要聚焦行为对齐）与认知对齐需求之间存在显著差距，识别了这一差距并提出了系统性的研究议程。

Conclusion: 认知错位是AI在众多预期应用中采用的主要潜在障碍；解决认知对齐问题对于创造用户既有意愿、又有充分理由信赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [178] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型的安全生产行为具有语言依赖性：在核打击等高危战略场景中，用日语提示词可使Claude和Gemini等模型的发射率显著下降，且机制在于模型被要求用日语推理时自发产生道德词汇（如"道德代价""数百万生命"），而非输入语言本身。仅用英语评估会遗漏多语言编码的安全风险与保障机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于战略与咨询场景，但安全对齐评估通常仅用英语进行。研究者希望探究提示词的语言是否可能改变模型在高风险场景中的决策，以揭示仅用英语评估可能存在的盲区。

Method: 对六家提供商的九种模型进行单轮博弈论小场景测试，场景中模型为核武装国家是否打击无防御对手提供建议。提示词刻意去道德化且在语言间策略上完全一致。进行跨语言实验以分离语言输入与推理语言的作用，并观察模型推理时自发产生的词汇。

Result: 日语提示使Claude系列模型发射率显著下降（Sonnet 4.6在不必要打击场景从40%降至0%，在争议场景从93%降至17%），Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。当在英语提示中指示用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%，证明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动该效应。五种其他模型无语言效应但几乎在所有条件下都发射。效应仅存在于已在英语中犹豫的模型。

Conclusion: LLM的安全对齐行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会同时遗漏其他语言中的风险与保障机制。语言本身会改变模型在高风险决策中的安全行为，这对安全评估方法论提出重要挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [179] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，一种在推理阶段不对称分配计算的方法：主流处理完整prompt并写入KV缓存，辅助流仅在续写解码阶段激活，在不增加prompt预填充成本的情况下增加续写预测的计算量。两流共享主要注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，在MoE模型中可通过独立控制主/辅助专家扇出来分别调节预填充和续写成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的推理成本（特别是累积推理成本）正变得比一次性训练成本更重要。但传统的宽度或深度扩展会同时增加预填充（prefill）和自回归解码（decode）两个阶段的成本，因为每一层在两种阶段都要被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算只分配给续写预测阶段，而不增加prompt处理阶段的开销。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流是完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最后一个prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测计算但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两流共享主要注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主流和辅助流的专家扇出可独立控制，分别调节预填充成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的对比中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下实现了更低的验证损失。MoE实验中研究了两种场景：固定预填充专家计算下增加解码计算，以及将固定解码专家预算在两流间重新分配，揭示了预填充-解码-质量之间的权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明可以打破传统模型中预填充成本与解码成本强耦合的限制，通过阶段特定的计算分配实现更高效的语言建模。这种设计在推理成本优化方面具有实际意义，特别是在需要大量续写生成的服务场景中。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [180] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化元LoRA框架，结合元学习LoRA初始化与证据质量感知的自适应更新强度校准，并将个性化先验分解为可读用户提示和拓扑保持软token领域条件，解决跨域零/少样本个性化中的过拟合与负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零/少样本个性化旨在仅用少量目标域交互生成用户偏好响应。现有自适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合；历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。需要有效机制来校准适应力度并解耦偏好来源。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法：1) 使用元学习的LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点和先验中心；2) 根据支撑集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，稀疏或模糊证据下限制过拟合，证据增长时允许更强的个性化；3) 将个性化先验函数化解耦为用户和领域两个组件：人类可读提示表达稳定偏好，拓扑保持软token实现领域特定隐空间条件化。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，相对最佳竞争基线将跨域胜率退化减少47.9%，在未见用户冷启动下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 证据质量感知的适应校准与偏好先验的功能性解耦是跨域个人化的关键设计，有效缓解了稀疏证据下的过拟合与源域伪影带来的负迁移，显著提升跨域与冷启动场景下的个性化质量。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [181] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统，该系统通过聊天界面整合了科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多源数据，支持快速问答和复杂多步骤研究任务，并已在公司研发工作流中规模化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员在广泛多样的生物医学数据源中进行探索，克服数据分散、查询复杂的挑战，提升日常研发工作效率。

Method: 系统采用聊天式界面，提供快速直接问答模式和面向复杂研究任务的多步骤模式；基于检索到的证据进行回答，并将回答链接回原始数据源。论文概述了系统架构、主要产品设计选择以及规模化部署经验。

Result: 系统已成功部署于AstraZeneca内部的日常研发工作流，支持科学家和临床人员在多类数据源中进行问答和复杂研究任务。

Conclusion: 本文以技术笔记形式分享了系统架构、设计决策和规模化部署的经验教训，为其他组织构建类似内部LLM研究助手提供了参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [182] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统研究了LLM在多重约束同时作用下的性能退化规律。通过15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查（k=1-12），发现：整体成功率随约束数量呈乘法式崩塌（k=8时仅5.7%），结构性约束比词汇约束退化更快，失败近似独立导致累积效应，可靠指令跟随在5-6个约束后崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛部署于需要同时满足多个显式约束的场景（推理结构、安全边界、输出格式），但组合约束场景的性能特征尚未被充分刻画。现有研究多聚焦单一约束的遵循能力，缺乏对约束组合退化机制的系统性理解，无法回答退化速率、退化根源及缓解策略等关键问题。

Method: 设计CSE基准：程序化生成测试样本，系统地改变同时约束的数量k（1到12），每个约束由确定性规则验证器评分，完全排除LLM法官的参与。共覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753项逐约束检查。通过逐约束通过率、全约束联合成功率、约束类型分组比较（结构vs.词汇）及失败独立性分析来表征组合退化。

Result: 三项关键发现：（1）逐约束通过率随k增加呈渐进可预测衰减，但全约束联合成功率急剧崩塌（k=8时40%+的单项通过率仅为5.7%的全成功概率）；（2）约束类型退化不均——结构性约束每增加一个约束其基础能力损失是词法约束的2倍，退化差异由理解-维持缺口决定，区分需要持续跟踪的约束与不受组合影响的二元决策约束；（3）失败几乎独立，使累积效应呈乘法性质；残余耦合源于共享输出特征而非逐对干扰（如句子计数错误会导致所有读取该特征的约束同时失败）。可靠指令遵循在5-6个约束后失效：最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时即告失败。

Conclusion: LLM在组合约束场景下的能力退化不是均匀的意外失误，而是具有可预测性的系统性失效。失败近似独立性意味着约束累积效果具备乘法放大效应，且不同类型的约束具有不同的退化敏感度。该发现为评估和改进模型在真实多约束部署场景中的可靠性提供了量化基准和方向性指导。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [183] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MindMemOS，一个可移植且自进化的记忆操作系统层，利用统一的"实体-属性-时间"结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能进化。核心算法MindMemEvolve通过验证驱动的进化搜索优化记忆模式，结合梦境机制合并冗余记录并解决冲突，同时利用隐式纠正反馈作为人在环路信号来修正不准确的记忆。MindSkillEvolve算法将智能体执行轨迹转化为可复用且不断精炼的技能。实验表明MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%，MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上将成功率提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体的记忆系统在开发完成后往往保持固定不变，缺乏自适应能力，无法根据持续使用调整其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了智能体在长期交互中的适应性和个性化表现。
