Conclusion: 研究为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和harness设计提供了具体方向，揭示了自主智能体在长周期科研任务中的能力边界与发展空间。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [248] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小型语言模型（SLM）在边缘设备上支持认知具身智能体的关键组件（"Think"与"Memory"），基于CEAA架构开发了一个边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上使用不同规模的Qwen2.5模型进行验证，通过仿真实验证明了SLM驱动原型智能体的路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟智能体需要在复杂虚拟世界和元宇宙中运行，要求具备持久性、适应性和上下文感知能力，但实现认知能力的智能体在概念和技术上均面临巨大挑战。现有CEAA框架提供了实现导向的架构蓝图，但如何在边缘计算和生成式AI的背景下高效实现其关键组件仍有待探索。

Method: 基于CEAA架构，聚焦"Think"和"Memory"两个核心认知过程，利用边缘计算和SLM（Qwen2.5不同规模模型）构建边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上部署，通过仿真实验评估服务请求处理能力、路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟。

Result: 仿真实验结果表明，SLM驱动的原型智能体系统能够部分实现CEAA的选定过程，在路由准确性和记忆读取性能上表现良好，延迟可接受，证明SLM可以在边缘端支持具身智能体的认知操作。

Conclusion: SLM驱动的边缘智能体网关系统为在沉浸式虚拟世界中开发认知能力高效、上下文敏感的具身智能体提供了一条可行路径，能够部分支持CEAA认知流程，为未来元宇宙交互体验奠定基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [249] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL）框架及其自动化评估方法RAIL。AIRL是一个九级序数尺度，结合环境证据阶梯、维度上限、通用性锚定规则和明确的赋值纪律，使就绪度可从自然语言描述直接判定。RAIL采用独立LLM专家小组分类器，由一个证据代理和六个维度代理组成，通过确定性最小规则聚合并由首席专家审查，避免单一LLM分类器的过估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估人工智能技术成熟度对于投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架（如TRL、MLTRL、AI/数据就绪度维度模型）存在异构性问题：TRL缺乏AI特定门控标准，MLTRL需要内部过程工件访问权，而AI/数据就绪度模型的尺度难以直接比较，且这些框架难以自动化应用。

Method: 本文包含两个贡献：（1）提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL），这是一个九级序数尺度，建立在环境证据阶梯之上，辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识和算法成熟度），并包含通用性锚定规则和显式赋值纪律，使就绪度仅从自然语言描述即可判定。（2）提出RAIL（通过独立LLM专家的就绪度评估），这是一个专家小组分类器：一个证据代理和六个独立维度代理（每个都是具有狭窄任务范围的大语言模型）给出判决，由确定性最小规则聚合，首席专家在不对称权限下审查——仅确认或降低小组推荐，绝不提升至上限之上。

Result: 该方法在分析多个研究工作时进行了测试，结果表明其具有一致性，并避免了单一LLM分类器的过估计问题。

Conclusion: 统一AI就绪度评估框架AIRL配合RAIL方法，能够实现从自然语言描述自动、可靠地判定AI技术成熟度，为投资、项目管理与政策监测提供了可操作的工具。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [250] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）采用的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目的方式整合教学、科研和大学延伸三大支柱，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织培养技术与横向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但缺少一个灵活且可复制的组织框架来整合教学、科研和大学延伸三大支柱。

Method: 提出一个结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织的组织框架，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响的AI应用等代表性项目进行实证说明。

Result: 该框架成功展示了多样化教育、科研和延伸活动可在统一组织架构下开展，同时促进领导力、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为工程与计算机教育中整合大学三大支柱提供了灵活且可复制的模型，可为学术联盟及类似学生组织提供实践指导。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [251] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统研究世界模型（WM），提出因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，强调WM不应仅具备生成能力，还需捕获实体属性、实体间及实体与环境间的交互关系，并将其与因果表示学习、因果发现、结构因果模型等现有文献建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为智能体超越训练分布进行预测、规划和行动的基础，但现有世界模型主要聚焦于生成能力，缺乏对环境中因果结构和动态机制的显式建模。作者认为，有用的世界模型必须捕获决定和解释系统动力学的实体属性与交互关系，而这一点在现有研究中尚未被系统性地形式化。

Method: 作者从多个抽象层次（感知观测到概念表征）出发，以因果视角对世界模型进行形式化定义，提出Causal World Models（CWMs）的框架。该框架将世界建模与因果表示学习、以对象为中心的学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等已有工作相连接，并通过可辨识性（identifiability）分析阐明世界模型的各组件何时以及能在何种等价程度上从数据中恢复。

Result: 作者提供了CWM的形式化定义，并建立了世界建模与因果相关研究领域的映射关系，同时澄清了世界模型组件可辨识性的条件与等价性，为此类模型的理论保证提供了依据。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型植根于支持因果推理和明智决策的表征与结构之中，作者论证了因果视角能为世界模型的研究提供更加坚实的理论基础，使其超越单纯的生成能力，服务于智能体的因果推理与决策制定。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [252] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源框架，用确定性多智能体编排替代传统的单体LLM提示，用于临床推理。它协调专门角色（提取、推理、答案生成、评估）的智能体，支持显式上下文传递和可追溯的中间输出，实现逐阶段失败归因。框架还引入Decomposer模块，可从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定智能体提示，无需手动提示工程，支持API和本地CPU部署，完全通过YAML配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单体LLM提示方法在临床推理中存在可解释性不足、难以进行失败定位、提示工程繁琐等问题，且对临床领域专家不友好，亟需一种可解释、可配置、无需编程技能的多智能体框架来改善临床推理流程。

Method: MARC采用确定性多智能体编排架构，包含四个角色专门化智能体：提取（extraction）、推理（reasoning）、答案生成（answer generation）和评估（evaluation）。通过显式上下文传递和可追溯的中间输出来支持逐阶段失败归因。引入Decomposer模块，自动从自然语言描述生成任务特定的智能体提示。框架通过YAML完全配置，支持API和本地CPU两种部署模式，设计为模型无关。

Result: 框架已开源发布于GitHub（https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1），支持API和本地CPU兼容部署，无需代码修改即可完成YAML配置。Decomposer模块消除了手动提示工程需求，使临床领域专家无需编程专业知识即可使用。

Conclusion: MARC通过确定性多智能体协作框架提升了临床推理的可解释性、可访问性和失败归因能力，是一个面向临床领域、模型无关、无需编程技能的开源解决方案。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [253] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlayaWorld的改进版本，核心思想是让条件信号在潜空间表示和时间结构上尽可能匹配生成内容。主要改动包括：用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代原有的深度扭曲空间记忆，并将视觉条件编码到与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜空间中，共引入六项具体修改以提升视频生成的条件一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视频生成任务中，条件信号（如空间记忆、视角控制等）如果与生成内容在潜空间表示和时间结构上不一致，会导致生成质量下降、动作不连贯和条件控制不精确等问题。作者希望通过重新设计条件信号的表示与集成方式，使条件与生成内容在表示和时间维度上紧密对齐，从而提升整体生成效果。

Method: 在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变的前提下，提出六项修改：(1) 用运动感知潜条件替代静态帧图像条件；(2) 将重新渲染的空间记忆以连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间中对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃（直接移除记忆token而非置零）；(5) 统一训练与推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视角控制完全由重新渲染的空间条件提供。

Result: 通过上述六项修改，该版本大幅改进了条件信号的表示与集成方式，使条件在潜空间和时间结构上与生成内容保持一致，预期在视频生成的质量、条件控制精度和时序一致性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: AlayaWorld的改进版本验证了"条件信号应与生成内容在潜表示和时间结构上对齐"这一设计原则的有效性，为视频生成中的条件建模提供了新的思路。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [254] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，通过精确最终状态验证评估LLM编码智能体在Bash命令生成中因接口序列化/包装/重解析引入的错误。实验显示，同一回复通过额外解析器后成功率下降55.4-73.2个百分点，披露边界后六个配置恢复30.4-60.7点。研究揭示匹配分数掩盖了生成错误与执行错误之间的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体通过接口发出Bash命令时，模型输出会被序列化、包装和重新解析。仅靠匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成阶段的错误与生成后执行传输阶段引入的错误，这导致对模型能力的评估存在根本性混淆。

Method: 构建QuoteBench基准，包含14个事件衍生家族共56个一次性任务，采用精确最终状态验证。通过刻意取消转义的额外解析器跨越"生成契约"与"执行传输"的边界，并在8种同窗口配置下进行重放实验，比较原始输出与经解析器处理后输出的成功率差异，同时测试披露边界后的恢复效果。

Result: 重放同一回复经额外解析器后成功率降低55.4-73.2个百分点；六个配置在披露边界后恢复30.4-60.7个百分点，另两个配置恢复为零或轻微负值。原始生成在前沿模型上接近饱和，边界适应能力成为模型区分的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的-3.6点匹配差异掩盖了-64.3点损伤和+60.7点补偿。部署配置会重排模型排名，26对可比模型中有一对明确反转，四对处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 评估命令发出智能体时应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、运行点和最终状态验证器，而非将匹配分数视为模型的固有属性。匹配分数掩盖了生成与执行之间的复杂交互，需要更透明的评估方法论。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [255] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端、全模态的AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图）进行多学科研究，通过感知层和3个自主代理（想法、实验、写作）组成确定性流水线，在36个真实数据案例中完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整路径，平均论文得分6.3，直接感知在全部7个评估维度上优于仅依赖预计算标量特征的盲变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽然覆盖了研究流程的各个环节，但通常只对文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，无法访问对科学发现起决定性作用的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系，导致智能体无法获得完整的证据基础。

Method: OmniScientist采用端到端全模态架构：包含一个感知层（处理异构原始证据）和3个自主代理（想法生成、实验执行、论文写作），运行在确定性流水线中。系统在代码层面执行想法检查、严谨性检查和声明检查，实现新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追踪性。

Result: 系统在36个跨越5个学科家族、4类科学证据、9种模态的真实数据案例中，全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整路径，平均论文得分6.3（参考推理骨干）。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体配对比较中，直接感知在全部7个评估维度上胜出，赢得85%的头部对比判断。

Conclusion: 生命周期级别的感知能力对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [ARIES-Mission2: A Zero-Shot Vision-Language-Action Framework for Fast Large-Scale Aerial Mission Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12763)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Haochen Li,Yifu Zhao,Dexing Yao,Baili Lu,Zikun Li,Yapeng Wang,Sio-Kei Im,Dingcheng Yang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出ARIES-Mission2，一个零样本视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，用于低空无人机任务生成。该框架将视觉语义感知与物理路线优化解耦，利用DeepSeek-V3进行任务解析、Molmo-7B进行零样本目标定位，并将检测到的像素位置通过地理空间插值转换为GPS航点。后端将多目标无人机关卡建模为旅行商问题(TSP)，对比PSO、GPSO、IPSO及原始VLM顺序四种路线方案，选择最小成本闭环路线。在UAV-VLPA-nano-30基准上，总飞行距离62.43 km，比未优化的VLA基线缩短21.6%，比人工规划缩短9.5%，每个任务平均19.18秒，比人类专家快约3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在语义理解上表现强劲，但直接用于低空无人机任务生成时受限于空间优化能力弱和路线规划效率低的问题。现有VLA方法生成的访问顺序常导致冗余回溯，缺乏物理层面的路线优化。

Method: 提出ARIES-Mission2零样本VLA框架：(1)前端使用DeepSeek-V3进行自然语言任务解析，Molmo-7B进行零样本目标定位(检测像素位置)；(2)通过地理空间插值将像素坐标转换为GPS航点；(3)后端将多目标遍历建模为TSP问题，对比原始VLM顺序与PSO、GPSO、IPSO四种候选闭环路线，选择最小成本路线生成任务。

Result: 在UAV-VLPA-nano-30基准上：总飞行距离62.43 km，比未优化VLA基线(79.66 km)缩短21.6%，比人工规划(69.00 km)缩短9.5%；30个任务总耗时575.40 s，平均19.18 s/任务，比人类专家快约3.6倍；VLM推理占主导(19.02 s/任务)，TSP求解仅需0.16 s/任务；可扩展性分析表明TSP模块在多目标场景下计算时间增长更平缓。

Conclusion: 将VLM的视觉语义理解与基于TSP的物理路线优化解耦，是提升无人机自主任务生成效率的有效范式。该框架在路线长度、规划时间和可扩展性三方面均显著优于VLA基线和人类专家，验证了语言-视觉-动作结合组合优化的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong semantic understanding capabilities, but their direct use in low-altitude Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) mission generation remains limited by weak spatial optimization and inefficient route planning. To address this issue, we propose ARIES-Mission2, a zero-shot Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that decouples visual-semantic perception from physical route optimization. Given natural-language instructions and satellite imagery, ARIES-Mission2 first uses DeepSeek-V3 for task parsing and Molmo-7B for zero-shot target grounding, and then converts detected pixel locations into GPS waypoints through geospatial interpolation. To reduce the redundant backtracking caused by raw VLM-generated visiting orders, the back end formulates multi-target UAV traversal as a Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and compares four candidate routes, including the raw VLM order and the routes optimized by PSO, GPSO, and IPSO. The minimum-cost closed-loop route is then selected for mission generation. Experiments on the UAV-VLPA-nano-30 benchmark show that ARIES-Mission2 achieves a total flight distance of 62.43 km, reducing the route length by 21.6% compared with the unoptimized VLA baseline (79.66 km) and by 9.5% compared with manual human planning (69.00 km). The complete 30-task workflow takes 575.40 s, averaging 19.18 s per task, which is approximately 3.6 times faster than human expert planning. Component-level timing shows that VLM inference dominates the runtime with 19.02 s per task, while the TSP solver requires only 0.16 s per task. Scalability analysis further indicates that the TSP module maintains lower growth in computation time as the number of targets increases.

</details>


### [257] [Julia for CFD: A Critical Survey of Ecosystem, Performance, and Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12801)
*Tianbai Xiao*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于Julia语言在计算流体力学（CFD）中应用的批判性综述，评估了Julia在高性能科学计算领域（包括大规模分布式CPU、多GPU、自动微分和软件组合）的适用性与局限性，指出Julia的核心优势在于通过共享类型、分派和特化实现各能力的一体化集成，但在生态广度、工业工具链和部署经验上仍不及成熟的C/C++/Fortran环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CFD越来越多地被嵌入到设计、推理、优化和数据驱动建模等工作流中，需要连接物理模型、数值内核、异构硬件、微分和机器学习。Julia提供了独特的高层科学抽象与性能特化相结合的能力，但缺乏对其实际应用价值的系统评估，因此需要一篇批判性综述来考察该范式的优势与局限。

Method: 作者采用批判性综述（critical survey）的方法，系统回顾了多个代表性开源Julia CFD项目，并综合应用层面的证据，从性能、可扩展性、加速器可移植性、自动微分和软件组合等维度进行评估。同时与C++性能可移植性框架、有限元领域特定语言（DSL）以及基于JAX的可微分CFD进行对比分析。

Result: 已发表的证据表明，Julia原生CFD在大型分布式CPU系统和多GPU平台上表现可信，并出现了新兴的可微分工作流。但与C++框架、有限元DSL和JAX进行比较后发现，Julia的这些能力并非独有；其独特之处在于通过共享类型、分派和特化实现了一体化集成。目前Julia已在多个CFD领域超越了概念验证阶段，但在生态完整性和工业部署方面仍有差距。

Conclusion: Julia在CFD中的应用证据是混合的——它已从概念验证迈向实际可用，但其最突出的当前角色是作为开发和测试将仿真与下游分析相连接的CFD架构的平台。Julia在生态广度、工业工具链和部署经验方面仍落后于成熟的C/C++/Fortran生态，不宜全盘取代传统方案。

Abstract: Modern CFD increasingly places simulation inside workflows for design, inference, optimization, and data-driven modeling, creating pressure to connect physical models, numerical kernels, heterogeneous hardware, differentiation, and learning. Julia offers a distinctive approach: high-level scientific abstractions can be specialized for performance and composed within a common language and compiler ecosystem. This critical survey examines where that model benefits CFD software and where its limits remain. We review representative open-source projects and synthesize application-level evidence on performance, scalability, accelerator portability, automatic differentiation, and software composition. Published results demonstrate credible Julia-native CFD on large distributed CPU systems and multi-GPU platforms, as well as emerging differentiable workflows. Comparisons with C++ performance-portability frameworks, finite-element domain-specific languages, and JAX-based differentiable CFD show that these capabilities are not unique to Julia. Julia's distinction is their integration through shared types, dispatch, and specialization. The evidence is mixed: Julia has progressed beyond proof of concept in several CFD regimes, but still lacks the ecosystem breadth, industrial tooling, and deployment experience of established C/C++/Fortran environments. Its strongest current role is as a platform for developing and testing CFD architectures that connect simulation with downstream analysis.

</details>


### [258] [Spinal Coupling in Frontal and Transversal Plane During Gait - A Segmental and Time-Dependent Analysis of the Thoracic and Lumbar Spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12945)
*Jonas Dully,Carlo Dindorf,Henriette Rönsch,Dennis Perchthaler,Jürgen Konradi,Ulrich Betz,Michael Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用非侵入性光栅立体摄影系统与仪器化跑步机，在642名受试者中首次在单个椎体水平（T3至L4）对步态中脊柱侧方偏移与轴向旋转之间的耦合行为进行了动态量化，揭示了颅-尾方向的耦合模式变化，并证明了静态矢状面姿势对这一耦合的显著调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱侧方偏移与轴向旋转之间的耦合是脊柱力学中已知的特征，但其在步态中单个椎体水平的具体表现以及其与矢状面姿势的关联仍知之甚少。现有研究缺乏对个体椎体层面动态耦合行为的系统性量化。

Method: 采用非侵入性光栅立体摄影系统结合仪器化跑步机，对642名受试者步态中T3至L4每个椎体的旋转与侧方偏移的时间依赖性耦合进行量化。使用绝对相位滞后、归一化有向面积和傅里叶级数导出的倾斜角三个指标分析耦合行为，并考察静态矢状面姿势对耦合的影响。

Result: 三个指标均呈现颅-尾方向的空间模式，几乎所有相邻椎体之间存在显著差异，且存在多个耦合行为发生变化的转折点。广义与合并的静态矢状面姿势均显著调节这些模式，个体相对于静态矢状面姿势的偏差亦对各指标产生影响。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了步态中单个椎体水平脊柱耦合的动态特征描述，为理解脊柱生物力学及静态矢状面姿势的影响奠定了基础，对脊柱疾病的诊断与治疗具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Introduction: Coupling between lateral deviation and axial rotation is a known feature of spinal mechanics, yet its behavior at the individual vertebral level during gait, as well as the association with sagittal posture remains poorly understood. Methods: This study analyzed spinal kinematics in a diverse cohort (n=642) using a non-invasive rasterstereography system with an instrumented treadmill, quantifying time-dependent coupling of rotation and lateral deviation for each vertebra from T3 to L4 during walking as well as the influence of sagittal posture on this coupling. Coupling behavior was analyzed with absolute phase lag, normalized signed area and tilt angle derived from the Fourier series. Results: Results revealed cranio-caudal patterns for all three metrics with differences between almost all adjacent vertebrae and different turning points, i.e., where the coupling behavior changed (from increase to decrease and vice versa). Generalized, as well as pooled static sagittal posture significantly modulated these patterns, while individual deviations from static sagittal posture influenced the metrics. Conclusion: These findings provide the first dynamic, vertebra-level characterization of spinal coupling during gait. Furthermore, they offer a foundation for an understanding of spinal biomechanics and the influence of static sagittal posture, potentially relevant to the diagnosis and treatment of spinal disorders.

</details>


### [259] [TIEM: Temporal Integration of Hypergraph Evidence and Skill Memory for Event-Driven Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13024)
*Wenjin Liu,Shen Pang,Tiesunlong Shen,Zhe Cui,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: TIEM是一个时间戳门控框架，用于事件驱动的催化剂结果预测，通过事件证据超图、基于案例的技能记忆和异构证据-经验融合推理三个组件，解决训练数据污染和时间泄漏导致的"证据鸿沟"问题。在五个金融预测基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索与记忆增强的大语言模型智能体在催化剂结果预测中表现良好，但训练数据污染和时间泄漏会形成"证据鸿沟"（Evidence Chasm），使报告的准确率与真实预测能力之间存在差距。现有的方法缺乏对时间戳的严格约束和对训练截止日期（training cutoff）的显式验证。

Method: TIEM框架包含三个协调组件：(1) 事件证据超图（EEH）用于时间戳过滤的多层级检索；(2) 基于案例的技能记忆（CSM）用于源标记的时间技能存储；(3) 异构证据-经验融合推理（HEFR）进行证据与经验的双轨融合及预测。此外引入FinPURE基准（近期A股留出集）和Name-Date Probe来评估每个模型的名称-日期敏感性，而不是假设训练截止日期。

Result: 在五个金融预测基准上的实验结果表明，TIEM优于当前基线方法。同时，FinPURE为近期时期提供了一个更可靠的无污染评测基准。

Conclusion: TIEM通过时间戳门控机制有效弥合了"证据鸿沟"，为事件驱动的金融催化剂预测提供了更可信的评测与预测方案。

Abstract: Event-driven catalyst-outcome forecasting increasingly uses retrieval- and memory-augmented large language model agents for prediction. However, training-data contamination and temporal leakage can create an Evidence Chasm between reported accuracy and true predictive ability. We propose TIEM, a timestamp-gated framework with three coordinated components: an Event-Evidence Hypergraph (EEH) for timestamp-filtered multi-tier retrieval; a Case-based Skill Memory (CSM) for source-tagged temporal skills; and Heterogeneous Evidence-Experience Fusion Reasoning (HEFR) for evidence-experience fusion and prediction. We also introduce FinPURE, a recent-period A-share holdout benchmark, and use a Name-Date Probe to assess per-model name-date sensitivity rather than assuming training cutoffs. Results on five financial forecasting benchmarks show TIEM outperforms current baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/Fin_TIEM.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LoKiFormer，一种新型LLM架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和MoE知识存储与计算路径耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练阶段存在两个主要低效问题：（1）自注意力缺乏对局部性的显式归纳偏置，导致对序列内部局部信息的冗余建模；（2）混合专家（MoE）模型将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准decoder架构上增加两个专用模块：1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA)，在注意力中引入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力能作用于更具信息量的表示；2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM)，引入参数化的键值记忆，在可寻址的槽位中显式存储全局知识，将存储与计算解耦，支持直接的知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在局部和全局信息集成上的高效性与有效性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过解耦局部信息建模（LFA）和全局知识存储（KMM），在预训练效率上显著优于现有LLM架构，展现了更高效的信息集成能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [261] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星气象），构建并评估预测模型，用于预测尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区冰湖溃决、降雨型滑坡及冰川池塘洪水三类灾害的易感性与触发时机。结果显示前期天气对触发时机的预测良好（ROC 0.73–0.83），而地形对易感性的预测在匹配对照组后仅为0.54–0.76；深度学习模型未显著优于梯度提升基线，最终产出尼泊尔预警名单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前可行性研究发现雷达干涉可识别冰碛坝变形（指示湖泊失稳）、卫星天气可指示湖泊受迫时段，但未提出预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，基于免费数据建立可预测"哪个站点易感、何时触发"的模型。

Method: 利用589个冰湖溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）与数千滑坡记录，将每个事件与相似的未失效站点配对；采用空间交叉验证（按图幅整体留出）防止空间泄漏。测试三类灾害（大型冰碛/冰坝溃决、降雨触发滑坡、冰川池塘小洪水），每类含两个独立问题（易感性排名与触发时机）。对比五个深度学习模型与梯度提升基线，并用三步决策树（崎岖度+季风降雨）复现基线结果。

Result: 前期天气对触发时机预测：大型溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、池塘洪水0.82。地形对易感性：朴素评分约0.9但存在聚类偏差，配对匹配后真实值为0.76、0.71和0.54（后者无异于随机）。尼泊尔单一区域溃决信号达0.89。五个深度学习模型未决定性胜过梯度提升基线，湖泊类灾害基线胜出，可被三步决策树复现。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以在一定程度上预测触发时机（天气信号较强），但地形对易感性的预测能力仅部分有效，尤其对小洪水基本无效。研究产出尼泊尔排序预警名单作为优先级辅助工具，并指出免费数据的适用范围与局限。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [262] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARCH架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，并结合内容条件锚键进行注意力聚合，使状态空间模型能够在保持计算效率的同时有效扩展到固定维度之外，在多个长上下文任务上超越线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具长上下文检索能力，但训练时平方级复杂度和推理时线性增长的KV缓存代价高昂；循环替代方案虽然解码高效但压缩全部历史到固定大小状态，早期关联易被后续更新覆盖，在召回密集型任务上表现欠佳。

Method: MARCH周期性缓存累积的循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，为每个锚点关联紧凑的内容条件锚键，形成可随上下文长度增长的记忆库。每个token产生锚点查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力聚合，沿当前状态整合历史锚点信息。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多个线性注意力变体。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存能够显著增强循环模型的长距离记忆能力，同时保留其固有的高效计算路径，在历史分辨率与记忆成本之间提供了可控的权衡。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [263] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA分层控制架构，用于多AUV自组网在受限声通信和海洋扰动下的协同目标跟踪，通过扩散策略与价值梯度引导实现更快的收敛和更高的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体强化学习方法在高维联合状态-动作建模上存在困难，且策略生成对噪声敏感，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能下降。需要一种能够应对动态拓扑、受限声通信和海洋扰动的鲁棒协同跟踪方案。

Method: 提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构（全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层、物理动作执行层），其中局部在线训练层采用VGG-MADiffRL算法。VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在逆向去噪过程中引入价值梯度引导动作生成，采用孪生价值网络联合优化和软目标更新来缓解过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验表明VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用性。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL结合MDCA架构有效解决了多AUV协同跟踪中训练不稳定和策略噪声敏感的问题，为水下自组网协同跟踪提供了可行的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [264] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生成式建模统一表述为一个路径积分，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均为同一主作用量的不同评估原则。通过MSRJD形式将自由与相互作用的概率流分离，并引入费曼图微扰论。一阶环修正可在不增加随机采样成本的情况下提升确定性采样器精度（将53%的树级误差降至1.6%），同时引入响应加权分数匹配目标与对称性等变漂移设计的EFT算符展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式建模中各种范式（流、扩散、变分、对抗）看似分散且理论基础各异，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们之间的深层联系。本文旨在以路径积分和场论语言建立起一个统一的理论基础，使不同生成模型成为同一主作用量下的不同评估方案，从而为改进采样精度、处理不完美分数以及引入对称性约束提供系统且可微扰计算的理论工具。

Method: 作者将生成建模公式化为路径积分，采用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de Dominicis（MSRJD）形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离。该方法将概率演化映射到场论框架，从而可运用费曼图微扰理论进行展开。具体包括：(1) 在树级之上引入一阶（单圈）环修正，无需额外随机采样代价；(2) 将不完美的学习分数视为算符插入，导出响应加权的分数匹配目标；(3) 对称性等变的漂移设计被重写为带EFT幂次计数的算符展开。

Result: 在可解漂移和非线性漂移系统上验证了一阶环修正的有效性：树级误差从53%显著降低至1.6%，且不产生额外随机采样成本。此外，该方法系统地处理了不完美学习分数的传播效应（通过响应加权分数匹配目标），并为对称性等变的生成模型设计提供了有效的算符展开框架。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一生成式建模各主要范式的路径积分/场论框架，不仅揭示了不同模型之间的深层结构联系，还提供了实用的微扰改进方案：单圈修正大幅提升采样精度且零额外采样成本。该框架还自然扩展了分数匹配理论的精细度（响应加权）以及对称性设计的系统化路径（EFT幂次计数），为生成模型的更高阶改进与理论分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [265] [FlowLOB: Efficient and Controllable Limit Order Book Generation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13096)
*Zhuohan Wang,Andreea Bacalum,Ollie Olby,Carmine Ventre,Namid Stillman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowLOB，一个基于条件流匹配的限价订单簿(LOB)轨迹生成器。在三种采样频率下使用多个港交所交易标的的训练数据，通过tick相对表示实现向未见标的的零样本迁移。流匹配仅需10步ODE求解器即可达到最佳质量，而扩散模型需要更多函数评估才能接近同等保真度。FlowLOB在多数分布度量上优于两种学习型和两种基于智能体的基线模型，并通过分布检验验证了反事实可控性，且效果可零样本迁移到留出标的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LOB模拟器（基于智能体的和深度生成的）无法同时满足实际从业者的四项需求：真实的市场动态、计算高效采样、可控场景生成以及超越训练标的的泛化能力。作者旨在构建一个在这四个维度上均表现良好的生成式LOB模拟器。

Method: FlowLOB采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）框架生成LOB轨迹，以tick相对表示编码数据，在多个HKEX标的和三种采样频率（0.1s、1s、10s）上训练。由于流模型和扩散模型共享统一表述，用相同的数据、架构和计算预算训练两者，并通过相同的固定步长ODE求解器采样，进行公平对比。评估包括分布度量真实性和基于分布检验的反事实可控性。

Result: 流匹配在仅10步ODE求解器步数下达到最佳质量，远超扩散模型所需的函数评估数。在此高效配置下，FlowLOB在两种较细采样频率的多数分布度量上优于四种基线模型（两种学习型、两种基于智能体）。反事实可控性检验表明FlowLOB在多数测试设定下满足标准，且真实性与控制效果均能零样本迁移到留出的交易标的上。

Conclusion: FlowLOB证明了条件流匹配是构建高保真、计算高效且具有跨标的泛化能力的LOB模拟器的有效框架，在现实性、可控性和泛化性上均优于现有方法，为实际量化研究和市场微观结构模拟提供了更优的工具。

Abstract: Limit order book (LOB) simulators are most useful to practitioners when they combine realistic market dynamics, computationally efficient sampling, controllable scenario generation, and the ability to generalize beyond the instruments seen during training---properties that existing agent-based and deep generative simulators provide only partially. We present \textbf{FlowLOB}, a conditional \textbf{flow}-matching generator of \textbf{LOB} trajectories, trained on multiple Hong Kong Exchange (HKEX) symbols at three sampling frequencies ($0.1$s, $1$s, $10$s) in tick-relative representation that transfers to unseen instruments. Because flow and diffusion models admit a common formulation, we train both with identical data, architecture, and budget, and sample both through the same fixed-step ODE solvers, yielding a controlled comparison of sampling efficiency and fidelity. Flow matching attains its best quality with only $10$ ODE-solver steps, whereas diffusion needs many more function evaluations to approach the same fidelity. At this efficient operating point, FlowLOB improves realism over baselines, two learned and two agent-based models, in most distributional metrics at the two finer sampling frequencies. We evaluate counterfactual controllability with a distributional test that asks whether changing a scenario condition moves the generated statistic toward the corresponding real tail regime; FlowLOB satisfies this criterion in most tested settings. Both realism and control effects transfer zero-shot on a held-out symbol. We additionally conduct ablation studies on the network architecture and the learning rate.

</details>


### [266] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图中边级异常检测器AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架，基于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制，在不改变检测性能的前提下为异常检测结果提供可审计的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中的深度学习异常检测器虽然准确率很高，但完全是不透明的——当一条边被标记为异常时，分析师只能看到一个分数而不知道原因。在合作性、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的，因为自动化决策必须可审计且可信赖。AddGraph作为动态图边级异常检测的基础框架，从未配备任何形式的可解释性，因此亟需弥补这一空白。

Method: X-AddGraph采用严格事后（post-hoc）的可解释性框架，基于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件，每个组件分别对应AddGraph的一个架构模块：（1）基于梯度的相关性归因作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；（2）直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；（3）通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器本身保持冻结状态。

Result: 检测性能被精确保留（Delta AUC = 0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC为0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph逐分复现所有分数并添加解释。在四类边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上评估，长期归因标识的历史快照携带的对抗信号显著多于随机选择（0.127 vs 0.074），这一能力是任何空间盲解释器都无法提供的。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph提供了零性能损耗的事后可解释性，填补了动态图异常检测中可解释性缺失的空白。其长期时间归因能够提供独特的反事实信号，这是空间维度解释无法覆盖的。作者发布了完整实现代码以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [267] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的睡眠分期参考图谱（LBH）构建方法，利用多评分者数据集（DOD-H和DOD-O）中的多位专家评分，通过混淆矩阵建模每个评分者的分期行为，再聚合多个专家的概率估计生成更可靠的参考标签。在随机森林、SVM和MLP分类器以及EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下，LBH均优于数据集图谱（DH）和最佳评分者图谱（BSH），最佳结果为随机森林+EEG+EMG组合，在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率，在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期对睡眠障碍的诊断和管理至关重要，但大多数自动分期研究使用单一参考图谱作为评估标准，忽略了评分者间的变异性（inter-scorer variability）。作者旨在利用多评分数据集构建更可靠的参考标签，以反映多位专家的集体行为，从而提升自动睡眠分期模型的评估质量和泛化能力。

Method: 提出学习型睡眠图谱方法（LBH）：首先从EEG（C3-M2）和下巴EMG信号中提取30秒epoch的分段特征（每模态30个特征，EEG+EMG共60个）；然后使用机器学习分类器（随机森林、SVM、多层感知机）训练模型，并从每个评分者的混淆矩阵中建模其分期行为；对混淆矩阵进行列归一化后，估计每个真实睡眠阶段在给定评分者标注下的条件概率；最后跨评分者聚合这些概率，为每个epoch分配最终标签。实验在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下进行，并与DH和BSH两种基线方法比较。

Result: LBH在整体性能上一致优于DH和BSH。最佳结果来自随机森林分类器结合EEG+EMG设置：在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模可以有效改进参考图谱的构建，在不丢弃个别专家信息的前提下，从多位专家的集体行为中生成更可靠的参考标签，为睡眠分期模型的评估提供了更稳健的参照标准。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [268] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型中，预测方向（token的unembedding方向）作为一个内容定义的"特权锚点"，残余流中的变异按与该锚点的距离呈几何和行为分层。该分层在全部18个模型（涵盖稠密/MoE、7B-120B、基座/指令微调）中成立，且预测方向几乎正交于主方差轴，使得基于方差的分析只能部分恢复该结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已发现Transformer会形成特权基（privileged bases），但尚不清楚这些基究竟选择了什么样的方向。本文旨在探究预测方向是否作为一个内容定义的特权锚点，以及残余流中高维计算如何与线性读出（linear readout）共存。

Method: 以预测方向的unembedding方向作为锚点，对残余流进行几何测度分析；对18个模型（稠密与MoE，7B-120B，基座与指令微调）进行系统比较；通过方差方向扰动实验（分层打断预测近端/远端方向）检验因果作用；使用方向而非强度的干预来区分行为驱动因素。

Result: 预测方向是一个内容定义的特权锚点，残余流变异按距离该锚点的远近呈分层结构。窄而尺度不变的预测接口集中了与读出相关的结构，而远离预测的互补区域随模型规模扩展。方差基分析只能部分恢复该组织，缺口随提示异质性增大。打断最接近预测的方差方向导致立即发散和任务框架转移，打断次一级方向则延迟发散但保持框架；互补区域方向-wise与读出对齐弱但在因果和时间上承载功能。行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一个区别于此前描述坐标轴的特权锚点，为高维计算如何与线性读出共存提供了几何解释。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [269] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习表示和汇总（summarization）的客观分类方法，用于组织跨多个飞行状态的阵风诱导载荷数据。在3480个压力载荷测量数据中识别出9种跨飞行姿态重现的基元响应类型，兼具可解释性和专家可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于飞行姿态等粗参数的标签分类方法缺乏客观性，而阵风诱导载荷在众多飞行条件下呈现出高度复杂性。本文旨在寻找一种既客观又可解释的分类准则，以替代依赖人工经验或粗粒度参数的分类方式。

Method: 将大量实验观测编码为机器学习表示，随后应用汇总（summarization）程序从全部观测中选择一个最小的高显著性样本子集（exemplars）。这些exemplars提供基于相似性的客观分类准则，供专家审查并作为精细实验的对象。数据库包含3480次随机阵风引起的压力载荷测量，覆盖飞翼模型的六个飞行姿态。

Result: 在六种飞行姿态的数据库中识别出九种基元响应类型，这些类型在多个姿态间重复出现；对类型瞬态响应的分析提供了对底层流体力学物理机制的直觉理解。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够以客观且可解释的方式有效组织阵风诱导载荷的复杂性，发现的响应类型不仅具有分类价值，还能促进对流体力学机理的物理洞察，可作为专家深入研究和针对性实验的基础。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [270] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对"结局因治疗决策而永久不可观测"场景的预测模型评估框架，以心脏骤停后神经学预后判断为案例（2,497名患者，其中1,429名结局被治疗决定干扰），通过区分"确定案例"与"不确定案例"分别评估模型，发现传统指标会掩盖不确定案例上的重要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设结局对每位患者都可清晰观测，但治疗决策会使临床相关结局永久不可观测（如心脏骤停后因治疗选择而无法判断真实神经学结局）。传统评估指标在这种情境下会产生误导，尤其是在最需要预后支持的患者群体中。作者希望揭示并解决这一评估盲区。

Method: 作者提出将评价明确拆分为"确定案例"（结局可观测）与"不确定案例"（结局由独立临床专家给出反事实猜测）两个子集分别评估。同时提出一个简单的预测模型，可同时利用两类案例的目标标签，并在两者之间进行权衡以调参。

Result: 在提出的神经模型及若干表格基线模型上，AUROC相似的模型在Brier分数及不确定案例的概率估计上差异显著。提高与不确定案例标签的对齐度通常会导致确定案例上的准确性下降，表明传统评估掩盖了一个显式的权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定了临床有意义结局是否可观测时，传统评估指标会遗漏最需要预后支持的患者的失败模式，因此需要显式拆分评估框架以捕捉这些被掩盖的风险。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [271] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k策略估计问题进行了系统性基准测试。在5个数据集、2个已知效应扫描和6种估计器上，验证了弱重叠、优化者诅咒和倾向性估计误差三大机制。研究发现：1) 弱重叠由记录器与目标动作的对齐决定而非记录器尖锐度；2) 仅交叉拟合结果扰动项无法解决优化者诅咒，需诚实的策略级分割；3) 倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下决定如何分配处理对象，并希望在使用某个定向规则（targeting rule）之前就知道该规则能带来多少收益。离线策略评估（OPE）承诺从日志数据中实现这一目标，但可部署的规则是确定性的top-k策略，它消除了对动作的所有平均，导致弱重叠（weak overlap）问题直接冲击估计精度。作者旨在系统性地揭示这一实践场景下的估计失败机制及其原因。

Method: 作者在5个数据集和2个已知效应扫描上基准测试了6种OPE估计器，并对照非模拟配对参考验证机制。具体包括：1) 分析弱重叠受记录器-目标动作对齐而非记录尖锐度控制的规律，使用有效样本量（ESS）排名风险；2) 检验交叉拟合结果扰动项对优化者诅咒的修复效果，对比诚实的策略级分割；3) 评估倾向性估计误差（特别是fold外估计）对IPS、DR估计和重叠诊断的影响。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 弱重叠由记录器对目标动作的概率决定，从目标自身分数构建的更尖锐记录器几乎不改变重叠；动作级分歧会崩溃重叠。ESS可跨日志排名风险，但在单个日志内部排名候选时较弱且截点不可迁移。(2) 仅交叉拟合扰动项无法修复优化者诅咒，反而使重用偏差更严重；诚实的策略级分割通过改变估计目标来避免重用。(3) 倾向性估计误差是最大退化来源：fold外估计对IPS伤害超过任何其他压力，对DR几乎不变，且会反转重叠诊断本身。所有失败均发生在有界权重下（倾向分数下限0.02），数据为公开的半合成/合成数据。

Conclusion: 在确定性top-k策略的真实部署场景下，OPE面临的核心挑战不是传统的倾向分数重叠问题，而是记录器与目标动作的语义对齐、优化者诅咒的估计目标选择，以及倾向性模型估计误差。研究揭示了现有去偏方法（如交叉拟合）在此场景下的局限性，并指出策略级诚实分割（改变估计目标）是更有效的路径。作者发布了公开数据基准，但精确值曲面均为合成或半合成数据。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [272] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HouseGNN，一种基于Householder反射变换和GroupSort激活的图神经网络层，通过保持节点欧几里得范数的分段正交操作来缓解深层GNN中的过平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络存在过平滑问题——随着网络层数增加，归一化图传播算子的重复应用会导致节点表示逐步坍缩到低信息子空间，使得深层模型难以捕捉有用信息。标准GCN将邻域聚合消息直接更新隐藏状态，加剧了这一退化过程。

Method: HouseGNN不再直接用聚合的邻域消息更新隐藏状态，而是将其仅用于估计一个反射方向。节点嵌入通过Householder反射器（reflector）更新，随后经过GroupSort激活函数，构成一个分段正交层，在每层保持所有节点的欧几里得范数不变。

Result: 论文证明了三个核心性质：(i) 每个内部层保留节点级欧几里得范数；(ii) Householder反射器在消息上具有尺度和符号不变性；(iii) 节点间的成对距离可以通过节点级正交算子之间的不匹配来改变（即不会坍缩）。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过设计保持范数的分段正交传播机制，从理论上保证了深层网络中节点表示不会坍缩到低信息子空间，为缓解过平滑问题提供了新的理论保证和设计范式。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [273] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散扩散的掩码采样问题，引入了一种名为"去掩码增长复杂度"(UGC)的路径解析数据几何度量，其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，从而实现对Bernoulli子集和固定基数去掩码方案的统一分析，并提出了能够达到指定KL误差且迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近oracle的认证最优采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中的掩码采样（masking diffusion）在离散采样中具有重要应用，但现有的调度策略（如均匀调度、粗粒度调度）未能充分利用数据的几何结构，导致计算效率低下。作者希望建立一种能够量化数据几何与离散化误差之间关系的理论框架，从而指导更优的采样调度设计。

Method: 作者引入路径解析的度量——去掩码增长复杂度(UGC)，其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一分析Bernoulli子集与固定基数的去掩码方案。在log-reveal-odds坐标下优化单块和多块调度；通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计UGC增量，构建高概率达到指定KL误差的认证最优采样器；并在细划分极限下推导出由UGC密度的平方根平方积分决定的锐利最优Euler离散化误差。

Result: 所提出的认证最优采样器能够以高概率达到指定的KL误差，且迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近oracle程序。理论分析展示了与经典多元依赖度量及先前离散扩散复杂度度量的联系。在细划分极限下得到了平方根UGC密度控制最优Euler离散化误差的精确结果。实例展示了对粗调度的显著维度相关增益，包括仅用常数个自适应放置块即可实现的Ω̃(√d)改进。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的、基于数据几何的离散扩散采样分析框架，UGC不仅提供了对KL离散化误差的精确控制，还给出了实际可行的、可认证最优的调度策略。这为自适应计算资源配置提供了理论基础，并在高维场景中展示了显著的性能提升潜力。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [274] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GENADA，一种基于生成模型的对抗攻击方法，通过单次前向传播直接生成欺骗性扰动，替代传统迭代梯度攻击中反复反向传播的计算开销。在时间序列领域多个模型和数据集上验证，攻击质量与强基线相当且推理时生成扰动耗时更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析中广泛使用但易受对抗攻击影响。传统的基于梯度的迭代攻击方法（一阶方法）需要反复对受害模型进行反向传播以计算输入梯度，计算开销大，推理效率低。

Method: 提出GENADA（GENerative ADversarial Attack），学习一个生成模型以在单次前向传播中直接产生欺骗性扰动，并设计了相应的训练流程。包含单步和迭代两种生成式攻击变体。

Result: 在时间序列领域多个神经网络模型和数据集（包括受控的低维设置）上进行验证。实验表明GENADA在攻击质量上与强基线相当，同时推理阶段生成扰动所需时间更少。

Conclusion: 生成式对抗攻击方法GENADA能够在不牺牲攻击效果的前提下显著降低对抗扰动生成的计算成本，为时间序列模型的红队测试和鲁棒性评估提供了更高效的攻击工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [275] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出World Model RL (WMRL)，以世界模型替代真实环境执行来消除自动科研（AutoResearch）智能体后训练中环境执行带来的计算瓶颈，并通过在线去偏和逆方差去噪两种缓解手段应对世界模型的不完美性，实现3-4倍训练加速且超越标准RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科研智能体的后训练（尤其是RL）中存在一个根本性资源张力：轨迹中的两个组成部分——智能体生成与环境执行——呈完全不同的扩展方式。生成部分通过批处理共享计算资源高效扩展，而每次环境执行独占沙盒和真实机器时间。随着轨迹增长，环境执行主导训练成本并成为瓶颈，亟需打破这一约束。

Method: 提出WMRL框架，核心思路是用世界模型替代真实环境执行以消除瓶颈。由于世界模型的奖励受偏差和噪声影响而不完美，作者进一步设计两种缓解机制：(1) Online Debiasing，用于抵消偏差；(2) Inverse-Variance Denoising，用于抑制噪声。二者共同保证训练收敛性。

Result: 理论上证明两种缓解机制严格改善收敛保证；实证上在多种任务和不同智能体规模下实现3-4倍训练加速，同时性能超过标准RL基线。后训练的4B和9B智能体在留出基准上超越48B和120B的更大开放权重智能体。WMRL还可迁移至具身VLA策略的后训练，展现良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: WMRL有效解决了AutoResearch智能体RL训练中环境执行的计算瓶颈问题，通过世界模型替代执行结合偏差/噪声双重缓解机制，在加速训练的同时提升性能，并展现出跨任务和跨领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [276] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种快速、可扩展、与输入分布无关的top-k选择算法，通过单遍过滤（小样本估计自适应阈值、流式缓冲、精确top-k）提供概率性正确性保证（置信度1-ε），相比PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x加速，并在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中显著降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k方法效率低下：精确方法内存和计算开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式规则，在对抗性或重尾输入下性能退化。需要一种既高效又具有鲁棒性保证的top-k算法。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤流程：1）从N个输入中抽取小随机样本估计自适应阈值；2）将N个元素流式处理一次，依据阈值过滤到紧凑缓冲区中；3）在缓冲区上执行精确top-k以恢复真正的top-k元素。算法从理论上推导了正确性和缓冲区大小的高概率保证，并给出了近似最优的样本量（作为N和k的函数）以最小化开销。用户可指定置信度参数ε，算法以至少1-ε的概率正确返回真top-k。

Result: 在实验中，Prof-K相比高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x的加速，在大型数据集、小到中等k值场景中收益最大。通过放松召回目标（如恢复95%的真正top-k值），可提供原则性的精度-速度权衡。在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中，Prof-K显著降低了构成训练成本重要部分的top-k选择开销。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供了一种分布无关、具有概率正确性保证的高效top-k算法，兼具精确方法的可靠性和近似方法的效率。其保证独立于输入分布，对对抗性设置也具有鲁棒性，并通过可调召回目标实现了灵活性，在数据库、信息检索到现代机器学习工作负载等多个领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [277] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCoGen两阶段框架，将多模态条件表示与目标信号生成解耦。阶段一用每模态独立的掩码自编码器将时变条件转换为紧凑且容忍缺失的token序列；阶段二用流匹配生成器融合token与静态条件合成目标信号。在三个生理基准（AI-READI连续血糖、MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压）上，于全部16个(dataset, task, metric)设置中超越六个代表性条件生成器，其中13个设置的下游效用以达到或超过真实信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床监测中许多最有信息量的生理信号是有创的、昂贵的或对特定患者不可用的。条件生成可以通过共记录信号和常规临床变量合成缺失信号，但现有生成器都基于单一条件模态构建，面对异构、不规则缺失的时变信号与静态协变量混合场景时性能退化。

Method: 两阶段框架ReCoGen：(1) 阶段一，每个模态训练一个掩码自编码器，将时变条件蒸馏为紧凑且对缺失容忍的token序列；(2) 阶段二，训练流匹配(flow-matching)生成器，融合这些token与静态条件来合成目标信号。关键设计包括对冻结的逐模态编码器进行可学习的交叉注意力，以及针对静态条件采用token加AdaLN的双路径。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV动脉血压生成三个生理基准上，ReCoGen在全部16个(dataset, task, metric)设置中取得最佳下游效用，超过六个代表性条件生成器；其中13个设置的下游效用达到或超过真实信号测量的效用。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明增益主要来自条件路径的设计。ReCoGen能够将常规采集的信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的有效替代，向更少侵入、更低成本的连续临床监测迈进一步。作者将以真实信号效用作为近似锚点而非上限。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [278] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机低维重参数化训练神经网络所需的潜在搜索空间维度，提出了方向分辨的二次主公式来预测随机切片残差，并基于此设计了Random Mapping Networks（RaMaN）网络架构，通过结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射避免稠密随机映射的存储开销，实验证明其能紧密追踪训练转变位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可通过随机低维重参数化进行训练或微调，即小的潜在向量通过冻结随机映射生成完整参数更新。核心实际问题是：潜在搜索空间需要多大才能到达低损失区域？现有Gaussian-width二次界等方法缺乏方向分辨率，且稠密随机映射的存储开销为O(dP)不可接受。需要更精确的预测理论和更高效的实现方式。

Method: 首先将已知的可达性转变表达为等价的锥形形式，以极锥的统计维度为中心；然后提出方向分辨的二次主公式，从曲率谱和参考到解的位移分布预测随机切片残差；进一步推导出自洽的各向同性方向预测器，以及保守的仅半径特化形式从而恢复之前的Gaussian-width二次界。基于此构建RaMaN网络：使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射避免O(dP)存储，将优化器状态内存从O(P)降至O(d)；并开发无矩阵曲率逼近和无扫描维度选择方法。

Result: 在受控二次实验和神经曲率实验中，方向分辨预测器紧密追踪测得的转变位置，在位移方向重要时优于方向无关的近似方法。端到端实验在图像和语言模型上均显示出尖锐的、协议依赖的训练转变。

Conclusion: 方向分辨的二次分析框架能更准确地预测随机低维重参数化的可达性转变；RaMaN网络证明了该理论在实践中的可行性，实现了高存储效率和维度选择可预测性，为低维随机训练提供完整理论和工程解决方案。

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [279] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在有限简单无向图（带单一布尔节点特征）上，任何由最终常数激活函数集合 Σ 定义的 Σ-MPLang 所能表达的布尔查询，严格包含于 ReLU-MPLang 所能表达的布尔查询类中，从而解决了 ReLU-MPLang 是否比 trReLU-MPLang 更强的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决一个近期提出的开放问题：在布尔查询方面，ReLU-MPLang 是否比 trReLU-MPLang（截断 ReLU）更强大。这关系到 ReLU 激活函数在图神经网络（GNN）表达力中的独特地位。

Method: 作者在带单一布尔节点特征的有限简单无向图上，对任意由最终常数激活函数组成的集合 Σ 构造 Σ-MPLang 的布尔查询类，并与 ReLU-MPLang 的布尔查询类进行包含关系的严格比较论证。

Result: 证明了 Σ-MPLang（对任意最终常数激活函数集合 Σ，带任意实数系数）表达的布尔查询类严格是 ReLU-MPLang 对应查询类的真子类。由此推导出 ReLU-GNN 在布尔查询上严格强于 {TrReLU, id}-GNN。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了 ReLU-MPLang 与 trReLU-MPLang 表达力关系的开放问题，确立了 ReLU 激活函数在布尔查询表达能力上的严格优势。
